📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 确定了 15 例疑似 猴痘 入院病例,占 330 例隔离病房入院病例的 4.5%(95% 置信区间 2.8-7.4)。2022 年入院人数集中(15 人中的 9 人;60.0%),其中 6 人于 2022 年 7 月入院,其中可能包括来自同一地区的 4 名 7-14 岁儿童和青少年,他们彼此在 8 天内就诊。记录了 3 例死亡(病死率 20.0%,95% 置信区间 7.0-45.2),其中包括 1 例并发急性呼吸窘迫综合征的播散性病例。
  • TK 缺陷型 MPXV 突变体在细胞培养中复制到与野生型病毒相似的滴度,但在体内减弱,如 CAST/EiJ 小鼠皮内感染模型所示。 [10.1080/22221751.2026.2717859]。
  • 典型病例通常包括发烧、淋巴结肿大和离心性水疱脓疱性皮疹。然而,最近的 暴发 揭示了非典型表现,包括生殖器和肛周病变、轻微或不存在前驱症状以及异步皮疹 进化。 [10.24171/j.phrp.2025.0402]。
  • 研究观察到,猴痘 被确定为一种罕见的性传播皮肤病,患者 队列研究 具有特定的临床特征。 [10.20452/jmr.2026.20060]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Fifteen suspected mpox admissions were identified, representing 4.5% (95% confidence interval 2.8-7.4) of 330 isolation-ward admissions. [10.64898/2026.08.25.26360975].
  • The TK-deficient MPXV mutant replicated to similar titers as the wild-type virus in cell culture but was attenuated in vivo, as shown in the CAST/EiJ mouse intradermal infection model. [10.1080/22221751.2026.2717859].
  • Classical cases typically involve fever, lymphadenopathy, and a centrifugal vesiculopustular rash; however, recent outbreaks have revealed atypical presentations, including genital and perianal lesions, minimal or absent prodromal symptoms, and asynchronous rash evolution.
  • The study observed that mpox was identified as a rare sexually transmitted dermatosis, with specific clinical features noted in the patient cohort. [10.20452/jmr.2026.20060].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 猴痘 暴发 在全球范围内持续存在,自 2022 年以来,分支 IIb 是导致 暴发 的主要类型。分支 I 猴痘 暴发 在美国和其他地区已有报道,病例与旅行或性接触有关。CDC 报告称,这些 暴发 正在受到监控和管理。
  • I型猴痘病毒(clade I monkeypox)病例自2023年底起在中非和东非地区持续报告,西欧国家的新发病例可能与持续流行相关。CDC确认,美国I型猴痘病例数自2026年3月起上升,截至2026年7月,报告病例超过50例。这些病例主要与旅行史或与感染者接触相关。
  • 澳门卫生局报告一宗输入性猴痘确诊病例;患者住院并接受 治疗 治疗;根据旅行和接触史,病例被归类为输入病例。 [news-0ce2a3a8508b444f]。
  • 几内亚比绍出现了新的 暴发 病例,这是该国首例病例。2022 年全球 暴发 被宣布为公共卫生紧急状态,随后于 2023 年 5 月解除。2024 年 暴发 也被宣布为公共卫生紧急状态,并于 2025 年 9 月解除。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Mpox virus news developments.
  • Monkeypox outbreaks are ongoing globally, with clade IIb being the primary type causing the outbreak since 2022.
  • Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox have been reported in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with newer outbreaks in Western European countries likely related to the ongoing outbreak. [news-b376fd2a7f832a03].
  • Macao Health Bureau reported one confirmed imported mpox (monkeypox) case; patient is hospitalized and receiving treatment; case classified as imported based on travel and exposure history. [news-0ce2a3a8508b444f].
  • A fresh outbreak of mpox in Guinea Bissau, marking the country’s first case, has occurred. The 2022 global outbreak was declared a public health emergency and later lifted in May 2023.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Reviews in medical virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-01  |  DOI: 10.1002/rmv.70196  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 2026, 36, 5, e70196
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

猴痘 机载 传播:系统回顾。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.
作者： Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
摘要中文翻译自 2022 年 5 月以来,多个非流行地区均报告了 猴痘,并在不同的地理环境中同时发生了 暴发,引发了人们对更广泛的国际传播的担忧。然而,有关空气传播 传播 风险的证据仍然有限。我们进行了系统审查,以评估空气中 传播 的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒。检索了 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,包括环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和 队列研究 研究。数据提取包括研究特征(作者、年份、设计和背景)、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、随访、评估的呼吸道颗粒类型(飞沫和/或气溶胶)以及主要发现。在确定的 286 份记录中,有四项研究符合纳入标准。所有这些都是 2022 年至 2023 年间发表的环境研究。在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 到 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或飞沫中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。总体而言,现有证据有限,并不支持空气传播感染的明确风险。需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸途径的潜力。系统审查注册:PROSPERO:CRD42023391123。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 猴痘 空气传播的风险以及空气样本中是否存在活病毒,因为 猴痘 已在多个非流行地区报告,并担心国际传播。
设计与对象
对猴痘的空气传播传播的风险进行了系统评价,包括2022年至2023年期间发表的环境研究、病例报告、横断面研究和队列研究研究。
核心方法
该综述涉及搜索 PubMed、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆以英文发表的研究,提取有关研究特征、样本量、诊断方法、参与者特征、评估的呼吸颗粒物和主要发现的数据。
主要结果
在表面和空气样本中检测到 MPXV DNA,循环阈值 (Ct) 值范围为 < 30 至 < 45。然而,没有一项研究表明气溶胶或液滴中病毒的生存能力一致,足以支持空气中的 传播。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,现有证据并不支持空气传播感染的明显风险,强调需要在人类环境中进行进一步精心设计的研究,以阐明 猴痘 传播 通过呼吸道途径的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,目前的证据不足以证实猴痘在空气中传播,这对公共卫生措施以及制定有针对性的监测和预防策略具有影响。
局限与证据强度
证据有限且基于环境研究,可能无法完全代表人类 传播 动态,导致结论的置信度评估适中。

Mpox Airborne Transmission: A Systematic Review.

Authors: Lucas Ferreira Theotonio Dos Santos, Leonardo Javier Arcuri, Viviane Aparecida R Sant'Anna, Patrícia Campos Couto, Guilherme de Paula P Schettino, Luiz Vicente Rizzo, Henrique Andrade R Fonseca
Original abstract (English)
Since May 2022, mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions, with concurrent outbreaks in diverse geographic settings, raising concerns about broader international spread. However, evidence regarding the risk of airborne transmission remains limited. We conducted a systematic review to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission and the presence of viable viruses in air samples. PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched for studies published in English, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies. Data extraction included study characteristics (authors, year, design, and setting), sample size, diagnostic methods, participant characteristics, follow-up, type of respiratory particles assessed (droplets and/or aerosols), and key findings. Of 286 records identified, four studies met the inclusion criteria. All were environmental studies published between 2022 and 2023. MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission. Overall, the available evidence is limited and does not support a clear risk of airborne infection. Further well-designed studies in human settings are needed to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes. SYSTEMATIC REVIEW REGISTRATION: PROSPERO: CRD42023391123.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the risk of airborne transmission of mpox and the presence of viable viruses in air samples, as mpox has been reported across multiple nonendemic regions with concerns about international spread.
Design and population
A systematic review was conducted to assess the risk of airborne transmission of mpox, including environmental studies, case reports, cross-sectional studies, and cohort studies published between 2022 and 2023.
Core methods
The review involved searching PubMed, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library for studies published in English, extracting data on study characteristics, sample size, diagnostic methods, participant features, respiratory particles assessed, and key findings.
Main results
MPXV DNA was detected in surface and air samples, with cycle threshold (Ct) values ranging from < 30 to < 45. However, none of the studies demonstrated consistent viral viability in aerosols or droplets sufficient to support airborne transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the available evidence does not support a clear risk of airborne infection, highlighting the need for further well-designed studies in human settings to clarify the potential for mpox transmission through respiratory routes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that current evidence is insufficient to confirm airborne transmission of mpox, which has implications for public health measures and the development of targeted surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited and based on environmental studies, which may not fully represent human transmission dynamics, leading to a moderate confidence assessment in the conclusions.

猴痘病毒分支IIb 基因组的分子克隆及减毒突变株构建用于基础与转化研究

Molecular cloning of a monkeypox virus clade IIb genome and construction of an attenuated mutant for basic and translational research
作者： Vincent Klatt, Christian Meyer zu Natrup, Laura Staliunaite, Sabrina Clever, Jan Knickmann, Sonja Ohrnberger, Jan Seib, Eleonore Ostermann, Hanna-Marie Weichel, Marylyn M. Addo
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 是一种啮齿动物传播的 正痘病毒,在人畜共患 传播 上引起人类 猴痘 疾病。最近的全球 暴发 提高了人们的认识并强调了基础研究和转化研究的必要性。然而,研究因 MPXV 被归类为风险组 3 病原体而受到阻碍。为了克服这一限制,我们通过在酵母中单步转化相关重组 (STAR) 克隆生成了完整的全长 MPXV clade IIb 细菌人工染色体 (BAC) 克隆。验证了克隆的 MPXV 基因组 序列的完整性和 BAC 衍生病毒的复制能力。为了获得减毒突变体,通过 BAC 重组工程删除了病毒胸苷激酶 (TK) 基因 OPG101。TK 缺陷型 MPXV 突变体在细胞培养中复制到与野生型病毒相似的滴度,但在体内减弱,如 CAST/EiJ 小鼠皮内感染模型所示。TK 缺陷型 MPXV 减毒突变体有可能被监管机构列为风险组 2 病原体。这将使研究能够在生物安全二级实验室中进行,并极大地促进基础和应用 MPXV 研究。例如,我们证明TK缺陷病毒可用于检测人血清样本中的中和抗体或用于抗病毒药物药物测试。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 是一种啮齿类动物传播的 正痘病毒,在人畜共患 传播 上引起人类 猴痘 疾病。最近的全球 暴发 提高了人们的认识并强调了基础研究和转化研究的必要性。
设计与对象
该研究涉及通过在酵母中单步转化相关重组 (STAR) 克隆生成完整的全长 MPXV clade IIb 细菌人工染色体 (BAC) 克隆。验证了克隆的 MPXV 基因组 序列的完整性和 BAC 衍生病毒的复制能力。为了获得减毒突变体,通过 BAC 重组工程删除了病毒胸苷激酶 (TK) 基因 OPG101。
核心方法
该研究在酵母中使用单步转化相关重组 (STAR) 克隆来生成完整的全长 MPXV clade IIb 细菌人工染色体 (BAC) 克隆。通过 BAC 重组工程删除病毒胸苷激酶 (TK) 基因 OPG101,以产生减毒突变体。
主要结果
TK 缺陷型 MPXV 突变体在细胞培养中复制到与野生型病毒相似的滴度,但在体内减弱,如 CAST/EiJ 小鼠皮内感染模型所示。
解释与创新
作者解释说,MPXV 减毒突变体的产生可能允许在生物安全 2 级实验室中进行研究,从而极大地促进 MPXV 的基础和应用研究。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,减毒的TK缺陷型MPXV突变体可用于检测人血清样本中的中和抗体或用于抗病毒药物药物检测,这对监测和诊断方法具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用小鼠模型进行体内复制的限制,这可能无法完全代表人类疾病。证据是中等的,因为结果基于单一模型系统,不包括更广泛的临床或流行病学数据。

Molecular cloning of a monkeypox virus clade IIb genome and construction of an attenuated mutant for basic and translational research

Authors: Vincent Klatt, Christian Meyer zu Natrup, Laura Staliunaite, Sabrina Clever, Jan Knickmann, Sonja Ohrnberger, Jan Seib, Eleonore Ostermann, Hanna-Marie Weichel, Marylyn M. Addo
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is a rodent-borne orthopoxvirus causing mpox disease upon zoonotic transmission to humans. Recent global outbreaks have raised awareness and highlighted the need for basic and translational research. However, research is hampered by the classification of MPXV as a Risk Group 3 pathogen. In an effort to overcome this limitation, we generated a complete full-length MPXV clade IIb bacterial artificial chromosome (BAC) clone by single-step transformation-associated recombination (STAR) cloning in yeast. The integrity of the cloned MPXV genome sequence and the replication capability of the BAC-derived virus were verified. To obtain an attenuated mutant, the viral thymidine kinase (TK) gene, OPG101, was deleted by BAC recombineering. The TK-deficient MPXV mutant replicated to similar titers as the wild-type virus in cell culture but was attenuated in vivo, as shown in the CAST/EiJ mouse intradermal infection model. The attenuated TK-deficient MPXV mutant has the potential to be classified as a Risk Group 2 pathogen by regulatory authorities. This would allow research to be conducted in Biosafety Level 2 laboratories and greatly facilitate basic and applied MPXV research. For example, we demonstrate that the TK-deficient virus can be used for the detection of neutralizing antibodies in human serum samples or for antiviral drug testing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monkeypox virus (MPXV) is a rodent-borne orthopoxvirus causing mpox disease upon zoonotic transmission to humans. Recent global outbreaks have raised awareness and highlighted the need for basic and translational research.
Design and population
The study involved the generation of a complete full-length MPXV clade IIb bacterial artificial chromosome (BAC) clone by single-step transformation-associated recombination (STAR) cloning in yeast. The integrity of the cloned MPXV genome sequence and the replication capability of the BAC-derived virus were verified. To obtain an attenuated mutant, the viral thymidine kinase (TK) gene, OPG101, was deleted by BAC recombineering.
Core methods
The study used single-step transformation-associated recombination (STAR) cloning in yeast to generate a complete full-length MPXV clade IIb bacterial artificial chromosome (BAC) clone. The viral thymidine kinase (TK) gene, OPG101, was deleted by BAC recombineering to create an attenuated mutant.
Main results
The TK-deficient MPXV mutant replicated to similar titers as the wild-type virus in cell culture but was attenuated in vivo, as shown in the CAST/EiJ mouse intradermal infection model.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the generation of an attenuated mutant of MPXV could potentially allow research to be conducted in Biosafety Level 2 laboratories, thereby greatly facilitating basic and applied MPXV research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the attenuated TK-deficient MPXV mutant could be used for the detection of neutralizing antibodies in human serum samples or for antiviral drug testing, which has implications for surveillance and diagnostic methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a mouse model for in vivo replication, which may not fully represent human disease. The evidence is moderate as the results are based on a single model system and do not include broader clinical or epidemiological data.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

巴西里约热内卢健康、年龄、性别和 HIV 性接触、获得性梅毒和 猴痘 检出率的社会决定因素协会:一项生态学研究

Association of Social Determinants of Health, Age, Sex and Sexual Exposure with HIV, Acquired Syphilis, and Mpox Detection Rates in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil: An Ecological Study
作者： Lara E. Coelho, Rosa M. S. M. Domingues, Valéria Saraceni, Thiago S. Torres, Guilherme T. Goedert, Juliano Genari, Claudio J. Struchiner, Paula M. Luz, Domingues RMSM
摘要中文翻译摘要:本研究采用社区级社会保护指数 (SPI) 来分析社会经济因素与 HIV、后天梅毒和 猴痘 检测的关系。我们使用地理定位到社区的病例通知,对巴西里约热内卢的 2022 年 监测 数据进行了回顾性分析。人口数据来自 2022 年人口普查。根据 SPI 将社区分为五等分,并根据性接触类别计算每 10 万人的检出率。使用随机截距的多级泊松回归模型分析年龄、SPI 和检出率之间的关联,分别针对男男性行为者 (MSM)、异性恋男性和女性进行拟合。在 2,876 例艾滋病毒新发病例中,MSM 的检出率最高(1,522/100,000)。13676例梅毒病例中,男女检出比例为1.7:1。在 957 例 猴痘 病例中,80% 发生在 MSM 中 (812/100,000)。HIV 和梅毒集中在 SPI 较低的社区,而 猴痘 集中在 SPI 较高的社区。对于 HIV 而言,MSM 中年龄较小的相关性最强,而女性中 SPI 较低的相关性最强。对于梅毒来说,女性中年龄较小最为重要,两性中 SPI 都有很强的相关性。在 MSM 中,猴痘 集中在 25-39 岁年龄段和 SPI 较高的社区。妇女之间强烈的社会经济梯度反映了性别和社会经济不平等的交叉。在 MSM 中,减弱的社会经济梯度和独特的 猴痘 模式表明,社会和性网络与结构因素一起塑造了 猴痘。预防必须解决社会决定因素和网络结构。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了巴西里约热内卢的社会经济因素、年龄、性别和性接触与 HIV、获得性梅毒和 猴痘 检出率的关系。作者强调需要了解这些关联,以便为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究使用地理定位到社区的病例通知,对巴西里约热内卢的 2022 年 监测 数据进行生态分析。人口数据来自 2022 年人口普查,社区根据社会保护指数 (SPI) 分为五等分。
核心方法
The researchers used multilevel Poisson regression models with random intercepts to analyze associations between age, SPI, and detection rates, fitting separate models for men who have sex with men (MSM), heterosexual men, and women. Detection rates per 100,000 population were calculated by sexual exposure category.
主要结果
在 2,876 例艾滋病毒新发病例中,MSM 的检出率最高(1,522/100,000)。13676例梅毒病例中,男女检出比例为1.7:1。在 957 例 猴痘 病例中,80% 发生在 MSM 中 (812/100,000)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HIV 和梅毒检出率在 SPI 较低的社区较高,而 猴痘 检测集中在 SPI 较高的社区。这些模式表明社会经济因素和性网络在这些疾病的 传播 动态中发挥着不同的作用。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对 猴痘 检测的社会经济和性别决定因素的见解,可以为有针对性的预防策略提供信息,并强调解决公共卫生工作中的社会和结构性不平等问题的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于生态数据,这可能会掩盖个体层面的因素。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于汇总数据,并且没有考虑个人层面的混杂变量。

Association of Social Determinants of Health, Age, Sex and Sexual Exposure with HIV, Acquired Syphilis, and Mpox Detection Rates in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil: An Ecological Study

Authors: Lara E. Coelho, Rosa M. S. M. Domingues, Valéria Saraceni, Thiago S. Torres, Guilherme T. Goedert, Juliano Genari, Claudio J. Struchiner, Paula M. Luz, Domingues RMSM
Original abstract (English)
Abstract This study employs a neighborhood-level Social Protection Index (SPI) to analyze how socioeconomic factors relate to the detection of HIV, acquired syphilis, and mpox. We conducted a retrospective analysis of 2022 surveillance data from Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, using case notifications geolocated to neighborhoods. Population data were obtained from the 2022 census. Neighborhoods were stratified by SPI into quintiles, and detection rates per 100,000 population were calculated by sexual exposure category. Associations between age, SPI, and detection rates were analyzed using multilevel Poisson regression models with random intercepts, fitted separately for men who gave sex with men (MSM), heterosexual men and women. Of 2,876 new HIV cases, the highest detection rate was among MSM (1,522/100,000). Of 13,676 syphilis cases, the male-to-female detection ratio was 1.7:1. Of 957 mpox cases, 80% occurred among MSM (812/100,000). HIV and syphilis clustered in lower SPI neighborhoods, while mpox concentrated in higher SPI neighborhoods. For HIV, younger age showed the strongest association among MSM, while lower SPI was strongest among women. For syphilis, younger age was most important among women, with strong SPI associations in both sexes. Among MSM, mpox was concentrated in 25-39 age group and higher SPI neighborhoods. Strong socioeconomic gradients among women reflect intersecting gender and socioeconomic inequalities. Among MSM, attenuated socioeconomic gradients and distinct mpox patterns suggest social and sexual networks shape transmission alongside structural factors. Prevention must address social determinants and network structures.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates how socioeconomic factors, age, sex, and sexual exposure relate to the detection rates of HIV, acquired syphilis, and mpox in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil. The authors highlight the need to understand these associations to inform targeted public health interventions.
Design and population
The study is an ecological analysis of 2022 surveillance data from Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, using case notifications geolocated to neighborhoods. Population data were sourced from the 2022 census, and neighborhoods were stratified by the Social Protection Index (SPI) into quintiles.
Core methods
The researchers used multilevel Poisson regression models with random intercepts to analyze associations between age, SPI, and detection rates, fitting separate models for men who have sex with men (MSM), heterosexual men, and women. Detection rates per 100,000 population were calculated by sexual exposure category.
Main results
Among 2,876 new HIV cases, the highest detection rate was observed in MSM (1,522/100,000). Of 13,676 syphilis cases, the male-to-female detection ratio was 1.7:1. Of 957 mpox cases, 80% occurred among MSM (812/100,000).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HIV and syphilis detection rates are higher in lower SPI neighborhoods, while mpox detection is concentrated in higher SPI neighborhoods. These patterns indicate that socioeconomic factors and sexual networks play distinct roles in the transmission dynamics of these diseases.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the socioeconomic and sexual determinants of mpox detection, which can inform targeted prevention strategies and highlight the importance of addressing social and structural inequalities in public health efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to ecological data, which may obscure individual-level factors. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on aggregated data and does not account for confounding variables at the individual level.

2022-2025 年尼日利亚东北部专门传染病隔离病房疑似 猴痘 入院人数:一项基于登记册的描述性研究,包含家庭聚集的证据

Suspected mpox admissions to a dedicated infectious-diseases isolation ward in northeastern Nigeria, 2022–2025: a register-based descriptive study with evidence of a household cluster
作者： Hayatu Ahmad, Ahmad Hayatu
摘要中文翻译猴痘 已重新成为尼日利亚各地的公共卫生优先事项,并于 2022 年和 2024 年连续宣布国际公共卫生紧急情况。来自尼日利亚东北部的收治临床疑似病例的设施级别描述仍然很少。我们对约拉莫迪博阿达玛大学教学医院传染病隔离病房的所有入院患者进行了一项基于登记的描述性研究,其中 猴痘 被记录为工作或差异 诊断 2022 年 2 月至 2025 年 1 月。年龄、性别、入院月份、地点、记录的临床印象和结果被提取和总结,并使用 Wilson 95% 置信度报告比例间隔。确定了 15 例疑似 猴痘 入院病例,占 330 例隔离病房入院病例的 4.5%(95% 置信区间 2.8-7.4)。中位年龄为 20 岁(四分位数范围 13-37;范围 7-57);18岁以下儿童6例(40.0%),男性12例(80.0%),男女比例为4:1。2022 年入院人数集中(15 人中的 9 人;60.0%),其中 6 人于 2022 年 7 月入院,其中可能包括来自同一地区的 4 名 7-14 岁儿童和青少年,他们彼此在 8 天内就诊。记录了 3 例死亡(病死率 20.0%,95% 置信区间 7.0-45.2),其中包括 1 例并发急性呼吸窘迫综合征的播散性病例。人口统计资料与先前报告的阿达马瓦州和国家 监测 数据非常匹配,而病例死亡率升高反映了转诊集中度和诊断不确定性,而不是真正的 猴痘 归因的 死亡率。病例是临床怀疑的而非实验室确诊的,这是本研究的主要局限性。我们建议有针对性地加强设施和地方层面的实验室 诊断,包括双重猴痘-水痘检测算法、使用现有分子平台而不是新基础设施,以及在病房登记册中强制记录实验室结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 已重新成为尼日利亚各地的公共卫生优先事项,并于 2022 年和 2024 年连续宣布国际公共卫生紧急情况。来自尼日利亚东北部的收治临床疑似病例的设施级别描述仍然很少。
设计与对象
我们对约拉莫迪博阿达玛大学教学医院传染病隔离病房的所有入院患者进行了一项基于登记的描述性研究,其中 猴痘 被记录为 2022 年 2 月至 2025 年 1 月期间的工作或差异 诊断。
核心方法
对年龄、性别、入院月份、地点、记录的临床印象和结果进行了抽象和总结,并使用威尔逊 95% 置信区间报告了比例。
主要结果
确定了 15 例疑似 猴痘 入院病例,占 330 例隔离病房入院病例的 4.5%(95% 置信区间 2.8-7.4)。2022 年入院人数集中(15 人中的 9 人;60.0%),其中 6 人于 2022 年 7 月入院,其中可能包括来自同一地区的 4 名 7-14 岁儿童和青少年,他们彼此在 8 天内就诊。记录了 3 例死亡(病死率 20.0%,95% 置信区间 7.0-45.2),其中包括 1 例并发急性呼吸窘迫综合征的播散性病例。
解释与创新
人口统计资料与先前报告的阿达马瓦州和国家 监测 数据非常匹配,而病例死亡率升高反映了转诊集中度和诊断不确定性,而不是真正的 猴痘 归因的 死亡率。
科研与公卫意义
我们建议有针对性地加强设施和地方层面的实验室 诊断,包括双重猴痘-水痘检测算法、使用现有分子平台而不是新基础设施,以及在病房登记册中强制记录实验室结果。
局限与证据强度
病例是临床怀疑的而非实验室确诊的,这是本研究的主要局限性。

Suspected mpox admissions to a dedicated infectious-diseases isolation ward in northeastern Nigeria, 2022–2025: a register-based descriptive study with evidence of a household cluster

Authors: Hayatu Ahmad, Ahmad Hayatu
Original abstract (English)
Mpox has re-emerged as a public-health priority across Nigeria, and successive international public-health emergencies were declared in 2022 and 2024. Facility-level descriptions of admitted, clinically suspected cases from northeastern Nigeria remain sparse. We conducted a register-based descriptive study of all admissions to the infectious-diseases isolation ward of Modibbo Adama University Teaching Hospital, Yola, in which mpox was recorded as the working or a differential diagnosis between February 2022 and January 2025. Age, sex, month of admission, locality, recorded clinical impression, and outcome were abstracted and summarised, with proportions reported using Wilson 95% confidence intervals. Fifteen suspected mpox admissions were identified, representing 4.5% (95% confidence interval 2.8-7.4) of 330 isolation-ward admissions. The median age was 20 years (interquartile range 13-37; range 7-57); six patients (40.0%) were children under 18 years and 12 (80.0%) were male, giving a male-to-female ratio of 4:1. Admissions clustered in 2022 (9 of 15; 60.0%), with six in July 2022, including a probable household cluster of four children and adolescents aged 7-14 years from a single locality who presented within eight days of one another. Three deaths were recorded (case fatality 20.0%, 95% confidence interval 7.0-45.2), including one disseminated case complicated by acute respiratory distress syndrome. The demographic profile closely matches previously reported Adamawa State and national surveillance data, whereas the elevated case fatality reflects referral concentration and diagnostic uncertainty rather than true mpox-attributable mortality. Cases were clinically suspected rather than laboratory-confirmed, which is the principal limitation of this study. We recommend targeted strengthening of laboratory diagnosis at facility and sub-national level, including dual monkeypox-varicella testing algorithms, use of existing molecular platforms rather than new infrastructure, and mandatory recording of laboratory results within ward registers.
View research seven-element analysis

Mpox has re-emerged as a public-health priority across Nigeria, and successive international public-health emergencies were declared in 2022 and 2024. Facility-level descriptions of admitted, clinically suspected cases from northeastern Nigeria remain sparse.

患病率、特征及猴痘病毒相关眼部疾病(MPOXROD)的临床进程:刚果民主共和国南基伍省一项前瞻性队列研究(MBOTE-EYE)

Prevalence, characteristics, and clinical course of mpox-related ophthalmic disease (MBOTE-EYE): a prospective cohort study in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo.
作者： Theophile Kabesha, Christophe Van Dijck, Samuel Mudwanga, Sarah Houben, Yves Mujula, Papy Munganga, Jean-Claude Tshomba, Guy Mukari, Tony Wawina-Bokalanga, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于猴痘病毒相关眼部疾病(MPOXROD)的系统性纵向眼科医师主导评估鲜有报道。本研究旨在在Ib分支猴痘病毒流行期间,在支持性治疗下,对MPOXROD的患病率及临床进程进行特征描述。
设计与对象
MBOTE-EYE是一项嵌套于刚果民主共和国南基伍省前瞻性队列研究中的子研究。所有在过去48小时内经PCR确诊为猴痘病毒感染的住院患者均符合入组条件。参与者在入组、出院及第29天和第59天接受眼科检查。
核心方法
收集结膜拭子用于猴痘病毒 PCR。对 89 名患者进行了 PCR,低于 25 的循环阈值仅发生在活动性 MPOXROD 期间。使用混合效应泊松回归评估 MPOXROD 的危险因素。
主要结果
Between Oct 28, 2024, and June 30, 2025, 310 participants were enrolled (median age 14·0 years [IQR 2·5-25·0]; 166 [54%] female). At enrolment, conjunctival disease was present in 112 (36% [95% CI 31-42]) participants, corneal disease in 24 (8% [5-11]), and anterior uveitis in two (1% [0-2]). Overall, 134 (43% [38-49]) participants developed MPOXROD during follow-up, most often bilaterally. Blindness following ulcerative keratitis occurred in two (1% [0-3]) of 224 participants.
解释与创新
Ib 猴痘 分支的眼部受累很常见,且常常是双侧的,具有严重的角膜炎负担和视力丧失的风险。系统的眼科检查应纳入 猴痘 护理中,并应开发有针对性的眼科治疗。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了将眼科评估纳入 猴痘 监测 和管理的重要性。他们还强调需要有针对性的眼科治疗,以解决与 Ib 猴痘 分支相关的眼部并发症的重大负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项前瞻性 队列研究 研究,嵌套在一项更大的研究中,这可能会限制普遍性。证据充分,基于详细的方法和清晰的结果报告进行了高置信度评估。

Prevalence, characteristics, and clinical course of mpox-related ophthalmic disease (MBOTE-EYE): a prospective cohort study in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo.

Authors: Theophile Kabesha, Christophe Van Dijck, Samuel Mudwanga, Sarah Houben, Yves Mujula, Papy Munganga, Jean-Claude Tshomba, Guy Mukari, Tony Wawina-Bokalanga, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Systematic longitudinal ophthalmologist-led assessment of mpox-related ophthalmic disease (MPOXROD) is rarely reported. We aimed to characterise the prevalence and clinical course of MPOXROD under supportive management during a clade Ib outbreak. METHODS: MBOTE-EYE was a substudy nested within a prospective cohort in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo. All hospitalised patients with PCR-confirmed mpox (within the last 48 h) were eligible. Participants underwent ophthalmological examination at enrolment, discharge, and days 29 and 59. Conjunctival swabs were collected for monkeypox virus PCR. Risk factors for MPOXROD were assessed using mixed-effects Poisson regression. FINDINGS: Between Oct 28, 2024, and June 30, 2025, 310 participants were enrolled (median age 14·0 years [IQR 2·5-25·0]; 166 [54%] female). At enrolment, conjunctival disease was present in 112 (36% [95% CI 31-42]) participants, corneal disease in 24 (8% [5-11]), and anterior uveitis in two (1% [0-2]). Overall, 134 (43% [38-49]) participants developed MPOXROD during follow-up, most often bilaterally. Blindness following ulcerative keratitis occurred in two (1% [0-3]) of 224 participants. Periorbital lesions (adjusted risk ratio [aRR] 3·65 [95% CI 2·42-5·49]) and severe acute malnutrition (aRR 3·16 [1·22-8·22]) were associated with MPOXROD. PCR was done among 89 patients and cycle threshold values below 25 occurred exclusively during active MPOXROD. INTERPRETATION: Ophthalmic involvement in clade Ib mpox is common and frequently bilateral, with a substantial burden of keratitis and risk of vision loss. Systematic eye examinations should be integrated into mpox care, and targeted ophthalmic therapies should be developed. FUNDING: Canadian Institutes of Health Research and the International Development Research Centre, the European & Developing Countries Clinical Trials Partnership, and the Belgian Directorate-General for Development Cooperation and Humanitarian Aid.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Systematic longitudinal ophthalmologist-led assessment of mpox-related ophthalmic disease (MPOXROD) is rarely reported. We aimed to characterise the prevalence and clinical course of MPOXROD under supportive management during a clade Ib outbreak.
Design and population
MBOTE-EYE was a substudy nested within a prospective cohort in South Kivu, Democratic Republic of the Congo. All hospitalised patients with PCR-confirmed mpox (within the last 48 h) were eligible. Participants underwent ophthalmological examination at enrolment, discharge, and days 29 and 59.
Core methods
Conjunctival swabs were collected for monkeypox virus PCR. PCR was done among 89 patients and cycle threshold values below 25 occurred exclusively during active MPOXROD. Risk factors for MPOXROD were assessed using mixed-effects Poisson regression.
Main results
Between Oct 28, 2024, and June 30, 2025, 310 participants were enrolled (median age 14·0 years [IQR 2·5-25·0]; 166 [54%] female). At enrolment, conjunctival disease was present in 112 (36% [95% CI 31-42]) participants, corneal disease in 24 (8% [5-11]), and anterior uveitis in two (1% [0-2]). Overall, 134 (43% [38-49]) participants developed MPOXROD during follow-up, most often bilaterally. Blindness following ulcerative keratitis occurred in two (1% [0-3]) of 224 participants.
Interpretation and novelty
Ophthalmic involvement in clade Ib mpox is common and frequently bilateral, with a substantial burden of keratitis and risk of vision loss. Systematic eye examinations should be integrated into mpox care, and targeted ophthalmic therapies should be developed.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the importance of integrating ophthalmological assessments into mpox surveillance and management. They also underscore the need for targeted ophthalmic therapies to address the significant burden of ocular complications associated with clade Ib mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a prospective cohort study nested within a larger study, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is strong, with a high confidence assessment based on the detailed methodology and clear reporting of results.

修正:使用深度学习模型的AI驱动的诊断的猴痘

Correction: AI-driven diagnosis of mpox using deep learning models
作者： Bassam W. Aboshosha, Shafiq Ul Rehman, Lamees N. Mahmoud, Ibrahim Sadek
摘要中文翻译[这更正了文章 DOI:10.1371/journal.pone.0352161。]。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在纠正之前一篇有关使用人工智能驱动的深度学习模型来诊断 猴痘 的文章。该更正解决了原始调查结果中的差异。
设计与对象
此更正是对 2026 年发表的原始文章的已发布勘误。它不提供新数据,但纠正了以前的错误。
核心方法
此次修正并没有引入新的方法,而是参考了原始研究的方法,其中涉及 猴痘 诊断 的深度学习模型。
主要结果
更正不会报告新结果,而是识别并解决原始文章研究结果中的错误。
解释与创新
作者将这一修正解释为原始研究结论准确性的提高,但没有引入新的发现。
科研与公卫意义
此次修正确保了 猴痘 人工智能驱动诊断工具的可靠性,这对于准确的 猴痘 和公共卫生应对至关重要。
局限与证据强度
更正并没有带来新的限制,但一旦错误得到解决,就证实了原始研究的结果是有效的。证据是强有力的,因为它是基于原始研究的数据。

机器学习辅助设计树突状细胞纳米疫苗,诱导针对猴痘的双胞胎免疫。

Machine learning-assisted design of a dendritic cell nanovaccine inducing twin immunity against monkeypox.
作者： Lu Yang, Xing-yu Xu, Yanrong Gao, Zhi-wen Gu, Yi Zhang, Si-Shi Lv, Abbaskhan Turaev, Huiyuan Wang, Yong-zhuo Huang
摘要中文翻译猴痘病毒(MPXV)在全球的出现迫切需要有效的 疫苗 来诱导强健的 免疫应答。在这里,我们报告了一种基于工程化树突状细胞膜囊泡(M1R-CMV)的仿生纳米疫苗平台,该平台通过基因工程设计,并通过机器学习辅助初步筛选物理化学参数,包括粒径(~190 nm)和zeta电位(-18 mV)。皮下递送 M1R-CMV 和 CpG 佐剂可诱导高滴度的 M1R 特异性 IgG 和强效中和抗体。疫苗 促进了广泛的 T 细胞激活,其特点是增强了脾脏和淋巴结中的 CD4 + 和 CD8 + T 细胞反应,并建立了持久的 T 细胞记忆。体外研究表明,M1R-CMV 可增强树突状细胞成熟和 T 细胞增殖。初免-加强免疫进一步增强了体液免疫和细胞免疫,中和抗体滴度和效应 T 细胞群显着增加。此外,疫苗 在体内表现出良好的安全性,没有观察到明显的毒性。我们的研究结果证明了一种仿生 疫苗 设计策略,将工程囊泡与机器学习辅助的理化分析相结合,为对抗猴痘提供了一种有前途的方法。

此外,疫苗 在体内表现出良好的安全性,没有观察到明显的毒性。

Machine learning-assisted design of a dendritic cell nanovaccine inducing twin immunity against monkeypox.

Authors: Lu Yang, Xing-yu Xu, Yanrong Gao, Zhi-wen Gu, Yi Zhang, Si-Shi Lv, Abbaskhan Turaev, Huiyuan Wang, Yong-zhuo Huang
Original abstract (English)
The global emergence of monkeypox virus (MPXV) has created an urgent need for effective vaccines that induce robust immune responses. Here, we report a biomimetic nano-vaccine platform based on engineered dendritic cell membrane vesicles displaying M1R (M1R-CMVs), designed via genetic engineering with machine learning-assisted preliminary screening of physicochemical parameters including particle size (~190 nm) and zeta potential (-18 mV). Subcutaneous delivery of M1R-CMVs with CpG adjuvant induced high titers of M1R-specific IgG and potent neutralizing antibodies. The vaccine promoted broad T cell activation, characterized by enhanced CD4 + and CD8 + T cell responses in spleen and lymph nodes, and established durable T cell memory. In vitro studies demonstrated that M1R-CMVs enhance dendritic cell maturation and T cell proliferation. Prime-boost immunization further amplified both humoral and cellular immunity, with significant increases in neutralizing antibody titers and effector T cell populations. Furthermore, the vaccine exhibited a favorable safety profile in vivo with no significant toxicity observed. Our findings demonstrate a biomimetic vaccine design strategy integrating engineered vesicles with machine learning-assisted physicochemical analysis, providing a promising approach to combat monkeypox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The global emergence of monkeypox virus (MPXV) has created an urgent need for effective vaccines that induce robust immune responses.
Design and population
Here, we report a biomimetic nano-vaccine platform based on engineered dendritic cell membrane vesicles displaying M1R (M1R-CMVs), designed via genetic engineering with machine learning-assisted preliminary screening of physicochemical parameters including particle size (~190 nm) and zeta potential (-18 mV).
Core methods
Our findings demonstrate a biomimetic vaccine design strategy integrating engineered vesicles with machine learning-assisted physicochemical analysis, providing a promising approach to combat monkeypox.
Main results
Subcutaneous delivery of M1R-CMVs with CpG adjuvant induced high titers of M1R-specific IgG and potent neutralizing antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

西北尼日利亚初级保健医师对猴痘知识水平的调查:一项在线横断面研究

Examining knowledge of Mpox among primary care physicians in North-Western Nigeria: an online cross-sectional study
作者： Godpower Chinedu Michael, Bukar Alhaji Grema, Hussaini Y. Magaji, Chinyere Nwosu, Taslim O. Lawal, Mohammed B. Usman, Ramatu Abubakar, Fatima A. Falaki, Muttaka Maude, Zainab Abdulkadir
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 猴痘 是一种罕见但重新出现的人畜共患疾病,可导致显着的发病率和 死亡率。由于初级保健医生充当医疗保健系统的看门人,因此他们必须精通识别和协调疑似 猴痘 患者的护理。然而,关于尼日利亚西北地缘政治区初级保健医生猴痘知识的研究却很少。目的 本研究评估了尼日利亚西北部初级保健医生对 猴痘 的了解及其相关的社会人口统计学因素。方法 这项横断面在线研究采用多阶段抽样技术对尼日利亚西北部七个州各医院的家庭医学/普通门诊和儿科门诊诊所工作的初级保健医生进行调查。最小样本量 153 是使用假设 50% 患病率 良好的 猴痘 知识、5% 的误差幅度和 95% 的置信水平计算得出的;添加 10% 无响应缓冲液后,该值增加至 170。共有 155 名医生完成了调查,完成率为 91.2%。使用预先测试的在线调查问卷收集数据,并使用 Epi Info(第 7 版)进行描述性和推论性统计分析。结果 参与者的平均年龄为 38.8 ± 7.4 岁,其中男性占多数(60.7%)。大部分为普通门诊医生(77.4%)、执业经验10年以上(63.2%)、高级挂号师(36.1%)。只有 18.9% 的人有诊断或治疗 猴痘 的经验。九人 (5.8%) 对 猴痘 有良好的了解,平均总体知识得分为 61.7 ± 12.5%。具有≥ 10年实践经验(p = 0.049)、高级专业干部(p = 0.031)以及参与诊断或处理猴痘(p = 0.021)的参与者与较高的平均知识得分相关。结论 总体良好知识率明显较低,表明参与者对该疾病各个方面的知识存在显着差距。高级专业干部、多年的实践经验以及诊断和猴痘管理经验与较高但次优的知识得分相关。因此,需要有针对性的教育干预措施,以增强他们在初级保健机构中识别和协调 猴痘 病例的准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘是一种再次出现的人兽共患病,可导致显著发病率和死亡率,而初级保健医师是识别和管理疑似病例的关键守门人。此前缺乏评估西北尼日利亚初级保健医师对猴痘知识水平的研究,形成了亟待本研究填补的知识空白。
设计与对象
本研究为一项横断面在线研究,对尼日利亚西北部七个州医院家庭医学/普通门诊和儿科门诊工作的初级保健医师进行了调查。共有155名医师完成了调查,完成率为91.2%。
核心方法
使用预先测试的在线问卷收集数据,并使用 Epi Info(第 7 版)进行描述性和推论性统计分析。采用多阶段抽样技术,使用假设的 50% 患病率 良好的 猴痘 知识、5% 的误差幅度和 95% 的置信水平计算出 153 的最小样本量,在添加 10% 的无响应缓冲区后增加到 170。
主要结果
参与者的平均年龄为 38.8 ± 7.4 岁,其中男性占多数(60.7%)。只有 18.9% 的人有诊断或处理 猴痘 的经验,5.8% 的人对 猴痘 有良好的了解,平均总体知识得分为 61.7 ± 12.5%。具有≥10年实践经验(p=0.049)、高级专业干部(p=0.031)以及诊断或处理猴痘经验(p=0.021)的参与者与较高的平均知识得分相关。
解释与创新
作者将明显较低的总体良好知识率解释为表明参与者对 猴痘 的知识存在显着差距。他们强调,高级专业干部、多年的实践以及诊断和管理经验与较高但次优的知识分数相关,这强调了有针对性的教育干预的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要有针对性的教育干预措施,以增强初级保健医生在初级保健机构中识别和协调 猴痘 病例的准备,这可以改善尼日利亚西北部的 监测、诊断 和患者管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和对自我报告知识分数的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能引入反应偏差。由于样本量较小且使用方便抽样方法,证据强度中等,这可能会影响对其他地区或医疗机构的普遍适用性。

Examining knowledge of Mpox among primary care physicians in North-Western Nigeria: an online cross-sectional study

Authors: Godpower Chinedu Michael, Bukar Alhaji Grema, Hussaini Y. Magaji, Chinyere Nwosu, Taslim O. Lawal, Mohammed B. Usman, Ramatu Abubakar, Fatima A. Falaki, Muttaka Maude, Zainab Abdulkadir
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Mpox is a rare but re-emerging zoonotic disease that causes significant morbidity and mortality. As primary care physicians serve as gatekeepers in the healthcare system, it is essential that they be proficient at identifying and coordinating care for suspected Mpox patients. However, there is a scarcity of studies on the knowledge of Mpox among primary care doctors in the northwestern geopolitical zone of Nigeria. Objectives This study assessed the knowledge of Mpox and its associated sociodemographic factors among primary care physicians in northwestern Nigeria. Methods This cross-sectional online study employed a multi-stage sampling technique to survey primary care physicians working in Family Medicine/General Outpatient and Pediatric Outpatient Clinics across hospitals in the seven states of North-Western Nigeria. The minimum sample size of 153 was calculated using an assumed 50% prevalence of good Mpox knowledge, a 5% margin of error, and a 95% confidence level; this was increased to 170 after adding a 10% non-response buffer. A total of 155 physicians completed the survey, giving a completion rate of 91.2%. Data were collected using a pretested, online questionnaire, and descriptive and inferential statistical analyses were done using Epi Info (version 7). Results The mean age of participants was 38.8 ± 7.4 years, with male preponderance (60.7%). Most were general outpatient clinic doctors (77.4%), with ≥ 10 years of practice experience (63.2%), and senior registrars (36.1%). Only 18.9% had prior experience in diagnosing or managing Mpox. Nine (5.8%) had good knowledge of Mpox, with a mean overall knowledge score of 61.7 ± 12.5%. Participants with ≥ 10 years of practice experience ( p = 0.049), senior professional cadres ( p = 0.031), and involvement in diagnosing or managing Mpox ( p = 0.021) were associated with higher mean knowledge scores. Conclusions The overall good knowledge rate was notably low, indicating a significant gap in participants’ knowledge of the various aspects of the disease. Senior professional cadres, long years of practice, and experience in the diagnosis and management of Mpox were associated with higher yet suboptimal knowledge scores. Hence, targeted educational interventions are needed to enhance their preparedness to identify and coordinate Mpox cases in primary care settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox is a re-emerging zoonotic disease with significant morbidity and mortality, and primary care physicians are critical gatekeepers for identifying and managing suspected cases. There was a scarcity of studies assessing Mpox knowledge among primary care doctors in North-Western Nigeria, creating a knowledge gap that this study aimed to address.
Design and population
This was a cross-sectional online study conducted among primary care physicians working in Family Medicine/General Outpatient and Pediatric Outpatient Clinics across hospitals in the seven states of North-Western Nigeria. A total of 155 physicians completed the survey, representing a 91.2% completion rate.
Core methods
Data were collected using a pretested, online questionnaire, and descriptive and inferential statistical analyses were performed using Epi Info (version 7). A multi-stage sampling technique was employed, and the minimum sample size of 153 was calculated using an assumed 50% prevalence of good Mpox knowledge, a 5% margin of error, and a 95% confidence level, increased to 170 after adding a 10% non-response buffer.
Main results
The mean age of participants was 38.8 ± 7.4 years, with a male preponderance (60.7%). Only 18.9% had prior experience in diagnosing or managing Mpox, and 5.8% had good knowledge of Mpox, with a mean overall knowledge score of 61.7 ± 12.5%. Participants with ≥10 years of practice experience (p=0.049), senior professional cadres (p=0.031), and experience in diagnosing or managing Mpox (p=0.021) were associated with higher mean knowledge scores.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the notably low overall good knowledge rate as indicating a significant gap in participants' knowledge of Mpox. They highlighted that senior professional cadres, long years of practice, and experience in diagnosis and management were associated with higher yet suboptimal knowledge scores, underscoring the need for targeted educational interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted educational interventions are needed to enhance primary care physicians' preparedness to identify and coordinate Mpox cases in primary care settings, which could improve surveillance, diagnosis, and patient management in North-Western Nigeria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported knowledge scores limit causal inference and may introduce response bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and the use of a convenience sampling method, which may affect generalisability to other regions or healthcare settings.

评估 猴痘 公共卫生咨询的支持搜索的大语言模型界面:基于指南的比较研究

Evaluating search-enabled large language model interfaces for mpox public health consultation: a guideline-based comparative study
作者： Qi-Qi Zheng, Ru Chen, Ming-Ming Cai, Yan-Yan Chen, Xiu-Li Lin, Ting-Ting Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 公众越来越多地使用支持搜索的大语言模型界面来获取健康信息,但其在 猴痘 相关公共卫生咨询中的表现仍不清楚。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了它们的安全性、准确性、同理心、可靠性/信息质量和可读性。我们使用 52 个预定义的 猴痘 相关公众咨询问题进行了单查询比较横断面评估。每个问题都向 6 个支持搜索的 LLM 界面中的每一个界面提交一次,产生 312 个第一响应。
核心方法
根据基于指南的参考框架对反应进行评估。安全性被编码为二元结果,而准确性和同理心则按 5 分制进行评分。使用 DISCERN、EQIP、JAMA 基准标准和 GQS 评估可靠性/信息质量。使用六个既定的可读性指数来评估可读性。五名经过培训的评估员独立评估了人工评分的结果。
主要结果
Unsafe responses were relatively infrequent but occurred in all six interfaces, with safe-response rates ranging from 86.5 to 92.3%. No pairwise difference in Safety remained statistically significant after Benjamini–Hochberg correction. Overall differences across interfaces were statistically significant for Accuracy, Empathy, all four reliability/information quality measures, and all six readability indices.
解释与创新
本杰明-霍赫伯格调整的事后分析确定了特定结果的成对差异,尽管成对模式因测量而异。经评估的支持搜索的 LLM 界面在安全性、准确性、同理心、可靠性/信息质量和可读性方面表现出异构性能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在评估或考虑进行 猴痘 相关公共卫生咨询时,需要基于指南的评估、来源透明度、可读性优化和强大的安全保障措施。
局限与证据强度
结果代表特定于时间和配置的接口级快照;它们不应被孤立地归因于底层基础模型,也不应被解释为跨会话、版本或设置建立可再现的性能或稳定的层次结构。

Evaluating search-enabled large language model interfaces for mpox public health consultation: a guideline-based comparative study

Authors: Qi-Qi Zheng, Ru Chen, Ming-Ming Cai, Yan-Yan Chen, Xiu-Li Lin, Ting-Ting Wang
Original abstract (English)
Background Search-enabled large language model interfaces are increasingly used by the public for health information, but their performance in mpox-related public health consultation remains unclear. This study evaluated their safety, accuracy, empathy, reliability/information quality, and readability. Methods We conducted a single-query comparative cross-sectional evaluation using 52 predefined mpox-related public consultation questions. Each question was submitted once to each of six search-enabled LLM interfaces, yielding 312 first responses. Responses were assessed against a guideline-based reference framework. Safety was coded as a binary outcome, while accuracy and empathy were rated on 5-point scales. Reliability/information quality was evaluated using DISCERN, EQIP, JAMA benchmark criteria, and GQS. Readability was assessed using six established readability indices. Five trained raters independently evaluated the human-scored outcomes. Results Unsafe responses were relatively infrequent but occurred in all six interfaces, with safe-response rates ranging from 86.5 to 92.3%. No pairwise difference in Safety remained statistically significant after Benjamini–Hochberg correction. Overall differences across interfaces were statistically significant for Accuracy, Empathy, all four reliability/information quality measures, and all six readability indices. Benjamini–Hochberg-adjusted post hoc analyses identified outcome-specific pairwise differences, although the pairwise patterns varied across measures. Conclusion The evaluated search-enabled LLM interfaces showed heterogeneous performance across safety, accuracy, empathy, reliability/information quality, and readability. Although unsafe responses were relatively uncommon, potentially harmful outputs occurred in every interface. These findings support the need for guideline-based evaluation, source transparency, readability optimization, and robust safety safeguards when such interfaces are evaluated or considered for mpox-related public health consultation. The results represent a time- and configuration-specific interface-level snapshot; they should not be attributed to the underlying base models in isolation or interpreted as establishing reproducible performance or a stable hierarchy across sessions, versions, or settings.

猴痘病毒 传播动力学的稳定性和灵敏度分析

Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus
作者： D.A. Arhinful, A. Y. Anafo, I. Ampofi, A.Y. Anafo, Ampofi I.
摘要中文翻译基本再生数$R_{0}$是使用下一代矩阵方法导出的。这些结果为设计有针对性的 猴痘 控制干预措施提供了定量基础,特别是通过减少接触和提高 治疗 覆盖范围。猴痘病毒 D.A.的传播动力学稳定性和灵敏度分析Ampofi 矿业技术大学数学科学系,加纳塔夸 通讯作者:[email protected] 文章信息:期刊:International Journal of Advances in Applied Mathematics and Mechanics (IJAAMM) Volume: 14,Issue: 1,Pages: 108-121 Issue Date: September 2026 License: CC BY 4.0 DOI: https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 文章历史:2026-06-22 00:00:00 接收,2026-08-14 00:00:00 修订,2026-08-25 00:00:00 接受,2026 年 8 月 27 日在线发布引用:D.A. Ampofi,传播 动力学的稳定性和灵敏度分析,Int。14(1)(2026)108-121。https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 摘要 针对人类和啮齿类动物群体相互作用中的 猴痘 传播 动态制定了确定性区室模型。人群分为易感人群、暴露人群、传染人群、隔离人群和康复人群;将啮齿类动物分为易感、暴露和传染性类别。纳入了隔离至易感人群的途径,以捕获重新进入高危人群的未发现病例。当 $R_{0}<1$ 时,无病平衡的局部渐近稳定性成立,并且使用 Castillo-Chavez 和 Song 方法证明了全局渐近稳定性。还导出了地方性平衡的劳斯-赫尔维茨条件。归一化前向敏感性分析确定了人与人之间暴露的比率和比例...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过稳定性和敏感性分析来分析 传播 的动态,以了解影响其传播的因素并为控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
制定了确定性区室模型来研究人类和啮齿动物群体相互作用中的 猴痘 传播 动态,其中人类群体分为易感、暴露、传染、隔离和康复类别,啮齿动物群体分为易感、暴露和传染类别。
核心方法
该研究使用了确定性区室模型,结合了从隔离到易感的途径来解释未发现的病例。使用下一代矩阵方法计算基本再生数$R_0$,并使用Routh-Hurwitz条件和Castillo-Chavez和Song方法分析平衡稳定性。
主要结果
推导了基本传染数$R_0$,发现人传人传播率和暴露人群进入传染类别的比例是传播的主导驱动因素。提高回收率和 治疗 率可减少 $R_0$。
解释与创新
研究结果提供了对驱动 猴痘 传播 的关键因素的定量理解,并强调了减少接触和提高 治疗 覆盖率在控制疾病方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果为设计有针对性的干预措施以减少 猴痘 传播 奠定了基础,特别是通过关注接触者追踪和增强 治疗 协议。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于确定性模型,该模型可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,例如 传播 比率的随机性或变异性。该模型的稳定性分析证据充分,但在未经进一步实证验证的情况下,其对现实场景的适用性有限。

Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus

Authors: D.A. Arhinful, A. Y. Anafo, I. Ampofi, A.Y. Anafo, Ampofi I.
Original abstract (English)
The basic reproduction number $R_{0}$ is derived using the next-generation matrix method. These results offer a quantitative basis for designing targeted Mpox control interventions, particularly through contact reduction and improved treatment coverage. Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus D.A. Ampofi Department of Mathematical Sciences, University of Mines and Technology, Tarkwa, Ghana Corresponding Author: [email protected] Article Information: Journal: International Journal of Advances in Applied Mathematics and Mechanics (IJAAMM) Volume: 14, Issue: 1, Pages: 108-121 Issue Date: September 2026 License: CC BY 4.0 DOI: https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 Article History: Received 2026-06-22 00:00:00, Revised 2026-08-14 00:00:00, Accepted 2026-08-25 00:00:00, Published online 27 August 2026 Cite: D.A. Ampofi , Stability and sensitivity analysis of transmission dynamics of Mpox virus, Int. 14(1) (2026) 108-121. https://doi.org/10.26541/ijaamm.2026.140110 Abstract A deterministic compartmental model is formulated for Mpox transmission dynamics in interacting human and rodent populations. The human population is divided into susceptible, exposed, infectious, quarantined, and recovered classes; the rodent population into susceptible, exposed, and infectious classes. A quarantined-to-susceptible pathway is incorporated to capture undetected cases re-entering the at-risk population. Local asymptotic stability of the disease-free equilibrium is established whenever $R_{0}<1$, and global asymptotic stability is proved using the Castillo-Chavez and Song approach. Routh-Hurwitz conditions are also derived for the endemic equilibrium. Normalised forward sensitivity analysis identifies the human-to-human rate and proportion of exposed h...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to analyze the transmission dynamics of Mpox virus through stability and sensitivity analysis to understand the factors influencing its spread and inform control strategies.
Design and population
A deterministic compartmental model was formulated to study Mpox transmission dynamics in interacting human and rodent populations, with the human population divided into susceptible, exposed, infectious, quarantined, and recovered classes, and the rodent population into susceptible, exposed, and infectious classes.
Core methods
The study used a deterministic compartmental model, incorporating a quarantined-to-susceptible pathway to account for undetected cases. The basic reproduction number $R_0$ was calculated using the next-generation matrix method, and stability of equilibria was analyzed using Routh-Hurwitz conditions and Castillo-Chavez and Song approaches.
Main results
The basic reproduction number $R_0$ was derived, and it was found that the human-to-human transmission rate and the proportion of exposed humans entering the infectious class are the dominant drivers of transmission. Increasing recovery and treatment rates was shown to reduce $R_0$.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a quantitative understanding of the key factors driving Mpox transmission and highlight the importance of contact reduction and improved treatment coverage in controlling the disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results offer a foundation for designing targeted interventions to reduce Mpox transmission, particularly by focusing on contact tracing and enhancing treatment protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a deterministic model, which may not fully capture real-world complexities such as stochasticity or variability in transmission rates. The evidence is strong for the model's stability analysis but limited in its applicability to real-world scenarios without further empirical validation.

HECA-YOLOv11:一种高精度混合注意力网络,用于实时猴痘皮肤病变检测并具有可解释性

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability
作者： Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
摘要中文翻译简介:全球 暴发 猴痘风险需要高效的诊断工具。目前的方法速度缓慢,并且难以区分相似的皮肤病变,而深度学习模型缺乏多类分类的准确性和可解释性。本研究旨在开发一种高性能、实时筛查工具,用于早期检测猴痘和类似病症。方法:本研究提出了一种增强的架构,将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制纳入 YOLOv11 中,以改进特征选择。HECA 对输入特征图执行全局平均池化 (GAP) 和最大平均池化 (MAP)。该研究利用空间变换和像素域增强,建立并增强了包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的四类数据集。梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 生成用于病变定位可视化的热图,而基于 PyQt5 的交互式诊断界面则简化了临床工作流程。结果:实验结果表明,与测试集上的现有方法(EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201 和 YOLOv11s)相比,所提出的模型具有优越的性能,具有增强的检测速度、准确度(98.9%)、精确度(99%)、召回率(99.3%)和 F1 分数(99.1%)。在所有评估指标中,HECA-YOLOv11 的性能均优于 SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11 超过 5.1%。热图分析证实了该模型对病变区域的关注,与医学推理相一致。讨论:集成的注意力机制和可解释性技术有效地解决了自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制。高性能和低延迟标志着可靠临床决策支持的重大进步,对早期 暴发 遏制具有重要意义。结论:HECA-YOLOv11框架为早期猴痘筛查提供了高度准确和高效的工具。其标准化界面在加速公共卫生应对和改善新发传染病的全球控制方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生对快速、准确的诊断工具的迫切需求,以减轻 猴痘 的全球 暴发 风险,强调现有方法速度缓慢,而且深度学习模型缺乏多类皮肤病变分类的准确性和可解释性。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一个深度学习框架 (HECA-YOLOv11),旨在使用四类图像数据集实时筛查 猴痘 和类似的皮肤病变。
核心方法
该架构将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制集成到YOLOv11中,将全局平均池化和最大平均池化应用于输入特征图,并使用空间变换和像素域增强来增强包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的数据集。
主要结果
在测试集上,HECA-YOLOv11 实现了 98.9% 的准确率、99% 的精确率、99.3% 的召回率和 99.1% 的 F1 分数,优于 EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201、YOLOv11s、SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11所有指标均为 5.1%。
解释与创新
作者将集成注意力机制和可解释性技术解释为有效解决自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制,标志着早期 猴痘 筛查的可靠临床决策支持的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
HECA-YOLOv11 的标准化界面和高性能表明有可能通过早期检测加速公共卫生反应并改善对新发传染病的全球控制。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告数据集规模、地理或人群范围、外部验证或前瞻性临床评估,限制了结论的普遍性,且证据强度为中等。

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: The global outbreak risk of monkeypox necessitates efficient diagnostic tools. Current methods are slow and struggle to differentiate similar skin lesions, while deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class classification. This study aims to develop a high-performance, real-time screening tool for early detection of monkeypox and similar conditions. Methods: This study proposes an enhanced architecture that incorporates the Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11 to improve feature selection. HECA performs Global Average Pooling (GAP) and Max Average Pooling (MAP) on input feature maps. Using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements, the research establishes and augments a four-class dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images. Gradient-Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) generates heatmaps for lesion localization visualization, while a PyQt5-based interactive diagnostic interface streamlines clinical workflows. Results: Experimental results demonstrate the proposed model’s superior performance compared to existing approaches (EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, and YOLOv11s) on the test set, with enhanced detection speed, accuracy (98.9%), precision (99%), recall (99.3%), and F1-score (99.1%). HECA-YOLOv11 outperforms SE-YOLOv11 and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all evaluation metrics. Heatmap analysis confirms the model’s focus on lesion regions, aligning with medical reasoning. Discussion: The integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques effectively address key limitations in automated skin lesion detection. The high performance and low latency signify a major advancement towards reliable clinical decision support, with important implications for early outbreak containment. Conclusion: The HECA-YOLOv11 framework provides a highly accurate and efficient tool for early monkeypox screening. Its standardized interface holds significant potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for rapid, accurate diagnostic tools to mitigate the global outbreak risk of mpox, highlighting that existing methods are slow and deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class skin lesion classification.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a deep learning framework (HECA-YOLOv11) designed for real-time screening of mpox and similar skin lesions using a four-class image dataset.
Core methods
The proposed architecture integrates a Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11, applying Global Average Pooling and Max Average Pooling to input feature maps, and augments a dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements.
Main results
On the test set, HECA-YOLOv11 achieved 98.9% accuracy, 99% precision, 99.3% recall, and 99.1% F1-score, outperforming EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, YOLOv11s, SE-YOLOv11, and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all metrics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques as effectively addressing key limitations in automated skin lesion detection, marking a major advancement toward reliable clinical decision support for early mpox screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The standardized interface and high performance of HECA-YOLOv11 suggest potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases through early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report dataset size, geographic or demographic scope, external validation, or prospective clinical evaluation, limiting generalisability and leaving the strength of evidence moderate.

猴痘:一种罕见的性传播皮肤病

Mpox: a rare sexually transmitted dermatosis

问题与背景

Question and background
The study aimed to investigate mpox as a rare sexually transmitted dermatosis, addressing the need to understand its epidemiology and clinical characteristics in this context.
Design and population

北卡罗来纳州,美国,2023年10月–2025年5月污水中发现的猴痘病毒监测

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025
作者： Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
摘要中文翻译我们使用 +17 天的窗口分析了废水检测 (n = 47) 和临床病例 (n = 45)。早期工作表明,通过尿液、粪便、呼吸道分泌物和皮肤病变排出的病毒可导致废水中可检测到 MPXV,支持基于废水的 流行病学 作为监测和量化群落 流行病学 的可行方法 (2,7,8)。在一项研究中,废水中的 MPXV 检测显示先于临床病例的识别,突出了其早期 暴发 检测的潜力 (9)。另一项研究报告称,在报告临床病例之前,废水病毒浓度有所增加 (5)。为了评估北卡罗来纳州的 猴痘 废水 监测 计划,我们进行了这项研究,以估计相对于已确诊的临床病例的敏感性、特异性、阳性预测值 (PPV) 和阴性预测值 (NPV)。研究区域和 监测 期间 我们进行了回顾性诊断性能分析,以评估废水 监测 在北卡罗来纳州检测 MPXV 循环的情况。该研究包括来自 16 家公有污水 治疗 工厂 (WWTP) 的数据,这些工厂具有明确的集水区,服务的总人口为 2,002,864 人(表 1)。免责声明:早期发布的文章不被视为最终版本。任何更改将在文章正式发布当月反映在在线版本中。第 32 卷增刊 – 2026 年夏季增刊问题 2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 5 月,美国北卡罗来纳州废水中的其他猴痘病毒 猴痘 引用本文媒体摘要 2023 年 9 月,北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部在上次报告 5 个月后发现了一个新的 猴痘 分支 II 病例。评估可能无法识别的传输...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自首例非流行区猴痘病例报告以来,猴痘已成为美国日益严重的公共卫生问题,促使需要改进临床病例报告以外的猴痘病毒监测方法。
设计与对象
During October 29, 2023–May 2025, 801 wastewater samples were collected from 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents in North Carolina, USA.
核心方法
使用 CDC Non-variola 正痘病毒 通用实时 PCR 测试对废水样本进行分析,病毒基因拷贝根据下水道内的流速和人口进行归一化。理论上估计检测限为 500 拷贝/L,并使用 +17 天的对齐窗口来评估与报告的临床病例的时间一致性。
主要结果
废水检测用于识别报告的时间一致的 猴痘 病例的总体敏感性为 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%–16.7%),当仅限于高于检测限的检测时,该敏感性增加至 1.4% (95% CI 0.4%–5.0%)。特异性较高(95.1% 至 97.7%),阴性预测值约为 83%。
解释与创新
该研究表明,尽管灵敏度较低,废水 监测 仍可提供补充性的公共卫生意识,因为没有时间一致病例的废水检测可能反映的是未被识别的感染,而不是误报。
科研与公卫意义
废水 监测 可以通过提供高特异性和阴性预测值来补充 猴痘 的临床病例报告,特别是在低 发病率 环境中,尽管早期预警的敏感性仍然有限。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖报告的临床病例作为不完善的参考标准,限制了诊断准确性的估计,并且限制在检测限以上的检测显着降低了灵敏度,可能会损害早期预警能力。

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025

Authors: Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
Original abstract (English)
We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window. Early work demonstrated that viral shedding through urine, feces, respiratory secretions, and skin lesions can result in detectable MPXV in wastewater, supporting wastewater-based epidemiology as a viable approach for monitoring and quantifying community transmission (2,7,8). In 1 study, MPXV detections in wastewater were shown to precede the identification of the clinical cases, highlighting its potential for early outbreak detection (9). Another study reported increases in wastewater viral concentrations ahead of reported clinical cases (5). To evaluate mpox wastewater surveillance program in North Carolina, we conducted this study to estimate sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value (PPV), and negative predictive value (NPV) relative to confirmed clinical cases. Study Area and Surveillance Period We conducted a retrospective diagnostic performance analysis to evaluate wastewater surveillance for detecting MPXV circulation in North Carolina. The study included data from 16 publicly owned wastewater treatment plants (WWTPs) with defined catchment areas serving a combined population of 2,002,864 persons (Table 1). Disclaimer: Early release articles are not considered as final versions. Any changes will be reflected in the online version in the month the article is officially released. Volume 32, Supplement –Summer 2026 SUPPLEMENT ISSUE Other Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025 Cite This Article Citation for Media Abstract In September 2023, the North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services identified a new case of mpox clade II 5 months after the last previous report. To assess possible unrecognized transm...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since the first mpox case in a nonendemic region, mpox has become an increasing public health concern in the United States, prompting the need for improved surveillance methods beyond clinical case reporting.
Design and population
During October 29, 2023–May 2025, 801 wastewater samples were collected from 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents in North Carolina, USA.
Core methods
Wastewater samples were analyzed using the CDC Non-variola Orthopoxvirus Generic Real-Time PCR Test, with viral gene copies normalized to flow rate and population within the sewershed. The assay limit of detection was theoretically estimated at 500 copies/L, and a +17-day alignment window was used to assess temporal concordance with reported clinical cases.
Main results
The overall sensitivity of wastewater detection for identifying temporally aligned reported mpox cases was 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%–16.7%), increasing to 1.4% (95% CI 0.4%–5.0%) when restricted to detections above the limit of detection. Specificity was high (95.1% to 97.7%), and negative predictive value was approximately 83%.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that wastewater surveillance provides complementary public health awareness despite low sensitivity, as wastewater detections without temporally aligned cases may reflect unrecognized infections rather than false positives.
Scientific and public-health significance
Wastewater surveillance can complement clinical case reporting for mpox by providing high specificity and negative predictive value, particularly in low-incidence settings, though sensitivity remains limited for early warning.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on reported clinical cases as an imperfect reference standard limits diagnostic accuracy estimation, and restricting to detections above the limit of detection markedly reduces sensitivity, potentially compromising early warning capacity.

感染和败血症

Infections and sepsis
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译摘要 本章提供了妊娠期感染筛查、管理和预防的最新概述,特别关注败血症。它涵盖了梅毒、艾滋病毒、乙型肝炎和风疹产前筛查的国家指南,强调早期检测和多学科护理对于减少垂直 传播 和改善结果的重要性。本章详细介绍了 B 族溶血性链球菌的重要性,概述了危险因素、筛查方案以及产时抗生素预防的建议。讨论了流感、尿路感染、水痘、弓形体病、猴痘 和 COVID-19 等常见感染,并提供了有关 诊断、治疗 选项和预防的实用助产建议。本章强调了由于免疫变化和产科干预导致妊娠期脓毒症风险增加,强调需要及时识别、快速干预和多学科合作。提出了关键的临床症状、评估工具和脓毒症六号方案以支持早期识别和管理。本章自始至终整合了当前的研究、国家标准和现实世界的实践,使助产士能够提供安全、有效的护理,并降低孕产妇和新生儿的发病率以及与感染和败血症相关的 死亡率。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本章讨论了更新妊娠期感染筛查、管理和预防指南的必要性,特别关注败血症,以改善孕产妇和新生儿的结局。
设计与对象
该研究是一本书的章节,概述了与妊娠期感染和败血症相关的国家指南和临床实践,涵盖了一系列病原体和病症。
核心方法
本章概述了基于当前研究和国家标准的针对各种感染(包括 猴痘)的筛查方案、诊断方法和 治疗 建议。
主要结果
本章报告称,由于免疫学变化和产科干预,猴痘 等感染会增加妊娠期败血症的风险。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调及时识别和快速干预妊娠期败血症的重要性,特别是对于 猴痘 等感染,以确保安全有效的护理。
科研与公卫意义
该发现为助产士和医护人员提供了妊娠期感染与脓毒症临床管理的信息,特别强调了猴痘的风险及相关管理规程。
局限与证据强度
证据基于对当前研究与国家标准的摘要式回顾,为妊娠期感染与脓毒症管理建议提供了中等水平的信心。

Infections and sepsis

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
Abstract This chapter provides an up-to-date overview of infection screening, management, and prevention in pregnancy, with a particular focus on sepsis. It covers national guidance on antenatal screening for syphilis, HIV, hepatitis B, and rubella, emphasising the importance of early detection and multidisciplinary care to reduce vertical transmission and improve outcomes. The chapter details the significance of group B haemolytic Streptococcus, outlining risk factors, screening protocols, and recommendations for intrapartum antibiotic prophylaxis. Common infections such as influenza, urinary tract infections, chickenpox, toxoplasmosis, Mpox, and COVID-19 are discussed, with practical midwifery advice for diagnosis, treatment options, and prevention. The chapter highlights the increased risk of sepsis in pregnancy due to immunological changes and obstetric interventions, stressing the need for prompt recognition, rapid intervention, and multidisciplinary collaboration. Key clinical signs, assessment tools, and the Sepsis Six protocol are presented to support early identification and management. Throughout, the chapter integrates current research, national standards, and real-world practice, equipping midwives to deliver safe, effective care and reduce maternal and neonatal morbidity and mortality associated with infections and sepsis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This chapter addresses the need for updated guidance on infection screening, management, and prevention in pregnancy, with a specific focus on sepsis, to improve maternal and neonatal outcomes.
Design and population
The study is a book chapter that provides an overview of national guidelines and clinical practices related to infections and sepsis in pregnancy, covering a range of pathogens and conditions.
Core methods
The chapter outlines screening protocols, diagnostic approaches, and treatment recommendations for various infections, including Mpox, based on current research and national standards.
Main results
The chapter reports that infections such as Mpox pose an increased risk of sepsis in pregnancy due to immunological changes and obstetric interventions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as emphasizing the importance of prompt recognition and rapid intervention for sepsis in pregnancy, particularly for infections like Mpox, to ensure safe and effective care.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform midwives and healthcare providers about the clinical management of infections and sepsis in pregnancy, specifically highlighting the risks and protocols for Mpox.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract-only review of current research and national standards, which provides a moderate level of confidence in the recommendations for managing infections and sepsis in pregnancy.

病毒与开发科学知识的发展需要我们理解

Suivre les virus et développer les connaissances scientifiques nécessaires à leur compréhension
作者： Sharon Muzaki
摘要中文翻译在喀麦隆出现疑似猴痘、病毒性出血热、狂犬病及其他病毒性疾病的疑似病例时,样本会被送至我在雅温得的喀麦隆巴斯德中心实验室。我对这些样本进行分析,并将结果传递给公共卫生部。但我的工作并不止于诊断。我希望了解病毒的来源、传播方式以及为何某些病毒仍隐藏不露。最初,我受母亲——一位护士的影响,立志成为医生。但由于最近的大学未设医学院,我转而攻读医学分析专业。在医院实习期间,我被致病微生物深深吸引,并意识到自己想要研究感染。获得学士学位后,我在雅温得继续攻读专业硕士。第二年,我被选中进入加蓬一个极具竞争性的区域研究型硕士项目。在完成这两个学业的同时,我继续开展研究工作,随后在法国获得了首个研究实习机会,这进一步增强了我对病毒学的兴趣。我的博士论文研究了人类T淋巴细胞趋化性病毒1型(HTLV-1),一种人类反转录病毒,其作用机制的研究程度远不及艾滋病病毒(HIV)。在加蓬攻读博士期间,我与森林社区合作,从2000多人身上采集样本。这一经历塑造了我的「One Health」(同一健康)理念。我意识到,病毒与其流行环境密切相关,人类、动物及生态系统的健康必须被视为一个整体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 传播 的起源、猴痘病毒 和持续性,特别是在基因组数据有限和喀麦隆当地研究能力需要的情况下。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 1979 年至 2022 年报告的 猴痘 病例,重点关注位于雅温得喀麦隆巴斯德中心的 猴痘、病毒性出血热和狂犬病国家参考实验室,并包括来自森林社区的 2,000 多个样本。
核心方法
该研究利用了基因组测序技术,包括下一代测序,并涉及与国际网络的合作来分析样本并发展当地的研究能力。
主要结果
研究发现,猴痘病毒 的两个主要分支都在喀麦隆传播,萨纳加河是它们分布的边界,不同的病毒谱系与不同的森林生态系统相关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,该病毒可能与特定环境中的不同动物 储存宿主 有关,这引发了对其 传播 动态以及综合 One Health 方法的必要性的疑问。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了解当地病毒生态学的重要性以及发展区域诊断和研究能力以有效解决 猴痘 暴发 问题的必要性,从而为 监测 战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于缺乏专门资金和依赖国际合作而受到限制,但由于全面的基因组分析和当地背景,证据是强有力的。

Suivre les virus et développer les connaissances scientifiques nécessaires à leur compréhension

Authors: Sharon Muzaki
Original abstract (English)
Lorsque des cas suspects de mpox, de fièvres hémorragiques virales, de rage et d’autres maladies virales apparaissent au Cameroun, des échantillons parviennent à mon laboratoire au Centre Pasteur du Cameroun, à Yaoundé. Je les analyse et je transmets les résultats au ministère de la Santé publique. Mais mon travail ne s’arrête pas au diagnostic. Je veux comprendre d’où viennent les virus, comment ils se propagent et pourquoi certains restent cachés. Au départ, je voulais devenir médecin, inspirée par ma mère, qui était infirmière. Mais comme l’université la plus proche ne disposait pas de faculté de médecine, j’ai étudié l’analyse médicale à la place. Au cours de mes stages à l’hôpital, j’ai été fascinée par les organismes à l’origine des maladies et j’ai réalisé que je voulais comprendre les infections. Après avoir obtenu ma licence, j’ai poursuivi un master professionnel à Yaoundé. Au cours de la deuxième année, j’ai été sélectionnée pour intégrer un programme régional de master de recherche très sélectif au Gabon. J’ai mené à bien ces deux cursus tout en poursuivant mes travaux de recherche, puis j’ai décroché mon premier stage de recherche en France, ce qui a renforcé mon intérêt pour la virologie. Ma thèse de doctorat portait sur le virus T-lymphotrope humain de type 1 (HTLV-1), un rétrovirus humain dont on connaît encore beaucoup moins les mécanismes d’action que ceux du VIH. Au cours de mes recherches doctorales au Gabon, j’ai travaillé avec des communautés forestières, prélevant des échantillons auprès de plus de 2 000 personnes. Cette expérience a façonné ma vision « One Health ». J’ai pris conscience que les virus sont liés aux environnements dans lesquels ils circulent, et que la santé humaine, animale et celle des écosystèmes doivent être considérées conjoi...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the origins, transmission and persistence of the mpox virus, particularly in the context of limited genomic data and the need for local research capacity in Cameroon.
Design and population
The research involved analyzing reported cases of mpox from 1979 to 2022, with a focus on the national reference laboratory for mpox, viral hemorrhagic fevers and rabies at the Centre Pasteur du Cameroun in Yaoundé, and included over 2,000 samples from forest communities.
Core methods
The study utilized genomic sequencing techniques, including next-generation sequencing, and involved collaboration with international networks to analyze samples and develop local research capabilities.
Main results
The study found that both main clades of the mpox virus circulate in Cameroon, with the Sanaga River acting as a boundary between their distribution, and different viral lineages associated with distinct forest ecosystems.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the virus may be linked to different animal reservoirs in specific environments, raising questions about its transmission dynamics and the need for integrated One Health approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform surveillance strategies by highlighting the importance of understanding local viral ecology and the necessity of developing regional diagnostic and research capacities to address mpox outbreaks effectively.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the lack of dedicated funding and the reliance on international collaboration, but the evidence is strong due to the comprehensive genomic analysis and local context.

新发传染病(猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯山脉):传播、环境生存和消毒的叙述性回顾

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection
作者： David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,导致与医院 传播 相关的 暴发。方法:这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。结果:猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒都可以在环境中生存,并且能够间接感染 传播。所有这些病毒都有包膜,对常用的防腐剂和表面消毒剂敏感。结论:快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新出现病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒是新出现的传染病,它们导致与院内 传播 相关的 暴发。
证据基础与方法
这篇叙述性综述总结了 猴痘、尼帕病毒和安第斯病毒 流行病学、临床表现、诊断 和 治疗,重点是环境生存、对防腐剂和消毒剂的敏感性以及感染预防。
共识与结论
快速识别已知或疑似感染这些新兴病毒的患者,并遵守感染预防建议,将预防与医疗保健相关的 传播。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
提供的证据没有明确说明具体的研究或实践意义。

Emerging infectious diseases (Mpox, Nipah, and Andes): A narrative review of transmission, environmental survival, and disinfection

Authors: David J. Weber, William A. Rutala, April Baller, Brooke E Brewer, Emily E. Sickbert-Bennett, E. Sickbert‐Bennett
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission. METHODS: This narrative review summarizes the Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus epidemiology, clinical manifestations, diagnosis, and treatment with a focus on environmental survival, susceptibility to antiseptics and disinfectants, and infection prevention. RESULTS: Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus all survive in the environment and are capable of indirect transmission. All these viruses are enveloped and susceptible to commonly used antiseptics and surface disinfectants. CONCLUSIONS: Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus are emerging infectious diseases that cause outbreaks associated with nosocomial transmission.
Evidence base and method
This narrative review summarizes the Mpox, Nipah virus, and Andes virus epidemiology, clinical manifestations, diagnosis, and treatment with a focus on environmental survival, susceptibility to antiseptics and disinfectants, and infection prevention.
Consensus and conclusions
Rapid identification of patients with known or suspected infections of these emerging viruses coupled with adherence to infection prevention recommendations will prevent healthcare-associated transmission.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific research or practice implication.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

面向精准疫苗学的猴痘:理性抗原设计、下一代平台与保护性免疫相关因子

Toward Precision Vaccinology for Mpox: Rational Antigen Design, Next-Generation Platforms, and Immune Correlates of Protection
作者： Yithenthrathevinair K. Paramasivam, Nur Syafiqah Mohamad Nasir, Mohd Zulkifli Salleh
摘要中文翻译猴痘已成为全球公共卫生关切,凸显了基于天花疫苗的传统疫苗接种策略的局限性,并迫切需要精准疫苗学方法。结构病毒学与免疫信息学的进展已使从成熟病毒体与细胞外病毒体两种形式中识别保守免疫优势抗原成为可能,从而支持开发可诱导广谱中和抗体(nAb)应答的多价抗原组合。新兴递送技术包括mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒、病毒媒介与自组装蛋白纳米颗粒,在快速可扩展性、增强免疫原性与改善安全性方面优于传统减毒活疫苗。解决抗原性进化、疫苗供应限制及人群特异性免疫变异性将是优化疫苗效果的关键。本综述整合了当前关于抗原设计、疫苗递送平台与免疫保护相关因子的证据,以勾勒下一代猴痘 疫苗的框架。因此,精准疫苗学代表了一种变革性策略,用于开发持久的、分支特异性猴痘 疫苗,并加强全球应对未来正痘病毒 暴发的能力。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 猴痘病毒(正痘病毒 猴痘)、MPXV 分支和 猴痘 疾病。它解决了对精确疫苗学方法的需求,以克服传统基于痘苗的策略的局限性,并开发 疫苗,该 疫苗 可以诱导针对不同病毒分支的广泛中和抗体反应。
证据基础与方法
该综述全面综合了有关抗原设计、疫苗 递送平台和保护的免疫相关性的当前证据。它包括结构病毒学和免疫信息学进展的一般背景信息,这些信息使得能够从成熟和细胞外病毒颗粒形式中鉴定保守的免疫显性抗原。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程,但它整合了各种研究的结果,概述了下一代 猴痘 疫苗 的框架。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,新兴的递送技术,如 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒、病毒 媒介 和自组装蛋白纳米颗粒,在可扩展性、免疫原性和安全性方面比传统的减毒活体 疫苗 具有显着优势。这些技术被认为是开发耐用、特定进化枝 猴痘 疫苗 的有前途的工具。
争议与缺口
该审查确定了几个证据差距,包括需要解决抗原 进化、疫苗 供应限制以及人群特异性免疫变异性,以优化 疫苗 的有效性。它还指出了了解保护的免疫相关性以指导 疫苗 开发的重要性。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调了精准疫苗学在开发下一代 猴痘 疫苗 中的重要性。它建议未来的研究应集中在抗原设计、疫苗 递送平台和保护的免疫相关性上,以提高 疫苗 的有效性和针对未来 正痘病毒 暴发 的准备。

Toward Precision Vaccinology for Mpox: Rational Antigen Design, Next-Generation Platforms, and Immune Correlates of Protection

Authors: Yithenthrathevinair K. Paramasivam, Nur Syafiqah Mohamad Nasir, Mohd Zulkifli Salleh
Original abstract (English)
Mpox has emerged as a global public health concern, highlighting the limitations of traditional vaccinia-based vaccination strategies and the urgent need for precision vaccinology approaches. Advances in structural virology and immunoinformatics have enabled the identification of conserved immunodominant antigens from both mature virion and extracellular virion forms, supporting the development of multivalent antigen combinations capable of inducing broad neutralizing antibody (nAb) responses. Emerging delivery technologies including mRNA-lipid nanoparticles, viral vectors, and self-assembling protein nanoparticles offer rapid scalability, enhanced immunogenicity, and improved safety compared with conventional live-attenuated vaccines. Addressing antigenic evolution, vaccine supply limitations, and population-specific immune variability will be crucial for optimizing vaccine effectiveness. This review synthesizes current evidence on antigen design, vaccine delivery platforms, and immunological correlates of protection to outline a framework for next-generation mpox vaccines. Precision vaccinology therefore represents a transformative strategy for developing durable, clade-specific mpox vaccines and strengthening preparedness against future orthopoxvirus outbreaks worldwide.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on Mpox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), MPXV clades, and mpox disease. It addresses the need for precision vaccinology approaches to overcome the limitations of traditional vaccinia-based strategies and to develop vaccines that can induce broad neutralizing antibody responses against different clades of the virus.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive synthesis of current evidence on antigen design, vaccine delivery platforms, and immunological correlates of protection. It includes general background information on advances in structural virology and immunoinformatics, which have enabled the identification of conserved immunodominant antigens from both mature and extracellular virion forms. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes, but it integrates findings from various studies to outline a framework for next-generation mpox vaccines.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that emerging delivery technologies such as mRNA-lipid nanoparticles, viral vectors, and self-assembling protein nanoparticles offer significant advantages over conventional live-attenuated vaccines in terms of scalability, immunogenicity, and safety. These technologies are highlighted as promising tools for the development of durable, clade-specific mpox vaccines.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies several evidence gaps, including the need to address antigenic evolution, vaccine supply limitations, and population-specific immune variability to optimize vaccine effectiveness. It also notes the importance of understanding immune correlates of protection to guide vaccine development.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the importance of precision vaccinology in developing next-generation mpox vaccines. It suggests that future research should focus on antigen design, vaccine delivery platforms, and immunological correlates of protection to improve vaccine effectiveness and preparedness against future orthopoxvirus outbreaks.

尼日利亚猴痘 血清流行率的系统性回顾:流行病学、人口统计学、风险因素与防控

SYSTEMATIC REVIEW OF MPOX SEROPREVALENCE IN NIGERIA: EPIDEMIOLOGY, DEMOGRAPHICS, RISK FACTORS, AND CONTROL
作者： Chinwe Grace Azuka, Kwarbai Aaron Maitala, Adamu Ishaku Akyala, John Etim Edet, Abah Michael Idoko
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是一种重新出现的人畜共患疾病,由 正痘病毒 属的双链 DNA 病毒引起。自2017年以来,尼日利亚出现了“猴痘”的死灰复燃,且“暴发”的规模不断加大。本研究分析了 监测 暴发 响应和管理系统 (SORMAS) 2017 年 9 月至 2025 年 2 月的 监测 数据,以描述 猴痘 的 流行病学 并评估对 监测 和响应的影响。共报告疑似病例5,979例,其中实验室确诊病例1,303例(21.8%)。男性占疑似病例和确诊病例的63%和65.9%,女性分别占37%和34.1%。受影响最大的年龄组是 20-29 岁(确诊病例的 47.7%),反映了天花后 疫苗接种 队列研究 的脆弱性。15岁以下儿童占确诊病例的16%。时间趋势显示 暴发 在 2017 年、2022 年和 2024 年达到峰值,表明 暴发 存在重复波和持续流行。五个州(索科托、阿夸伊博姆、拉各斯、奥贡和翁多)贡献了约 60% 的病例,表明地理聚集。总体病死率 (CFR) 为 7.8%,其中女性 (8.1%) 和儿童 (8.2%) 的 死亡率 略高。风险因素分析强调了职业暴露(商业/贸易商、学生、农民)和家庭聚集(家庭主妇、婴儿)。然而,只有 9.6% 的疑似病例和 5% 的确诊病例报告称与传染源有过接触,这凸显了病例调查的薄弱。监测 通过 SORMAS 改进了数据捕获和及时性,但受到报告不足、暴露数据不完整和诊断瓶颈的限制。调查结果强调需要增强 One Health 监测、分散诊断、社区驱动的报告和有针对性的风险沟通。本研究得出的结论是,尼日利亚的 猴痘 已从零星流行的 跨物种溢出 转变为持续流行的 传播。加强 监测 和应对系统对于降低发病率、死亡率 以及进一步国际传播的风险至关重要。

共识与结论
共报告疑似病例5,979例,其中实验室确诊病例1,303例(21.8%)。受影响最大的年龄组是 20-29 岁(确诊病例的 47.7%),反映了天花后 疫苗接种 队列研究 的脆弱性。总体病死率 (CFR) 为 7.8%,其中女性 (8.1%) 和儿童 (8.2%) 的 死亡率 略高。调查结果强调需要增强 One Health 监测、分散诊断、社区驱动的报告和有针对性的风险沟通。
