只有 9.6% 的疑似病例和 5% 的确诊病例报告称与传染源有过接触,这凸显了病例调查的薄弱。风险因素分析强调了职业暴露(商业/贸易商、学生、农民)和家庭聚集(家庭主妇、婴儿)。时间趋势显示 暴发 在 2017 年、2022 年和 2024 年达到峰值,表明 暴发 存在重复波和持续流行。

SYSTEMATIC REVIEW OF MPOX SEROPREVALENCE IN NIGERIA: EPIDEMIOLOGY, DEMOGRAPHICS, RISK FACTORS, AND CONTROL

Authors: Chinwe Grace Azuka, Kwarbai Aaron Maitala, Adamu Ishaku Akyala, John Etim Edet, Abah Michael Idoko
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is a re-emerging zoonotic disease caused by a double-stranded DNA virus of the genus Orthopoxvirus. Since 2017, Nigeria has witnessed a resurgence of Mpox, with outbreaks of increasing magnitude. This study analyzed surveillance data from the Surveillance Outbreak Response and Management System (SORMAS) spanning September 2017 to February 2025, to describe the epidemiology of Mpox and assess implications for surveillance and response. A total of 5,979 suspected cases were reported, of which 1,303 (21.8%) were laboratory-confirmed. Males accounted for 63% of suspected and 65.9% of confirmed cases, while females comprised 37% and 34.1% respectively. The largely affected age group was 20–29 years (47.7% of confirmed cases), reflecting vulnerability among post-smallpox vaccination cohorts. Children under 15 years represented 16% of confirmed cases. Temporal trends revealed outbreak peaks in 2017, 2022, and 2024, suggesting repeated waves and ongoing endemic transmission. Five states (Sokoto, Akwa Ibom, Lagos, Ogun, and Ondo) contributed ~60% of cases, indicating geographic clustering. The overall case fatality rate (CFR) was 7.8%, with slightly higher mortality among females (8.1%) and children (8.2%). Risk factor analysis highlighted both occupational exposure (business/traders, students, farmers) and household clustering (housewives, infants). However, only 9.6% of suspected and 5% of confirmed cases reported contact with an infectious source, underscoring weak case investigation. Surveillance through SORMAS improved data capture and timeliness but was limited by underreporting, incomplete exposure data, and diagnostic bottlenecks. Findings emphasize the need for enhanced One Health surveillance, decentralized diagnostics, community-driven reporting, and targeted risk communication. This study concludes that Mpox in Nigeria has transitioned from sporadic spillover to sustained endemic transmission. Strengthening surveillance and response systems is critical to reducing morbidity, mortality, and the risk of further international spread.
View review five-element analysis

Mpox is a re-emerging zoonotic disease caused by a double-stranded DNA virus of the genus Orthopoxvirus. Since 2017, Nigeria has witnessed a resurgence of Mpox, with outbreaks of increasing magnitude.

Surveillance through SORMAS improved data capture and timeliness but was limited by underreporting, incomplete exposure data, and diagnostic bottlenecks.
Consensus and conclusions
A total of 5,979 suspected cases were reported, of which 1,303 (21.8%) were laboratory-confirmed. The largely affected age group was 20–29 years (47.7% of confirmed cases), reflecting vulnerability among post-smallpox vaccination cohorts. The overall case fatality rate (CFR) was 7.8%, with slightly higher mortality among females (8.1%) and children (8.2%). Findings emphasize the need for enhanced One Health surveillance, decentralized diagnostics, community-driven reporting, and targeted risk communication.
Controversies and gaps
Only 9.6% of suspected and 5% of confirmed cases reported contact with an infectious source, underscoring weak case investigation. Risk factor analysis highlighted both occupational exposure (business/traders, students, farmers) and household clustering (housewives, infants). Temporal trends revealed outbreak peaks in 2017, 2022, and 2024, suggesting repeated waves and ongoing endemic transmission.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening surveillance and response systems is critical to reducing morbidity, mortality, and the risk of further international spread. Children under 15 years represented 16% of confirmed cases. Five states (Sokoto, Akwa Ibom, Lagos, Ogun, and Ondo) contributed ~60% of cases, indicating geographic clustering.

重新思考 猴痘:流行病学、临床变异、诊断 和反应

Rethinking mpox: epidemiology, clinical variants, diagnosis, and response
作者： Renu Thakur, Devendra Pratap Singh Rajput, Nikunj Rajkumar Agrawal, Abhimanyu Agrawal
摘要中文翻译猴痘病,现在通常被称为 猴痘,曾经被认为是一种地域限制性人畜共患疾病,但已成为全球公共卫生问题。2022 年跨国 暴发 标志着一个转折点,传播 动态、受影响人群和临床表现发生了显着变化。典型病例通常包括发烧、淋巴结肿大和离心性水疱脓疱性皮疹。然而,最近的 暴发 揭示了非典型表现,包括生殖器和肛周病变、轻微或不存在前驱症状以及异步皮疹 进化。这些变化使早期临床识别变得复杂,并可能导致延迟 诊断 和向前的 传播。本综述综合了 猴痘 不断演变的 流行病学 的当前证据,强调了地方性和非地方性 暴发 之间的差异,并描述了典型和非典型临床特征的全部范围。它还评估诊断方法,包括分子和即时护理技术,并评估当前和新兴 疫苗(例如 JYNNEOS 和 ACAM2000)的功效和局限性。未来的优先事项包括改进全球 监测、细化病例定义、扩大诊断访问范围以及确保公平的 疫苗 分配。了解 猴痘 不断扩大的临床和流行病学特征对于及时 诊断、遏制和为未来的 暴发 做好准备至关重要。

范围与问题
猴痘病毒(正痘病毒 猴痘)、MPXV 分支和 猴痘 疾病。该综述重点关注 猴痘 的演变、地方性和非地方性 暴发 之间的差异,以及典型和非典型临床特征的全谱。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了 猴痘 不断演变的 流行病学 的当前证据,强调了地方性和非地方性 暴发 之间的差异,并描述了典型和非典型临床特征的全部范围。它还评估诊断方法,包括分子和即时护理技术,并评估当前和新兴 疫苗(例如 JYNNEOS 和 ACAM2000)的功效和局限性。
共识与结论
典型病例通常包括发烧、淋巴结肿大和离心性水疱脓疱性皮疹。然而,最近的 暴发 揭示了非典型表现,包括生殖器和肛周病变、轻微或不存在前驱症状以及异步皮疹 进化。
争议与缺口
这些变化使早期临床识别变得复杂,并可能导致延迟 诊断 和向前的 传播。了解 猴痘 不断扩大的临床和流行病学特征对于及时 诊断、遏制和为未来的 暴发 做好准备至关重要。
科研与实践启示
未来的优先事项包括改进全球 监测、细化病例定义、扩大诊断访问范围以及确保公平的 疫苗 分配。

Rethinking mpox: epidemiology, clinical variants, diagnosis, and response

Authors: Renu Thakur, Devendra Pratap Singh Rajput, Nikunj Rajkumar Agrawal, Abhimanyu Agrawal
Original abstract (English)
Monkeypox disease, now commonly referred to as mpox, was once considered a geographically restricted zoonotic disease but has emerged as a global public health concern. The 2022 multinational outbreak marked a turning point, with notable shifts in transmission dynamics, affected populations, and clinical manifestations. Classical cases typically involve fever, lymphadenopathy, and a centrifugal vesiculopustular rash; however, recent outbreaks have revealed atypical presentations, including genital and perianal lesions, minimal or absent prodromal symptoms, and asynchronous rash evolution. These variations have complicated early clinical recognition and may contribute to delayed diagnosis and onward transmission. This review synthesizes current evidence on the evolving epidemiology of mpox, highlights differences between endemic and non-endemic outbreaks, and describes the full spectrum of typical and atypical clinical features. It also assesses diagnostic approaches, including molecular and point-of-care techniques, and evaluates the efficacy and limitations of current and emerging vaccines, such as JYNNEOS and ACAM2000. Future priorities include improving global surveillance, refining case definitions, expanding diagnostic access, and ensuring equitable vaccine distribution. Understanding the expanding clinical and epidemiological profile of mpox is essential for timely diagnosis, containment, and preparedness against future outbreaks.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Monkeypox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), MPXV clades and mpox disease. The review focuses on the evolving epidemiology of mpox, differences between endemic and non-endemic outbreaks, and the full spectrum of typical and atypical clinical features.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes current evidence on the evolving epidemiology of mpox, highlighting differences between endemic and non-endemic outbreaks, and describes the full spectrum of typical and atypical clinical features. It also assesses diagnostic approaches, including molecular and point-of-care techniques, and evaluates the efficacy and limitations of current and emerging vaccines, such as JYNNEOS and ACAM2000.
Consensus and conclusions
Classical cases typically involve fever, lymphadenopathy, and a centrifugal vesiculopustular rash; however, recent outbreaks have revealed atypical presentations, including genital and perianal lesions, minimal or absent prodromal symptoms, and asynchronous rash evolution.
Controversies and gaps
These variations have complicated early clinical recognition and may contribute to delayed diagnosis and onward transmission. Understanding the expanding clinical and epidemiological profile of mpox is essential for timely diagnosis, containment, and preparedness against future outbreaks.
Research and practice implications
Future priorities include improving global surveillance, refining case definitions, expanding diagnostic access, and ensuring equitable vaccine distribution.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

病毒 跨物种溢出 的驱动因素:病原体传播方式的检查

Drivers of Viral Spillover: An Examination of How Pathogens Spread
作者： Elenoire Sole, Giulia Montalbano, Giuseppe Motta, Maria Maddalena Pansera, Angelina Midiri, Mariarita Iacopino, Paolo Liotta, Giuseppe Mancuso, Carmelo Biondo
摘要中文翻译由于人们对禽流感、亨德拉病毒、猴痘、尼帕病毒和蝙蝠冠状病毒等病毒威胁的日益担忧,人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出(从动物传染给人类的病毒 传播)的出现引起了全世界的关注。显然,这些事件与自然发生的事件有很大的偏差,它们是生态、环境和社会因素的综合体,深刻地改变了动物和人类领域之间的界限。因此,为了了解人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出 的当代和未来驱动因素,协调一致、全面的全球“同一个健康”应对措施至关重要。本综述的目的是分析动物 储存宿主 和人类之间 传播 的普遍通路。这项检查涉及对历史病例的分析,包括 SARS 和埃博拉病毒的病例,以及最近发生的事件,例如 SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) 的全球大流行。本次综述的第二个目标是理解跨物种 传播 的基本机制。制定有效策略来减少人畜共患病毒的出现并预防未来的流行病取决于对这些机制的深入了解。该分析对于识别大流行病原体和防止人畜共患病毒传播至关重要。总之,该审查对当前防止人畜共患病毒传播的全球战略进行了全面评估,突显了我们理解上的差距和需要进一步研究的领域。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注病毒人畜共患 跨物种溢出 的驱动因素,特别研究 猴痘病毒 如何从动物 储存宿主 传播到人类。它阐述了跨物种 传播 的机制以及导致人畜共患病毒出现的因素,重点是 猴痘病毒 及其进化枝。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 跨物种溢出 病毒的历史和近期病例进行了系统分析,包括全球流行的 SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19)。它包括对影响动物和人类之间病毒 传播 的生态、环境和社会因素的讨论。
共识与结论
协调一致、全面的全球“同一个健康”应对措施对于了解和减轻人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出 的驱动因素至关重要。它强调需要对跨物种 传播 机制有深入的了解,以制定预防未来流行病的有效策略。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了我们对 猴痘病毒 和其他人畜共患病原体的跨物种 传播 的具体机制的理解上的差距。它还指出了病毒 跨物种溢出 所涉及因素的复杂性,这些因素可能因不同的生态和社会背景而异。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进一步研究以更好地了解 猴痘病毒 及其进化枝的 传播 动力学。它还建议加强全球 监测 和准备策略,以防止人畜共患病毒的出现和传播。

Drivers of Viral Spillover: An Examination of How Pathogens Spread

Authors: Elenoire Sole, Giulia Montalbano, Giuseppe Motta, Maria Maddalena Pansera, Angelina Midiri, Mariarita Iacopino, Paolo Liotta, Giuseppe Mancuso, Carmelo Biondo
Original abstract (English)
The occurrence of viral zoonotic spillover (the transmission of viruses from animals to humans) has attracted worldwide attention due to mounting concerns regarding viral threats such as avian influenza, Hendra, monkeypox, Nipah and bat coronaviruses. It is evident that these events deviate significantly from natural occurrences, being the conflation of ecological, environmental and social factors that are profoundly altering the boundaries between the animal and human domains. Consequently, in order to comprehend both the contemporary and prospective drivers of zoonotic viral spillover, a coordinated, comprehensive global One Health response is essential. The purpose of the present review is to analyze prevailing pathways of transmission between animal reservoirs and human populations. This examination involves the analysis of historical cases, including those of SARS and Ebola, as well as recent occurrences, such as the global pandemic of SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19). A secondary objective of this review is to comprehend the fundamental mechanisms of cross-species transmission. The development of effective strategies to mitigate the emergence of zoonotic viruses and prevent future pandemics is contingent on a robust understanding of these mechanisms. This analysis is key to identifying pandemic pathogens and preventing the spread of zoonotic viruses. In conclusion, the review provides a thorough evaluation of current global strategies to prevent the spread of zoonotic viruses, highlighting gaps in our understanding and areas for further research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the drivers of viral zoonotic spillover, specifically examining how the Mpox virus spreads from animal reservoirs to human populations. It addresses the mechanisms of cross-species transmission and the factors contributing to the emergence of zoonotic viruses, with an emphasis on the Mpox virus and its clades.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of historical and recent cases of viral spillover, including the global pandemic of SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19). It includes a discussion of the ecological, environmental, and social factors that influence the transmission of viruses between animals and humans.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that a coordinated, comprehensive global One Health response is essential to understand and mitigate the drivers of zoonotic viral spillover. It emphasizes the need for a robust understanding of cross-species transmission mechanisms to develop effective strategies for preventing future pandemics.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights gaps in our understanding of the specific mechanisms of cross-species transmission for the Mpox virus and other zoonotic pathogens. It also notes the complexity of the factors involved in viral spillover, which may vary across different ecological and social contexts.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further research is needed to better understand the transmission dynamics of the Mpox virus and its clades. It also recommends strengthening global surveillance and preparedness strategies to prevent the emergence and spread of zoonotic viruses.

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

美国和世界各地的猴痘:现状 - 疾病控制与预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Monkeypox in the United States and Around the World: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报The CDC's August 27, 2026, report outlines the current situation of monkeypox globally and in the United States. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox, which began in 2022, has led to over 100,000 cases in 122 countries, including 115 where it was previously unreported. In the U.S., more than 50 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported since November 2024, primarily linked to travel from Central and Eastern Africa or Western Europe, or through sexual contact. The risk to most people in the U.S. remains low, with clade Ib cases having a death rate of less than 0.5%. The CDC emphasizes vaccination as a key tool for prevention and has provided recommendations for those at risk. They are also enhancing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing, to detect monkeypox early and are collaborating with international partners to manage the outbreak. The report highlights the importance of clinical management and public health measures to control the spread of the virus.
查看新闻五要素
时间
现状报告于2026年8月27日发布。全球正在进行的IIb支系猴痘暴发于2022年开始,最近的暴发自2025年秋季以来已有报道。
地点与对象
该报告涵盖美国和全球各地,包括中非和东非、西欧以及亚洲、中东、北美、南美和澳大利亚等其他地区。受影响的人群包括从 暴发 地区旅行过或与这些地区有联系的个人,特别是那些涉及男性对男性性接触的人。
事件
猴痘 暴发 在全球范围内持续存在,自 2022 年以来,分支 IIb 是导致 暴发 的主要类型。分支 I 猴痘 暴发 在美国和其他地区已有报道,病例与旅行或性接触有关。CDC 报告称,这些 暴发 正在受到监控和管理。
规模、影响与风险
当前全球暴发的IIb分支猴痘暴发已在122个国家造成超过100,000例病例,其中包括115个此前未报告猴痘的国家。在美国,自2024年11月以来,已报告超过50例I分支猴痘的实验室确诊病例。I分支猴痘对美国大多数民众的风险仍然较低,其中Ib分支的死亡率低于0.5%,而Ia分支的死亡率约为3%。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC正在积极提高医护人员对猴痘的认识,为猴痘诊断和临床决策提供指导。同时,与公共卫生部门合作通过监测系统和废水检测提升检测能力。已为高风险人群制定疫苗接种建议,并与国际伙伴合作降低疫情扩散风险。

Monkeypox in the United States and Around the World: Current Situation - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC's August 27, 2026, report outlines the current situation of monkeypox globally and in the United States. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox, which began in 2022, has led to over 100,000 cases in 122 countries, including 115 where it was previously unreported. In the U.S., more than 50 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported since November 2024, primarily linked to travel from Central and Eastern Africa or Western Europe, or through sexual contact. The risk to most people in the U.S. remains low, with clade Ib cases having a death rate of less than 0.5%. The CDC emphasizes vaccination as a key tool for prevention and has provided recommendations for those at risk. They are also enhancing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing, to detect monkeypox early and are collaborating with international partners to manage the outbreak. The report highlights the importance of clinical management and public health measures to control the spread of the virus.

What to know There are two types of the virus that causes monkeypox, clade I and clade II. Both types can be spread, treated, and prevented in the same ways. Each clade has two subclades (clade Ia and clade Ib; clade IIa and IIb). Recent outbreaks of monkeypox have spread initially through intimate or sexual contact. In some cases, monkeypox was then spread within households. Recent travel-associated clade I monkeypox cases have almost exclusively been attributed to subclade Ib; there have been no deaths associated with these cases and most people had relatively mild illness. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox that began in 2022 has caused more than 100,000 cases in 122 total countries, including 115 countries where monkeypox was not previously reported. CDC has vaccination recommendations for people at risk in the United States or who are traveling to countries with clade I outbreaks or new clade II outbreaks. Current situation In the United States Clade I monkeypox The risk posed by the clade I monkeypox outbreak to most people within the United States remains low. Since November 2024, there have been more than 50 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox in the United States. More than half were diagnosed in people who: Had recently traveled from areas associated with the outbreak in Central and Eastern Africa; Had recently traveled from parts of Western Europe where newer outbreaks are being reported; OR Were linked to people who had traveled from these areas. We expect additional cases in Europe and the United States. Reminder Vaccination is an important tool in stopping the spread of monkeypox. If you have certain risk factors that make you eligible, you can help protect yourself from monkeypox by getting the 2-dose monkeypox vaccine. Monkeypox Vaccination Clade II monkeypox Clade II monkeypox is still circulating at low levels. Fall 2025 marked the highest monthly case counts since the peak of the outbreak in 2022. Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks Cases of clade I monkeypox have been diagnosed worldwide. Clade II Monkeypox Outbreaks Clade II monkeypox is spreading in the United States and around the world. Across the globe Clade I monkeypox Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox in Central and Eastern Africa continue. Beginning in Fall 2025, several countries in Western Europe began reporting clade Ib monkeypox cases among individuals who had no documented history of international travel. These cases were likely related to male-to-male sexual contact. Other travel-associated cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported from other parts of Africa, as well as Asia, Europe, the Middle East, North America, South America, and Australia. Recent monkeypox outbreaks have low death rates During recent outbreaks, death rates ranged from than less than 0.5% (clade Ib) to about 3% (clade Ia). Clade II is the type that caused the ongoing global outbreak that began in 2022. Fewer than 0.1% of people infected with clade II die from the disease. Clade II monkeypox For global case data for clade I and clade II monkeypox since January 1, 2024, see Global Mpox Trends. Confirmed cases include those that are laboratory confirmed as monkeypox virus and may include cases only confirmed as orthopoxvirus. These data are provided for situational awareness and are subject to change. What CDC is doing CDC raises awareness for U.S. healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC continues critical work to protect the United States from emerging and infectious diseases. CDC has collaborated closely with key partners in affected countries to help support efforts to stop monkeypox at the source. CDC works closely with U.S. state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of domestic monkeypox cases. in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC collaborated with governmental and civil society partners in affected countries to collect and analyze case data, and to identify how monkeypox is spreading. CDC coordinated technical assistance in response to urgent in-country needs, including: increasing the number of monkeypox testing sites; improving specimen transport networks to quickly identify new cases; assisting with Ministry of Health-led vaccine implementation; strengthening emergency management systems; improving case surveillance; and training healthcare workers. CDC's staff throughout Africa provide critical, real-time information to inform U.S. preparedness efforts. In the United States CDC works closely with state, tribal, local, and territorial public health departments to provide recommendations for clinical management, diagnosis, and prevention of monkeypox cases in the U.S. CDC continually increases capacity in communities across the United States for early detection of monkeypox through existing surveillance systems, including wastewater testing. CDC raises awareness for healthcare providers, including the latest guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. CDC has information and recommendations for members of the public, including travelers: Destinations | Travelers' Health, Travel Health Notices, and Health Alert Network advisories. CDC continues to work with international partners to help reduce monkeypox transmission. View five news elements
Time
The current situation report was published on August 27, 2026. The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox began in 2022, and recent outbreaks have been reported since Fall 2025.
Location and population
The report covers the United States and global locations, including Central and Eastern Africa, Western Europe, and other regions such as Asia, the Middle East, North America, South America, and Australia. The affected population includes individuals who have traveled from outbreak areas or been linked to them, particularly those involved in male-to-male sexual contact.
Event
Monkeypox outbreaks are ongoing globally, with clade IIb being the primary type causing the outbreak since 2022. Clade I monkeypox outbreaks have been reported in the United States and other regions, with cases linked to travel or sexual contact. The CDC reports that these outbreaks are being monitored and managed.
Scale, impact and risk
The ongoing global outbreak of clade IIb monkeypox has caused more than 100,000 cases in 122 countries, including 115 countries where monkeypox was not previously reported. In the United States, since November 2024, there have been more than 50 laboratory-confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox. The risk posed by clade I monkeypox to most people in the U.S. remains low, and the death rate for clade Ib is less than 0.5%, while for clade Ia it is about 3%.
Response, status and uncertainty
The CDC is actively raising awareness among healthcare providers, providing guidance for diagnosing monkeypox and clinical decision-making. They are also working with public health departments to increase detection capacity through surveillance systems and wastewater testing. Vaccination recommendations are in place for people at risk, and the CDC collaborates with international partners to reduce transmission.

I 型猴痘 暴发 - 疾病控制和预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)报告称,自2023年底起,I型猴痘病毒(clade I monkeypox)在中非和东非地区持续流行。自2024年1月以来,全球累计报告确诊病例超过55,000例,死亡病例超过150例。该病毒已扩散至西欧国家及美国,自2026年3月以来,美国病例数持续上升。截至2026年7月,美国报告I型猴痘确诊病例超过50例,主要与旅行史或与感染者接触相关。对美国大多数人群而言,感染风险仍较低,但男男性行为者的感染风险为中等。在高收入国家中,未报告Ib分支猴痘病例死亡病例,该分支是本次疫情的主要驱动因素。CDC正在刚果民主共和国(DRC)积极支持研究与应对工作,并与合作伙伴合作开展疾病监测、实验室能力建设及公共卫生措施。数据可能随时更新,仅供情势感知参考。
查看新闻五要素
时间
I 支系猴痘 暴发 自 2023 年底以来在中非和东非开始流行,自 2026 年 3 月以来在美国的发病频率有所增加。截至 2026 年 7 月,美国报告了 50 多例病例,截至 2026 年 4 月,刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 仍然是 Ib 支系病例最多的国家。疾病预防控制中心预计欧洲和美国将出现更多病例。
地点与对象
I型猴痘病毒(clade I monkeypox)疫情发生于中非和东非地区,特别是刚果民主共和国(DRC),并已扩散至西欧国家及美国。受影响人群包括来自疫情流行地区的旅行者、与感染者密切接触者,以及西欧地区的男男性行为者。
事件
I型猴痘病毒(clade I monkeypox)病例自2023年底起在中非和东非地区持续报告,西欧国家的新发病例可能与持续流行相关。CDC确认,美国I型猴痘病例数自2026年3月起上升,截至2026年7月,报告病例超过50例。这些病例主要与旅行史或与感染者接触相关。
规模、影响与风险
自2024年1月以来,全球报告了超过55,000例I型猴痘确诊病例,其中150多人死亡。截至 2026 年 7 月,美国报告了 50 多例病例,大多数人面临的风险仍然很低。然而,男男性行为者面临的风险为低至中等。高收入国家尚未报告 Ib 分支病例死亡,且病程相对较轻。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 正在积极支持刚果民主共和国的研究和应对工作,并一直与受影响地区的政府机构和合作伙伴就疾病 监测 进行合作,建设当地实验室能力,加强公共卫生和实验室劳动力能力、病例调查、病例管理、感染预防和控制、边境卫生以及风险沟通和社区参与。数据可能会发生变化,并用于态势感知。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Clade I Monkeypox Outbreaks - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC reports that clade I monkeypox outbreaks have been ongoing in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with more than 55,000 confirmed cases and over 150 deaths globally since January 2024. These outbreaks have spread to Western European countries and the United States, where cases have increased since March 2026. As of July 2026, over 50 clade I monkeypox cases were reported in the U.S., primarily linked to travel or contact with infected individuals. The risk to most people in the U.S. remains low, but it is moderate for men who have sex with men. No deaths have been reported in high-income countries for clade Ib cases, which have been the main driver of the outbreak. The CDC is actively supporting research and response efforts in the DRC and collaborating with partners on disease surveillance, laboratory capacity, and public health measures. The data are subject to change and provided for situational awareness.

What to know There have been outbreaks of clade I monkeypox in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023. Since January 2024, more than 55,000 confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported globally, including more than 150 deaths. Newer outbreaks in several Western European countries are likely related to this ongoing outbreak. Transmission dynamics are changing. Only a small proportion of exported clade I monkeypox cases worldwide this year have originated in Central or Eastern African countries. Recent travel-associated clade I monkeypox cases have almost exclusively been attributed to subclade Ib; there have been no deaths associated with these cases and available data have detailed relatively mild disease courses. Outbreak overview In the United States The risk posed by the clade I monkeypox outbreak to most people within the United States remains low. The risk to men who have sex with men is low to moderate. Clade I cases in the United States have increased in frequency since March 2026. As of July 2026, there have been more than 50 clade I monkeypox cases reported in the United States. More than half of all U.S. cases were diagnosed in people who: Had recently traveled to areas associated with the outbreak in Central and Eastern Africa; Had recently traveled to parts of Western Europe where newer outbreaks are being reported; OR Were linked to people who had traveled from these areas. CDC expects additional cases in Europe and the United States. Preventing Monkeypox While Traveling Learn where monkeypox is spreading and how to lower your risk of getting it during travel. In Central and Eastern Africa Clade Ia monkeypox outbreaks began impacting western areas of the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) in 2024. Available data indicate that clade Ia monkeypox has spread through sexual contact, regular household contact, and encounters with infected dead or live wild animals. Clade Ib monkeypox cases initially impacted eastern areas of DRC in late 2023. This subclade has driven much of the spread throughout Central and Eastern Africa and most travel-associated cases to other parts of the world. Available data indicate that these cases at first spread through heterosexual intimate or sexual contact between adults, then spread likely occurred within households, including to children. The disease spread in 2024 and 2025 to neighboring countries of DRC, only some of which are endemic for the virus that causes monkeypox, and sustained local spread occurred. As of April 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo is still the country with the majority of clade Ib cases. Outside Central and Eastern Africa Several countries in other parts of Africa, Asia, Europe, North America, and South America, as well as Australia, have confirmed clade I monkeypox cases mostly in travelers who'd recently been in areas with clade I outbreaks. To date, there have been more than 300 clade Ib cases in high-income countries, and no deaths have been reported. Beginning in Fall 2025, several countries in Western Europe began reporting clade Ib monkeypox cases among individuals who had no documented history of international travel. These cases were likely related to intimate or sexual exposure among men who have sex with men. We expect additional cases in Europe and the United States. For data since January 1, 2024, see 2022-24 Monkeypox (Monkeypox) Outbreak: Global Trends (shinyapps.io). Confirmed cases include those that are laboratory confirmed as monkeypox virus and may include cases only confirmed as orthopoxvirus. These data are provided for situational awareness and are subject to change. Clade I monkeypox and children Early in the Central and Eastern African outbreak, a high proportion of suspected cases in some areas were reported in children younger than 15 years of age. Historically, cases of monkeypox in children where monkeypox occurs regularly often happen after contact with wild animals that carry the disease. People then can spread the disease to close contacts, including in their households. The number of children with suspected monkeypox in this outbreak is probably due to spread within and between close households, the large number of younger people in affected countries, misdiagnosis of other diseases (such as measles), poor sanitary conditions, limited access to health services, and a high level of food insecurity and malnutrition. Based on what we know today, we don't expect to see the same sort of pattern in the United States for several reasons, including smaller household sizes, access to cleaning and disinfecting products, and improved access to medical care, equipment, and information about preventing spread while caring for patients. Notably, there are no consistent reports of schools as drivers of monkeypox transmission during any recent outbreaks. If cases of clade I monkeypox do happen in children the United States, CDC has clinical and public health guidance for reporting, testing, and effective case management. What CDC is doing CDC has been supporting DRC monkeypox research and response for more than 20 years. CDC and other U.S. government agencies have continued working in-country with partners in affected areas on disease surveillance, building local laboratory capacity, strengthening public health and laboratory workforce capacity, case investigation, case management, infection prevention and control, border health, and risk communication and community engagement. CDC staff brought laboratory supplies and trained local staff on laboratory equipment Resources Monkeypox in the United States and Around the World: Current Situation There are outbreaks of clade I and clade II monkeypox around the world. View five news elements
Time
The outbreak of clade I monkeypox began in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with increased frequency in the United States since March 2026. As of July 2026, more than 50 cases were reported in the U.S. and as of April 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) remains the country with the majority of clade Ib cases. The CDC expects additional cases in Europe and the United States.
Location and population
The outbreaks have occurred in Central and Eastern Africa, particularly in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), and have spread to Western European countries and the United States. The affected population includes travelers from outbreak areas, individuals with close contact with infected people, and men who have sex with men in Western Europe.
Event
Outbreaks of clade I monkeypox have been reported in Central and Eastern Africa since late 2023, with newer outbreaks in Western European countries likely related to the ongoing outbreak. The CDC has confirmed that clade I monkeypox cases in the United States have increased since March 2026, and as of July 2026, over 50 cases were reported. These cases are primarily linked to travel or contact with infected individuals.
Scale, impact and risk
Since January 2024, more than 55,000 confirmed cases of clade I monkeypox have been reported globally, with over 150 deaths. In the United States, more than 50 cases were reported as of July 2026, and the risk to most people remains low. However, the risk to men who have sex with men is low to moderate. No deaths have been reported in high-income countries for clade Ib cases, and the disease course is relatively mild.
Response, status and uncertainty
The CDC is actively supporting research and response efforts in the DRC and has been working with government agencies and partners in affected areas on disease surveillance, building local laboratory capacity, strengthening public health and laboratory workforce capacity, case investigation, case management, infection prevention and control, border health, and risk communication and community engagement. The data are subject to change and provided for situational awareness.

卫生局接获1例输入性猴痘病例报告– 澳门特别行政区政府入口网站 - 澳門特別行政區政府入口網站

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报澳门卫生局于2026年8月25日确认首例输入性猴痘病例,患者为一名年轻男性居民,他在旅行后出现发烧、头痛和皮疹。该患者未接种疫苗,于8月24日确诊,目前住院,情况稳定。流行病学调查表明可能通过高风险性接触在国外传播;没有报告当地 传播 或动物暴露。接触者将接受预防性猴痘,而澳门自2023年9月起继续对高危人群进行有针对性的暴露前预防。当局强调,猴痘对普通人群的风险仍然较低,并重申安全性行为和卫生措施。

时间
发表于2026-08-25;事件于 2026 年 8 月 18 日开始,症状出现,诊断 于 2026 年 8 月 24 日确认。
地点与对象
中国澳门特别行政区(SAR);居住的年轻成年男性。
事件
澳门卫生局报告一宗输入性猴痘确诊病例;患者住院并接受 治疗 治疗;根据旅行和接触史,病例被归类为输入病例。
规模、影响与风险
确诊病例1例;患者目前情况稳定;没有死亡报告;自2022年以来,全球非地方病病死率约为0.1%;家庭接触者中没有继发病例报告。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生局正在开展流行病学调查;患者的家庭接触者可通过猴痘 疫苗接种进行预防;自2023-09-14起,澳门高风险人群可在当地卫生中心接种疫苗进行暴露前预防;公众整体风险评估为低;已发布安全性行为指导及避免接触疑似病例或污染物品的建议。
A high priorityPublic-health news

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Chinese)
卫生局接获1例输入性猴痘病例报告– 澳门特别行政区政府入口网站 - 澳門特別行政區政府入口網站
News Brief
Macao’s Health Bureau confirmed its first imported mpox case on 25 August 2026 in a young male resident who developed fever, headache and rash after travel. The patient, who had not been vaccinated, was diagnosed on 24 August and is hospitalized in stable condition. Epidemiological investigation indicates likely acquisition abroad through high-risk sexual contact; no local transmission or animal exposure was reported. Contacts will receive preventive vaccination, while Macao continues targeted pre-exposure prophylaxis for high-risk groups since September 2023. Authorities emphasize that mpox risk for the general population remains low and reiterate safe-sex and hygiene measures.

Time
Published on 2026-08-25; event onset began on 2026-08-18 with symptom onset and diagnosis confirmed on 2026-08-24.
Location and population
Macao Special Administrative Region (SAR), China; resident young adult male.
Event
Macao Health Bureau reported one confirmed imported mpox (monkeypox) case; patient is hospitalized and receiving treatment; case classified as imported based on travel and exposure history.
Scale, impact and risk
1 confirmed case; patient currently stable; no reported deaths; global non-endemic case fatality rate approximately 0.1% since 2022; no secondary cases among household contacts reported.
Response, status and uncertainty
Health Bureau conducting epidemiological investigation; patient’s household contacts to receive mpox vaccination as prevention; high-risk individuals in Macao can receive pre-exposure prophylaxis via local health centers since 2023-09-14; general public risk assessed as low; guidance issued on safe sexual practices and avoidance of contact with suspected cases or contaminated items.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

猴痘 回来了 – 并且在新的国家/地区。这个暴发怎么能被收容呢? - 卫报

Mpox is back – and in new countries. How can this outbreak be contained? - The Guardian
新闻简报文章讨论了 猴痘 在新国家(包括几内亚比绍)的死灰复燃,这些国家以前没有报告过病例。猴痘(原名猴痘)已在全球 145 个国家报告,其中 2022 年 暴发 宣布为公共卫生紧急状态,随后于 2023 年 5 月解除。2024 年 暴发 也被列为公共卫生紧急状态,由于病例数下降,该状态于 2025 年 9 月解除。疾病预防控制中心警告说,猴痘 可能在美国流行,而世界卫生组织则指出其在密切接触环境中传播。2024 年 暴发 报告了 46 例疑似病例,其中 23 例是 15 岁以下儿童。Aula Abbara 博士强调了儿童患严重疾病的风险,尽管由于儿童较高的就医行为,数据可能存在偏差。该病毒在社区中传播范围更广,确诊病例之间没有关联,表明存在不明病例。文章强调需要继续采取 监测 和应对措施来遏制 暴发。
查看新闻五要素
时间
暴发曾于2022年和2024年报告过,其中2024年暴发于2024年8月14日宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件,并于2025年9月解除其状态。目前的情况涉及几内亚比绍的新病例,该国此前从未报告过猴痘病例。
地点与对象
全球 145 个国家已报告 猴痘,最近在几内亚比绍、布隆迪和刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 出现了病例。受影响的人群包括儿童,特别是四岁以下的儿童,以及生活条件接近的社区。
事件
几内亚比绍出现了新的 暴发 病例,这是该国首例病例。2022 年全球 暴发 被宣布为公共卫生紧急状态,随后于 2023 年 5 月解除。2024 年 暴发 也被宣布为公共卫生紧急状态,并于 2025 年 9 月解除。
规模、影响与风险
In 2022, there were almost 190,000 lab-confirmed cases globally. In the 2024 outbreak, 46 suspected cases were reported, with 23 being children under 15. The WHO and CDC have noted the potential for mpox to become endemic in the US and other regions, with concerns about its spread in close-contact settings.
应对、状态与不确定性
公共卫生应对措施包括接触者追踪和大规模 疫苗接种 计划。由于病例和死亡人数下降,世界卫生组织解除了紧急状态,但该病毒仍在社区中传播。基因检测和 Z​​XQTERMGQXZ 正在进行中,以监测新的变异或传播增加。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox is back – and in new countries. How can this outbreak be contained? - The Guardian

News Brief
The article discusses the resurgence of mpox in new countries, including Guinea Bissau, which had not previously reported cases. Mpox, formerly known as monkeypox, has been reported in 145 countries globally, with the 2022 outbreak declared a public health emergency and later lifted in May 2023. The 2024 outbreak also saw a public health emergency status, which was lifted in September 2025 due to declining case numbers. The CDC warned that mpox could become endemic in the US, while the WHO noted its spread in close-contact settings. In the 2024 outbreak, 46 suspected cases were reported, with 23 being children under 15. Dr. Aula Abbara highlighted the risk of severe disease in children, though data may be biased due to higher healthcare-seeking behavior for children. The virus is circulating more widely in communities, with confirmed cases not linked to each other, suggesting unidentified cases. The article emphasizes the need for continued surveillance and response measures to contain the outbreak.
Original source text (English)

Photograph: Tchandrou Nitanga/AFP/Getty Images View image in fullscreen A man infected with mpox at a hospital in Burundi. Photograph: Tchandrou Nitanga/AFP/Getty Images Mpox is back – and in new countries. How can this outbreak be contained? The public health emergencies in 2022 and 2024 saw the virus reported in 145 countries, but in the new cases children appear particularly affected A fresh outbreak of mpox in Guinea Bissau – the country’s first – means the virus formerly known as monkey pox is back in the headlines. So what has changed? Since 2022, there have been almost 190,000 lab-confirmed cases of mpox reported worldwide in 145 countries. Before then, outbreaks of mpox tended to be relatively small and in a handful of African countries, where the viral infection was known to be endemic. What is mpox? Mpox is an infectious disease that typically starts with flu-like symptoms, including fever, chills and aching muscles. These are followed by a distinctive rash, beginning as raised spots that turn into blisters filled with fluid before forming scabs. View image in fullscreen A colourised electron microscope image shows Mpox virus particles, represented in green, found within infected cultured cells, represented in blue. The virus particles are in various stages of maturity, which accounts for their differences in shape. Photograph: AP It used to be called monkeypox, because the virus was first discovered in captive monkeys in 1958. But during an international outbreak in 2022, there were concerns that the name was fuelling racist and stigmatising language on and offline, prompting the World Health Organization (WHO) to recommend changing it to mpox. It is endemic to many parts of Africa, with outbreaks historically starting when people ate meat from infected wild animals. But in recent years it has spread beyond the usual areas, with sustained chains of human-to-human transmission. The Atlanta-based Centers for Disease Control and Prevention said this month that mpox was on course to become endemic in the US. Why is it spreading to new countries? There are, broadly, two types of mpox, known as “clades”. Clade I was once called the “Congo Basin clade” and clade II the “West African clade”. The latter sparked a major global outbreak in 2022 when it appeared to undergo some changes that allowed it to spread more efficiently from person to person. It is passed on via close contact – in that outbreak, mostly by sexual contact, with men who have sex with men at the highest risk. View image in fullscreen A Red Cross worker disinfects a tent in the Don Bosco camps for internally displaced people in Goma, in the Democratic Republic of Congo. Photograph: Moise Kasereka/EPA In June 2024, researchers raised the alarm about a new variant of clade I in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), which also appeared to be spreading more easily between people. That grew into another major international outbreak, which was declared a public health emergency of international concern by the WHO on 14 August 2024. “Emergence of mpox in more countries in west Africa is to be expected,” said Prof Michael Marks, of the London School of Hygiene & Tropical Medicine and University College London hospital. “Mpox outbreaks have now been declared in a number of countries in the region, including Guinea and Sierra Leone within the last 12 months. “Mpox spreads by close physical contact. This makes it suitable to spread in a variety of different contexts, such as in close sexual networks, as was seen during the 2022 global pandemic, or areas where many people are living closely together such as settings with refugees and internally displaced people, as was seen in the DRC outbreak over the last few years,” Marks said. What happened to those two big outbreaks? The 2022 outbreak was declared a public health emergency in July that year. After contact-tracing and mass vaccination programmes, the status was lifted in May 2023. There had been about 90,000 infections. View image in fullscreen A passenger passes mpox alerts at Chennai airport in India. Photograph: R Satish Babu/AFP The 2024 outbreak had its international public health emergency status lifted in September 2025, not because the disease had disappeared but because numbers of cases and deaths were declining and officials felt the situation had become better controlled. If genetic testing reveales a new variant, or surveillance suggested an outbreak was spreading particularly fast, making people particularly sick or proving more deadly, global health authorities would be likely to step in again. How worrying is the new outbreak? Cases had not been reported in Guinea Bissau before this outbreak. Unicef said it was concerned that of 46 suspected cases, 23 were children aged under 15 and most of those were aged under four. “The concern about children is that they are at the highest risk of severe disease and death from mpox,” said Dr Aula Abbara, a senior lecturer at Imperial College London and adviser to Médecins Sans Frontières UK. “Young children can become much sicker than healthy adults.” View image in fullscreen Furaha Elisabeth applies medication to the skin of her child, Sagesse Hakizimana, at a health centre near Goma, in the DRC. Photograph: Arlette Bashizi/Reuters However, she cautioned that the apparent over-representation of children in the outbreak data should be interpreted carefully. She said: “There may also be some bias in the data. Families are often more likely to seek healthcare for a sick child than for an adult, particularly in a resource-constrained setting with a fragile health system, such as Guinea-Bissau.” Marks added: “Children have close physical contact with their parents and with each other, and in settings such as Guinea-Bissau are often living in cramped conditions, which make close physical contact and transmission more likely.” Confirmed cases do not appear linked to each other, which suggests the virus is circulating more widely in the community and there are unidentified cases. Are there vaccines and treatments? The WHO recommends two vaccines, which can be given to people at high risk or offered to people who have been in contact with a known case to prevent further spread. The question is where they are available. Marks said: “The major challenge remains that supplies of vaccines are best in high-income countries but worst in low-income settings, where the need and conditions for mpox transmission are highest.” A new global stockpile is due to be launched within weeks, which should mean vaccines can be deployed more rapidly to quell outbreaks. Officials also hope it will allow manufacturers to plan ahead and produce more of the jabs by providing a clear, concrete demand. View image in fullscreen A young mpox patient receives treatment at the Kavumu hospital in Karanrhada, in the Democratic Republic of Congo. Photograph: Michael Lunanga/EPA An antiviral called tecovirimat did not appear any better than a placebo when tested on patients with mpox clade I in the DRC, although the question of whether it might help some immunocompromised patients – such as those living with HIV – remains open. That trial did show, however, that giving patients good, supportive care dramatically improved their chances of survival. While vaccines remained an important tool for outbreak control, basic supportive care could be lifesaving, Abbara said. She added that strengthening surveillance, testing, contact tracing and broader public health measures would be critical to understanding and containing the outbreak. “Vaccines are important, but outbreaks are not controlled by vaccines alone,” she said. “Early detection, contact tracing, supportive care and strong public health surveillance remain absolutely essential.” Explore more on these topics Global health Mpox Guinea-Bissau Infectious diseases World Health Organization Health Microbiology news Share Reuse this content Most viewed Trump says NBC’s Kristen ‌Welker should face ‘punishment’ from FCC The kindness of strangers: My car had been towed and my kids had no shoes, then a man made a wonderful offer Man killed by falling sign at Christian festival named as church pays tribute Headteacher saves 900 children from deadly flash flood in Nepal Devastated by floods and overwhelmed by bodies, Nepalis weep for what remains of their homes Most viewed Most viewed
View five news elements
Time
The outbreak was reported in 2022 and 2024, with the 2024 outbreak declared a public health emergency of international concern on 14 August 2024 and its status lifted in September 2025. The current situation involves new cases in Guinea Bissau, which had not previously reported mpox cases.
Location and population
Mpox has been reported in 145 countries globally, with recent cases in Guinea Bissau, Burundi, and the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). The affected population includes children, particularly those under four, and communities with close living conditions.
Event
A fresh outbreak of mpox in Guinea Bissau, marking the country’s first case, has occurred. The 2022 global outbreak was declared a public health emergency and later lifted in May 2023. The 2024 outbreak was also declared a public health emergency and lifted in September 2025.
Scale, impact and risk
In 2022, there were almost 190,000 lab-confirmed cases globally. In the 2024 outbreak, 46 suspected cases were reported, with 23 being children under 15. The WHO and CDC have noted the potential for mpox to become endemic in the US and other regions, with concerns about its spread in close-contact settings.
Response, status and uncertainty
Public health responses included contact-tracing and mass vaccination programs. The WHO lifted the emergency status due to declining case and death numbers, but the virus remains circulating in communities. Genetic testing and surveillance are ongoing to monitor for new variants or increased spread.

台湾记录首例本土 猴痘 分支 Ib 株病例 - 焦点台湾

Taiwan records first domestic cases of Mpox clade Ib strain - Focus Taiwan
新闻简报2026年8月26日,台湾疾病预防控制中心(CDC)报告两例国内新病例,涉及Ib分支株猴痘,这是该国首例此类病例。两名患者均为30多岁男性,未接种疫苗,并与不明伴侣进行高危性行为。第一位患者在七月下旬出现发烧、腹部和腹股沟出现皮疹和脓疱后就医,第二位患者则在八月初出现躯干和腿部出现皮疹、随后出现发烧和肌肉酸痛后就医。医生根据症状怀疑 猴痘 后,疾病预防控制中心通过检测确认了这些病例。截至2026年8月23日,台湾已记录28例猴痘病例,其中本土病例19例,输入病例9例。自2026年5月出现首例输入性病例以来,已有9例确诊为Ib分支株。CDC 强调,Ib 分支更容易在家庭内传播,而免疫系统较弱的人,如儿童、孕妇和慢性病患者,出现严重症状的风险更高。两名患者的感染源尚不清楚,疾控中心正在开展接触者追踪。该机构还强调,疫苗接种是预防猴痘最有效的方法,包括感染Ib分支菌株,而且许多病例涉及高危性行为和缺乏疫苗接种。
查看新闻五要素
时间
台湾疾病预防控制中心于 2026 年 8 月 26 日报告两例 猴痘 clade Ib 株新病例。第一例患者于 7 月底就医,第二例患者于 8 月初就医。活动日期范围为2026年7月下旬至8月上旬。
地点与对象
台湾,特别是国内人口。受影响的人是30多岁的男性,他们没有接种疫苗,并与不明伴侣进行高风险性行为。
事件
台湾疾病预防控制中心报告了两例涉及 Ib 分支菌株的 猴痘 国内新病例,这是该变种的首例国内病例。医生根据症状怀疑猴痘后,通过医学检测确认了这些病例。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年8月23日,台湾已记录28例猴痘病例,其中本土病例19例,输入病例9例。自2026年5月出现首例输入性病例以来,已有9例确诊为Ib分支株。CDC 指出,Ib 分支更容易在家庭内传播,免疫系统较弱的人更有可能出现严重症状。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 确认了这些病例,并表示 疫苗接种 仍然是预防 猴痘 的最有效方法,包括感染分支 Ib 和分支 II 菌株。两名患者的感染源尚不清楚,疾控中心正在开展接触者追踪。该机构还强调,许多案件涉及高危性行为,且缺乏疫苗接种。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Taiwan records first domestic cases of Mpox clade Ib strain - Focus Taiwan

News Brief
On August 26, 2026, Taiwan's Centers for Disease Control (CDC) reported two new domestic cases of Mpox involving the clade Ib strain, marking the first such cases in the country. Both patients were men in their 30s who had not been vaccinated and engaged in high-risk sexual behavior with unspecified partners. The first patient sought medical attention in late July after developing a fever, rashes, and pustules on his abdomen and groin, while the second patient did so in early August after experiencing rashes on his trunk and legs, followed by a fever and muscle soreness. The CDC confirmed the cases through testing after doctors suspected Mpox based on symptoms. As of August 23, 2026, Taiwan had recorded 28 Mpox cases, including 19 domestic and nine imported cases. Since the first imported case in May 2026, nine cases have been identified as the clade Ib strain. The CDC highlighted that clade Ib is more easily transmitted within households and that individuals with weakened immune systems, such as children, pregnant women, and those with chronic illnesses, are at higher risk of severe symptoms. The sources of infection for the two patients remain unclear, and the CDC is conducting contact tracing. The agency also emphasized that vaccination is the most effective way to prevent Mpox, including infection with the clade Ib strain, and that many cases involved high-risk sexual behavior and lack of vaccination.

A microscopic image of Mpox virus for illustrative purposes only. Image by NIAID, CC BY 2.0, via Wikimedia Commons Taipei, Aug. 26 (CNA) Taiwan's Centers for Disease Control (CDC) on Wednesday reported two new cases of Mpox involving the clade Ib strain, marking the first domestic cases of the variant, which is believed to be more transmissible and more easily spread through close physical or intimate contact. Both patients were men in their 30s who had not been vaccinated and had engaged in high-risk sexual behavior with unspecified partners, according to contact tracing. However, the sources of their infections remain unclear, CDC spokesperson Lin Ming-cheng (林明誠) told reporters. The first patient sought medical attention in late July after developing a fever, as well as rashes and pustules on his abdomen and groin. He tested positive for Mpox after his doctor suspected the disease, Lin said. The second patient sought treatment in early August after developing rashes on his trunk and legs, followed by a fever and muscle soreness. He also tested positive after his doctor suspected Mpox and conducted testing, Lin said. As of Aug. 23, Taiwan had recorded 28 Mpox cases, including 19 domestic and nine imported cases, according to the CDC. The figure was lower than the 48 cases reported in 2024 and 31 in 2025 in the same period. A total of nine cases, including the two newly reported domestic cases, have been identified as the clade Ib strain since Taiwan recorded its first imported case in May 2026. The CDC said global experience shows that people infected with either clade Ib or clade II can develop symptoms including fever, swollen lymph nodes, rashes and blisters. Clade Ib is more easily transmitted within households, while people with weakened immune systems, including children, pregnant women and those with chronic illnesses, are more likely to develop severe symptoms, the agency said. The CDC said many people who have contracted Mpox had engaged in high-risk sexual behavior, while nearly 90 percent had not been vaccinated. Vaccination remains the most effective way to prevent Mpox, including infection with the clade Ib and clade II strains, the agency said. Those eligible for publicly funded vaccines include people who have engaged in high-risk sexual behavior in the past year, people who have been diagnosed with a sexually transmitted disease, and people who have had sexual contact with anyone in either of those categories. 台湾确认两例本地 猴痘 分支 Ib 病例 - 台北时报

Taiwan confirms two local mpox clade Ib cases - Taipei Times
新闻简报2026年8月27日,台湾疾病控制中心(CDC)确认两例本地猴痘 clade Ib病例,这是该菌株的首次本地感染。两名患者均为 30 多岁男性,未接种疫苗,病情稳定。CDC 指出,猴痘 疫苗 对 Ib 分支有效,截至上个月,已有 79,216 人完成了推荐的两剂系列,而 31,755 人仅接受了一剂。Ib 分支菌株表现出与 II 分支相似的症状,包括发烧、皮疹和水泡,但可能更容易在家庭内传播。一般人群面临的风险仍然很低,CDC 敦促有较高风险性行为的个人寻求 疫苗接种。报告还强调,大多数确诊的 猴痘 病例涉及 20 至 50 岁的男性,有较高风险的性活动史。
查看新闻五要素
时间
The news report was published on 2026-08-26. The event date is August 27, 2026, when the CDC confirmed two local mpox clade Ib cases. The time range for the report is August 1, 2026 - August 30, 2026.
地点与对象
这些病例是在台湾,特别是台北确诊的。受影响人群包括两名 30 多岁的男性,两人均未接种疫苗,病情稳定。
事件
台湾确诊两例本地 猴痘 分支 Ib 病例,这是该菌株首次本地感染。CDC 报告了这些病例,并表示当前的 疫苗 对 Ib 分支有效。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年8月27日,台湾今年新增猴痘病例28例,其中本地感染病例19例,输入病例9例。Ib 分支菌株具有与 II 分支相似的症状,有可能出现更广泛的皮疹,并且更容易在家庭内传播。幼儿、孕妇和患有慢性疾病的人如果被感染,患严重疾病的风险更高。
应对、状态与不确定性
疾病预防控制中心通过基因分型确认了这些病例。他们强调 疫苗接种 是最有效的预防方法,并指出 79,216 人已完成两剂接种,31,755 人已接种一剂。普通人群面临的风险仍然很低,疾病预防控制中心提供了有关症状、预防和检测的指导。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Taiwan confirms two local mpox clade Ib cases - Taipei Times

News Brief
On August 27, 2026, the Centers for Disease Control (CDC) in Taiwan confirmed two local cases of mpox clade Ib, marking the first locally acquired infections of this strain. Both patients, men in their 30s, were unvaccinated and in stable condition. The CDC noted that mpox vaccines are effective against clade Ib, and as of last month, 79,216 people had completed the recommended two-dose series, while 31,755 had received only one dose. The clade Ib strain exhibits symptoms similar to clade II, including fever, rashes, and blisters, but may spread more easily within households. The risk to the general population remains low, and the CDC urged individuals with higher-risk sexual activity to seek vaccination. The report also highlighted that most confirmed mpox cases involved men aged 20 to 50, with a history of higher-risk sexual activity.
Original source text (English)
Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages Search Search All Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages August 1, 2026 - August 30, 2026 GO Home Front Page Thu, Aug 27, 2026 page1 Taiwan confirms two local mpox clade Ib cases PROTECTION: Nearly 90 percent of confirmed mpox cases were unvaccinated, the CDC said, adding that current vaccines are effective against clade Ib By Hou Chia-yu and Sam Garcia / Staff reporter, with staff writer Add TT as Preferred Source Two domestic cases of mpox clade Ib have been confirmed this month, marking the first locally acquired infections of the virus strain, the Centers for Disease Control (CDC) said yesterday. The patients, both men in their 30s, are in stable condition and had not received an mpox vaccine before developing symptoms, the CDC said. The first man developed a fever late last month, followed by a rash and pustules on the abdomen and groin, CDC Deputy Director-General Lin Ming-cheng (林明誠) said. Centers for Disease Control physician Lin Yung-ching explains mpox symptoms and preventive measures in a news conference at the centers in Taipei on April 21. Photo: Lin Chih-yi, Taipei Times He was diagnosed with mpox after seeking medical care and undergoing testing, Lin said. The other man developed a rash on the torso and legs early this month, followed by fever and muscle aches, and after rashes appeared on multiple parts of his body, he was tested, Lin said. Genetic typing confirmed that they were infected with mpox clade Ib, he added. Mpox is a viral infectious disease primarily spread through close contact, including sexual contact, hugging, kissing or direct skin-to-skin contact, the CDC said. The clade Ib strain has symptoms similar to the more common clade II, including fever, swollen lymph nodes, rashes and blisters, but its rash might be more widespread and the virus might spread more easily within households, Lin said. Young children, pregnant women and people with chronic illnesses are at higher risk of severe disease if infected, he said. Mpox vaccines are effective against clade Ib, while the antiviral drug tecovirimat could be used to treat severe cases, he added. As of Sunday, 28 new mpox cases had been recorded in Taiwan this year, including 19 locally acquired cases and nine imported cases, all involving men aged 20 to 50, the CDC said. Since the first imported mpox clade Ib case was reported in May, nine clade Ib infections have been confirmed, including seven imported and two locally acquired cases, it said. Since mpox was designated a Category 2 communicable disease in 2022, Taiwan has recorded 544 cases, including 500 locally acquired cases and 44 imported cases, CDC data showed. Most people with confirmed mpox had a history of higher-risk sexual activity, and nearly 90 percent were unvaccinated against mpox, the centers said. People who engage in higher-risk sexual activity, particularly those without a steady sexual partner, face a higher risk of infection, it said. The risk to the general population remains low, it added. Vaccination remains the most effective way to prevent infection, Lin said, adding that as of last month, 79,216 people had completed the recommended two doses of the vaccine, while 31,755 people had received only one dose. As two doses provide about 90 percent protection against mpox, compared with 40 to 80 percent protection after one dose, those who are considered at risk should complete the two-dose series as soon as possible, he said. In Taiwan, 318 medical institutions offer government-funded mpox vaccines, the CDC said. People who have engaged in higher-risk sexual activity within the past year, have previously had a sexually transmitted infection or have sexual partners with related risk factors are eligible for the government-funded vaccine, it said. Mpox has an incubation period of up to 21 days, and some people might be infectious one to four days before symptoms appear, the centers said. People who develop rashes, blisters or pustules along with symptoms such as fever, headaches, muscle aches or swollen lymph nodes should wear a mask and seek medical attention, and inform their doctor of any travel, exposure and contact history, it said. 猴痘 的飞机接触者追踪已结束,新的 CDC 数据显示旅行风险的实际情况 - 医学日报

Airplane Contact Tracing for Mpox Has Ended, and New CDC Data Show Where Travel Risk Actually Sits - Medical Daily
新闻简报CDC 根据基因组和流行病学数据得出结论,航空旅行期间感染 猴痘 传播 的风险较低。这一决定是在 2025 年停止对所有 猴痘 分支和分支进行飞机接触者追踪之后做出的。该机构于 2026 年 8 月 24 日发布的最新分析报告称,2024 年至 2025 年期间有 5,322 例确诊病例和疑似病例,其中有 21 次病毒输入该国。这些发现与之前的结论一致,即在航空旅行期间不太可能出现分支 I 猴痘,因为大多数 猴痘 病例是通过与病变、结痂或受污染物品密切、持续的身体接触传播的,而不是通过飞机上的短暂互动传播的。CDC 强调,疫苗接种 状态、目的地以及目的地接触性质等因素在评估旅行风险时比飞行行为本身更为重要。

美国,病例分布在全国各地。

2024 年至 2025 年报告了 5,322 例确诊病例和疑似病例,其中 21 起病毒输入该国。对于分支 I,航空旅行期间 传播 的风险被认为较低,这与分支 II 的早期发现一致。

CDC 于 2025 年停止了对所有 猴痘 分支和分支的飞机接触者追踪。调查发现接触者中没有继发病例,并且认为航空旅行期间 传播 的风险较低。研究结果基于基因组和流行病学数据。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Airplane Contact Tracing for Mpox Has Ended, and New CDC Data Show Where Travel Risk Actually Sits - Medical Daily

News Brief
The CDC has concluded that the risk of mpox transmission during air travel is low, based on genomic and epidemiologic data. This decision followed the discontinuation of aircraft contact tracing for all mpox clades and subclades in 2025. The agency's latest analysis, published on August 24, 2026, reported 5,322 confirmed and probable cases across 2024 and 2025, with 21 separate importations of the virus into the country. The findings align with earlier conclusions that clade I transmission during air travel is unlikely, as most mpox cases spread through close, sustained physical contact with lesions, scabs, or contaminated items, and not through brief interactions on planes. The CDC emphasized that factors such as vaccination status, destination, and the nature of contact at the destination are more critical in assessing travel risk than the act of flying itself.

Healthy Living Airplane Contact Tracing for Mpox Has Ended, and New CDC Data Show Where Travel Risk Actually Sits Published Aug 24, 2026 10:30 PM EDT By Cole Mercer YASUYOSHI CHIBA | AFP via Getty Images Share on Twitter Share on Facebook Share on Pocket The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention published its most complete national picture of mpox in more than a year last Thursday, and the numbers point travelers toward a different set of precautions than many have assumed. The new analysis in the agency's Morbidity and Mortality Weekly Report counted 5,322 confirmed and probable cases across 2024 and 2025. Detections of travel-associated cases increased between the two years, and genomic and epidemiologic data showed evidence of 21 separate importations of the virus into the country. Further importations were observed in 2026 and are expected to continue. What has changed for anyone boarding a plane is the agency's own posture. The CDC discontinued aircraft contact investigations for all mpox clades and subclades in 2025, a decision explained in a separate April report on aircraft contact tracing that examined what happened when infected passengers flew. That earlier investigation followed 60 aircraft contacts of three men who traveled on five commercial flights while infectious with clade I virus, the strain that has driven international concern since 2024. One contact developed fatigue, body aches, and a skin lesion afterward and tested negative. No secondary cases were identified. The authors concluded that the risk of clade I transmission during commercial air travel "appears to be low," consistent with earlier findings for clade II. The Cabin Was Never Where the Risk Concentrated The reason is mechanical rather than reassuring rhetoric. Mpox spreads mainly through close, sustained physical contact with lesions, scabs, or contaminated bedding and clothing, and most often through sexual contact. The kind of contact that produces secondary cases rarely occurs between strangers seated near each other for a few hours. An earlier and much larger investigation reached the same conclusion for the 2022 outbreak strain, finding no secondary cases among 1,538 people who had contact with 113 infected travelers across 221 flights. That finding led the agency to stop routine clade II aircraft investigations back in February 2023, and the newer clade I analysis extended the same reasoning to the remaining clades. The practical translation for a family planning international travel is that seat assignment is not the variable worth worrying about. Vaccination status, destination, and the nature of contact at that destination are. Importations Are Rising Even as Total Cases Hold Steady The topline national figures are close to flat. There were 2,803 cases in 2024 and 2,519 in 2025. The overwhelming majority were clade II, the strain that has circulated domestically since 2022, with about 97.5 percent of sequenced samples descending from the 2022 outbreak lineage. Underneath that stability, two shifts stand out. Reported cases roughly doubled from 423 during September and October 2024 to 873 during the same two months in 2025, before falling to 448 during November and December and returning to background levels by the end of the year. And clade Ib detections rose from a single travel-related case in 2024 to 10 in 2025. Of those 11 cases, three patients were hospitalized and recovered without antiviral treatment, and no deaths were reported. Federal counts have continued climbing since. The CDC's clade I situation summary reports more than 40 laboratory-confirmed clade I cases in the United States since November 2024, and assesses the risk posed by the clade I outbreak to most people in the country as low. No deaths have been reported among roughly 150 clade Ib cases identified in high-income countries. Local transmission has occurred but has not taken hold. A domestic cluster of three cases in October 2025 was traced back to a travel-associated introduction and did not spread further. Sustained clade Ib transmission continues in several European countries, where about 89 percent of cases have occurred among men who have sex with men. City-level surveillance shows the ongoing background. New York City has recorded 102 positive mpox tests since the start of 2026, including 14 in the month ending August 8, according to figures posted by NYC Health and current as of August 13. Vaccination Status Separated Mild Illness from a Hospital Bed The most consequential finding for individual readers concerns outcomes rather than counts. Among 860 patients with complete records, the odds of hospitalization were 9.73 times as high for unvaccinated patients as for those who completed the two-dose JYNNEOS series, after controlling for age group and HIV status. Patients living with HIV had 2.37 times the odds of hospitalization compared with those without. Unvaccinated people accounted for 76.1 percent of cases where vaccination status was recorded. Overall, 9.5 percent of patients were hospitalized, compared with 2 percent among those known to be vaccinated. Deaths remained rare, with five in 2024 and one in 2025. The CDC's conclusion is that persistent low-level transmission of the 2022 strain suggests "a likely transition toward endemic circulation," and the agency recommends that people at risk of exposure complete both doses. That recommendation now explicitly includes travelers heading to countries with ongoing clade I or clade II transmission. Gaps in the Data That Deserve Attention Both reports carry limitations that the agency states directly, and readers should weigh them. The air travel analysis was small. Sixty contacts across five flights is a small base, and health departments managed to interview only 29 of them. The absence of detected transmission in a small sample is weaker evidence than its absence in a large one, though it points in the same direction as the much larger clade II investigation. The hospitalization finding is also less precise than the headline number suggests. The 9.73 figure has a 95 percent confidence interval ranging from 2.93 to 60.65, which is wide enough that the true effect could be much smaller or much larger. The direction is clear; the magnitude is not. The surveillance report relies on passive reporting, so mild infections that do not prompt a medical visit are not captured, and total infections are likely undercounted. Missing data were substantial, with vaccination status absent for 62 percent of cases and HIV status for 68 percent. Analyzing only complete records can introduce selection bias. For households, the reasonable steps are unchanged and modest. Anyone at elevated risk who has not completed the two-dose series can ask a primary care provider, sexual health clinic, or local health department about JYNNEOS, and travelers to affected regions should raise it before departure. People with mpox are advised to delay travel until their illness has resolved. Anyone who develops an unexplained rash after travel or close contact should contact a clinician promptly, mention the exposure, and avoid close physical contact until evaluated. Rapidly worsening symptoms, difficulty breathing, or severe pain warrant urgent care. The agency's recommendations on treatment and vaccination remain the same regardless of clade. The agency updates its monthly U.S. case data in the first week of every month. Read more NIH Plans to Bar Scientists on Visas from the Award That Launches Independent Labs A Smuggling Prosecution Over Inactivated Mpox Samples Has Scientists Warning About Outbreak Preparedness Africa CDC Says Contact Tracing Is Not Working in Congo Ebola Outbreak as Deaths Pass 1,700 Key Questions Answered Can someone catch mpox from a passenger on their flight? Investigations to date have not identified any such case. Among 60 contacts of three infected clade I travelers on five flights, no secondary cases were found, and a larger clade II investigation of 1,538 contacts also found none. Why did the CDC stop tracing airline contacts? Because repeated investigations found no evidence of onboard transmission. The agency discontinued aircraft contact investigations for all mpox clades and subclades in 2025. Does that mean mpox is not a travel concern? No. Travel-associated introductions are rising, and the agency documented 21 importations across 2024 and 2025. The risk is tied to close contact at the destination rather than to the flight itself. Who faces the greatest risk of severe illness? People who are unvaccinated and people living with HIV. Unvaccinated patients had 9.73 times the odds of hospitalization compared with fully vaccinated patients, though the confidence interval around that estimate is wide. Should travelers get vaccinated before an international trip? The CDC recommends the two-dose JYNNEOS series for people at risk of exposure, including travelers to countries with ongoing clade I or clade II transmission. A clinician or local health department can advise on individual eligibility. What symptoms should prompt a call to a clinician? An unexplained rash or lesions, particularly following travel or close physical contact, along with fever, swollen lymph nodes, body aches, or exhaustion. Mention any recent travel when seeking care. How current are these numbers? The national surveillance figures cover cases through December 31, 2025, and are provisional. The count of more than 40 clade I cases reflects the agency's situation summary page as currently posted. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

SF:卫生官员更新 猴痘 疫苗 推送

SF: Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push
新闻简报旧金山卫生官员敦促人们在即将于 9 月在市场南部社区举行的福尔瑟姆街集市之前接种 猴痘 疫苗。截至周一,2026 年已报告超过 70 例 猴痘 病例,其中 8 例为 I 型变异,这是一种更严重的变异。公共卫生部发布公告,强调疫苗接种可降低感染和严重疾病的风险,但并不能消除感染病毒的可能性。疫苗 可通过医疗保健提供者和当地药房购买,没有保险的人可以访问 sf.gov/猴痘 获取资源。官员们指出,大多数美国病例都是男男性行为者和跨性别者,他们建议完成两剂系列,第二剂在第一剂后至少 28 天注射。目前不建议那些已经接受过两种剂量的人接受加强剂量。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于 2026 年 8 月 26 日。活动日期没有明确说明,但该建议是在定于 9 月 26 日举行的福尔瑟姆街博览会之前发布的。
地点与对象
该活动以旧金山为中心,特别是在市场南部附近。受影响的人群包括有 猴痘 风险的个人,特别是那些计划前往有 I 型分支报告的地区的人,以及 LGBTQ+ 社区的人,包括男同性恋、双性恋和与男性发生性关系的变性男性。
事件
旧金山卫生官员在福尔瑟姆街博览会之前再次推动 猴痘 疫苗接种,理由是 猴痘 病例增加以及活动期间 传播 病例增加的可能性。该建议由旧金山公共卫生部发布。(N3、N17、N18、N19)。
规模、影响与风险
截至周一,2026年在旧金山已报告逾70例猴痘,其中包括8例I分支猴痘。I分支可能较II分支导致更严重的疾病。该部门建议使用疫苗接种以减少感染风险并预防重症、住院及死亡。(N7、N10、N11、N12、N14、N24)
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员鼓励有 猴痘 风险的人接受两剂 疫苗。第二剂可以在第一剂之后至少 28 天注射,等待时间超过 28 天的人不需要重新开始该系列。目前不建议已完成两剂注射的患者接受加强剂量。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

SF: Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push

News Brief
San Francisco health officials are urging people to get vaccinated against mpox ahead of the upcoming Folsom Street Fair, which is scheduled for September in the South of Market neighborhood. As of Monday, over 70 mpox cases have been reported in 2026, with eight cases of clade I, a more severe variant. The Department of Public Health issued an advisory, emphasizing that vaccination reduces the risk of infection and severe illness, though it does not eliminate the possibility of contracting the virus. The vaccine is available through healthcare providers and local pharmacies, and those without insurance can access resources at sf.gov/mpox. Officials note that most U.S. cases have been among men who have sex with men and transgender individuals, and they recommend completing the two-dose series, with the second dose given at least 28 days after the first. Booster doses are not currently advised for those who have already received both doses.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content BREAKING Rare August rainfall makes way for plummeting temps in Bay Area forecast News|Bay Area & State SF: Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push By Bay City News ServiceAug 26, 2026 A colorized transmission electron micrograph shows two mature mpox (monkeypox) virus particles (pink) attached to the surface of an infected VERO E6 cell (blue/teal). The image was captured at the National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases (NIAID) Integrated Research Facility (IRF) in Fort Detrick, Maryland. (National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases/National Institutes of Health via Bay City News) National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases/National Institutes of Health/National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases/National Institutes of Health via Bay City News With the Folsom Street Fair about a month away and more than 70 mpox cases reported in San Francisco this year, city health officials this week urged people to get vaccinated and watch for symptoms. The Folsom Street Fair is scheduled for Sept. 27 from 11 a.m. to 6 p.m. in San Francisco's South of Market neighborhood, according to organizers. The San Francisco Department of Public Health issued the advisory ahead of the fair, saying there is still time for people who haven't been vaccinated to begin the two-dose series. Advertisement Article continues below this ad As of Monday, more than 70 cases of mpox had been reported in San Francisco in 2026, including eight cases of clade I mpox, according to the Department of Public Health. The department confirmed the city's first clade I case, one of the two types of mpox, on April 14. The case involved an unvaccinated adult who was hospitalized and improving when health officials announced it two days later. The person reported close contact with someone who had traveled internationally. The department says all confirmed clade I cases in San Francisco so far have been associated with international travel. Clade I may cause more severe illness than clade II, which caused a large U.S. outbreak in 2022. Both spread primarily through close skin-to-skin contact, including during sexual activity, and can cause similar symptoms. Both clade strands can cause similar symptoms such as fever, swollen lymph nodes or fatigue, followed by a rash that can resemble pimples or blisters and may be painful. The department recommends that people who develop a rash that looks like mpox contact a health care provider for testing and notify partners who may have been exposed. Advertisement Article continues below this ad Anyone can contract mpox, but the department said most reported U.S. cases have been among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men, as well as transgender people who have sex with men. "While we are in a much better place than we were during the 2022 outbreak, mpox infections can and still do occur," San Francisco Health Officer Dr. Susan Philip said in the department's advisory. Health officials are encouraging people at risk for mpox, including those planning to travel to parts of Africa and Europe where clade I has been reported, to receive both doses of the vaccine. The second dose can be given at least 28 days after the first, and the department says people who wait longer than 28 days don't need to restart the series. Booster doses aren't currently recommended for people who have completed both doses, and vaccination is not recommended at this time for people who previously had mpox. Advertisement Article continues below this ad Dr. Julia Janssen, deputy director of the department's sexually transmitted infection and HIV branch, said vaccination reduces the chance of infection and helps protect against severe illness, hospitalization and death. The department also says the vaccine can be given to people who are immunocompromised, including people living with HIV or taking medications that suppress the immune system. BECOME A FRIEND. Fully vaccinated people should still watch for symptoms and seek testing if they develop them. The vaccine is available through health care providers and local pharmacies. The Department of Public Health directs people without insurance or those having difficulty finding a vaccine to its mpox information and vaccination resources at sf.gov/mpox. 随着疫情蔓延,几内亚比绍的 猴痘 疑似病例中有一半是儿童 - 《卫报》

Children make up half of suspected mpox cases in Guinea-Bissau as epidemic spreads - The Guardian
新闻简报在几内亚比绍,随着疫情蔓延,儿童占 猴痘 疑似病例的一半。在该国出现首例确诊病例后,于 2026 年 7 月 4 日宣布了国家 暴发。截至2026年8月,已有疑似病例46例,确诊病例7例,无死亡报告。地方当局和卫生工作者担心,由于该国脆弱的卫生系统,病毒可能会在未被发现的情况下传播。暴发 已促使采取紧急公共卫生措施,包括卫生部和国际机构支持的挨家挨户宣传活动。跨境 传播 的风险仍然很高,因为分支变异与邻国的变异一致。目前正在努力加强 监测 和一线能力,以解决正在进行的 传播 问题并减轻对儿童的影响,因为儿童受到的影响尤其严重。
查看新闻五要素
时间
暴发 于 2026 年 7 月 4 日被宣布,首例确诊病例报告时间较其早约一周。本报告发布日期为 2026-08-25。自宣布之日起,该事件持续至今,新发病例在随后数周内间歇性出现。
地点与对象
几内亚比绍,特别是比绍自治区和附近的比翁博地区。受影响人群包括儿童,其中一半疑似病例为儿童,其中四岁以下有16人。
事件
几内亚比绍地方当局报告称,儿童占 猴痘 疑似病例的一半,并于 2026 年 7 月宣布为 猴痘。该事件被确认为 猴痘 暴发,报告了 7 例确诊病例和 46 例疑似病例。
规模、影响与风险
截至报告,疑似病例46例,确诊病例7例,无死亡病例。由于 监测 系统的健康状况脆弱,人们担心该病毒会在未被发现的情况下传播。跨境 传播 的风险仍然很高,因为分支变异与邻国传播的变异一致。
应对、状态与不确定性
在 暴发 宣言发布后,社区卫生工作者在卫生部和国际机构的支持下,通过扩音器和翻译传单发起了挨家挨户的公众宣传活动。由于基础设施挑战和总统选举暂停的后果,该国的卫生系统面临压力。优先考虑增加监测和加强一线能力。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Children make up half of suspected mpox cases in Guinea-Bissau as epidemic spreads - The Guardian

News Brief
In Guinea-Bissau, children account for half of the suspected mpox cases as an epidemic spreads. The national outbreak was declared on 4 July 2026, following the first confirmed case in the country. As of August 2026, there have been 46 suspected cases and 7 confirmed cases, with no deaths reported. Local authorities and health workers are concerned that the virus may be spreading undetected due to the country’s fragile health surveillance systems. The outbreak has prompted urgent public health measures, including a door-to-door awareness campaign supported by the health ministry and international agencies. The risk of cross-border transmission remains high, as the clade variants align with those in neighboring countries. Efforts are underway to strengthen surveillance and frontline capacity to address the ongoing transmission and mitigate the impact on children, who are disproportionately affected.
Original source text (English)
