There is a sense of urgency in the heath sector, with schools due to reopen in September. Photograph: Vincent Tremeau/Unicef Children make up half of suspected mpox cases in Guinea-Bissau as epidemic spreads West African country reports 46 suspected cases in outbreak declared in July, amid fears virus may be spreading undetected More than half of the mpox cases reported in Guinea-Bissau have been among children, local authorities have said, almost two months since the country announced an epidemic. A national outbreak was declared on 4 July, just over a week after a 27-year-old woman became the first confirmed case of mpox in the west African country. Since then, there have been seven confirmed cases and 46 suspected cases. Half of those patients have been children, with 16 under the age of four. In a statement on Thursday, Unicef warned that because the country’s health surveillance systems were fragile, the virus is probably spreading undetected, leaving even more children at risk. Sandra Martins, the country’s deputy representative for Unicef, said: “This is the first time mpox cases have been reported in Guinea-Bissau, making even a small outbreak a major public health event.” Mpox is an infectious viral disease that can be transmitted through close contact. There have been more than 2,000 mpox-related deaths among tens of thousands of cases recorded across east, central and west Africa since January 2024. Health workers say the Clade variants are consistent with viruses circulating in neighbouring countries, meaning that a risk of cross‐border transmission in the region remains strong. The vast majority of confirmed cases are in the Bissau autonomous sector, where the capital is, while one case is in the nearby Biombo area. No deaths have been reported to date. Dr Magda Robalo, a former health minister who led the country’s Covid-19 response, said that to eliminate the “biggest gaps in the health system right now”, increased surveillance would have to be prioritised and frontline capacity reinforced. “Even though the numbers are small, new cases have appeared intermittently over several weeks, which means transmission is ongoing,” said Robalo, who is also co-founder of the Institute for Global Health and Development and former director of communicable diseases for the World Health Organization. The outbreak comes as the country grapples with infrastructure challenges and the aftermath of the suspension of last November’s presidential election by the military, which remains in power. There was no electricity throughout Thursday in Bissau for instance because of petrol scarcity since the weekend. First malaria drug for babies is approved in ‘major public health milestone’ Read more Many public health laboratories in the region lack equipment to deal with epidemics but have institutional knowledge from handling other crises. In Guinea-Bissau, that is no different but there is also a sense of urgency, with primary schools scheduled to reopen across the country in September. After the outbreak was declared, community health workers immediately began a door-to-door public awareness campaign with megaphones and translated leaflets, supported by the health ministry and international agencies. These grassroots initiatives are seen as vital for building public trust in the public health sector. Earlier this year, the country suspended a controversial US-funded trial on hepatitis B vaccinations for newborns, citing ethical concerns and procedural flaws. The decision was lauded by many across the continent and has highlighted sensitivities around emergency health interventions. Location and population
Guinea-Bissau, specifically the Bissau autonomous sector and the nearby Biombo area. The affected population includes children, with half of the suspected cases being among them, and 16 under the age of four.
Event

Scale, impact and risk

The risk of cross-border transmission remains strong, as the clade variants are consistent with those circulating in neighboring countries.
Response, status and uncertainty

The country’s health system is under pressure due to infrastructure challenges and the aftermath of the suspended presidential election. Increased surveillance and frontline capacity reinforcement are being prioritized.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

教育、隔离、疫苗接种:肯尼亚沿海地区应对 猴痘 - Gavi、疫苗 联盟

Education, isolation, vaccination: coastal Kenya tackles mpox - Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance
新闻简报在肯尼亚沿海地区,人们正在努力通过教育、隔离和 疫苗接种 来对抗 猴痘病毒。Gavi(疫苗 联盟)正在支持这些措施,强调免疫护士在前线工作中的作用。报告指出,猴痘 继续在该地区传播,但没有提供具体病例数、死亡人数或暴露人数。重点是加强卫生系统和免疫规划,但没有提及明确的风险级别。回应包括公共卫生策略,但调查或测试的详细信息未包含在证据中。该报告是全球疫苗和免疫联盟在全球卫生和免疫领域更广泛工作的一部分,强调了伙伴关系和创新融资机制对于应对卫生挑战的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
跳至主要内容 国家中心 资助指南 次要导航 全球疫苗免疫联盟的新战略时期 道德热线 English Français 主要导航 关于我们 关于我们 全球疫苗免疫联盟 (Gavi),疫苗 联盟帮助世界上一半以上的儿童接种疫苗,预防世界上一些最致命的疾病。
地点与对象
肯尼亚,沿海地区。
事件
肯尼亚沿海地区正在实施教育、隔离和 疫苗接种 等措施来应对 猴痘。报告提到了在 猴痘病毒 前线工作的免疫护士。
规模、影响与风险
猴痘病毒 继续在肯尼亚传播,但提供的证据中没有报告具体的病例数、死亡数或暴露数量。风险没有明确说明。
应对、状态与不确定性
Gavi、疫苗 联盟参与支持免疫工作,包括 猴痘。回应包括教育、隔离和 疫苗接种,但没有提供有关调查或测试的具体细节。

Education, isolation, vaccination: coastal Kenya tackles mpox - Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance

News Brief
In coastal Kenya, efforts are being made to combat the mpox virus through education, isolation, and vaccination. Gavi, the Vaccine Alliance, is supporting these measures, highlighting the role of immunised nurses in frontline efforts. The report notes that mpox continues to circulate in the region, but no specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers are provided. The focus is on strengthening health systems and immunisation programs, with no explicit risk level mentioned. The response includes public health strategies, but details on investigation or testing are not included in the evidence. This report is part of Gavi's broader work in global health and immunisation, emphasizing the importance of partnerships and innovative finance mechanisms to address health challenges.

猴痘 传播到柏林的更多人群

Mpox spreads to further groups of people in Berlin
新闻简报据国家卫生和社会事务办公室 (Lageso) 称,猴痘 正在柏林蔓延,尤其是在参加性积极活动的异性恋和酷儿人群中。据报道,2026 年 8 月,两名女性和一名男性通过俱乐部或家中的匿名性行为感染了该病毒。这标志着与前几年的转变,前几年每年只有一名女性或没有女性被感染。今年柏林猴痘病例总数为156例,高于去年同期的144例。罗伯特·科赫研究所 (RKI) 报告称,德国各地有 375 例病例,其中 98% 是男性,主要是男男性行为者和经常更换伴侣的人。RKI 的 Klaus Jansen 指出,疫苗 有效且耐受性良好,接种疫苗的人通常会出现较轻微的症状。流行病学家克劳迪娅·拉舍尔(Claudia Ruscher)强调,病毒在女性中的传播令人担忧,因为她们大多未接种疫苗,也不了解这种疾病。她警告说,通过接触皮肤病变而感染的风险最高,而避孕套可能无法提供足够的保护。疫苗接种常务委员会建议针对高危人群,包括男男性行为者和性工作者,进行针对性的疫苗接种,以防止进一步传播。
查看新闻五要素
时间
猴痘病毒 在柏林越来越多地通过男女之间的性行为传播(N1)。据国家卫生和社会事务办公室 (Lageso) 称,最近报告了几起与异性恋和酷儿人群的性积极事件有关的病例 (N2)。8 月份到目前为止,已向 Lageso (N3) 报告了两名患有 猴痘 的女性。同一时期,一名男子也被感染,声称他可能在与女性发生性行为时感染了该病毒(N4)。据认为,这三人都是通过在俱乐部或家里的匿名性接触而被感染的(N5)。
地点与对象
猴痘病毒 正在柏林蔓延,特别是在参加性积极活动的异性恋和酷儿群体中(N2)。感染发生在短时间内(N14)。今年迄今为止,柏林报告的 猴痘 病例总数为 156 例(N8)。近年来,要么根本没有女性被感染,要么每年只有一名女性被感染(N15)。仅在 2022 年重大 暴发 期间,女性病例总数为 4 例(N16)。
事件
猴痘 正在通过俱乐部或家里的匿名性接触传播到柏林的更多人群,包括女性(N5)。
规模、影响与风险
然而,暴发 的病例总数比现在(N17)增加了大约十倍。未接种疫苗且首次感染 猴痘 的人更有可能经历更严重的病程 (N18)。
应对、状态与不确定性
疫苗接种 常设委员会建议 猴痘 疫苗 适用于男性、跨性别者和与男性发生性行为并经常更换伴侣的非二元性别者、性工作者以及与感染者接触的人 (N32)。RKI (N33) 的 Jansen 表示,疫苗 非常有效且耐受性良好。尽管接种了疫苗但仍被感染的人通常会经历明显较轻的病程(N34)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox spreads to further groups of people in Berlin

News Brief
Mpox is spreading in Berlin, particularly among heterosexual and queer individuals attending sex-positive events, according to the State Office for Health and Social Affairs (Lageso). In August 2026, two women and one man were reported to have contracted the virus through anonymous sexual encounters in clubs or at home. This marks a shift from previous years, where only one woman or none were infected annually. The total number of mpox cases in Berlin this year is 156, up from 144 in the same period last year. The Robert Koch Institute (RKI) reported 375 cases across Germany, with 98% being men, primarily those who have sex with men and frequently change partners. The RKI's Klaus Jansen noted that the vaccine is effective and well-tolerated, and those vaccinated typically experience milder symptoms. Epidemiologist Claudia Ruscher emphasized that the spread to women, who are largely unvaccinated and unaware of the disease, is a cause for concern. She warned that the risk of infection is highest through contact with skin lesions, and condoms may not provide sufficient protection. The Standing Committee on Vaccination recommends targeted vaccination for high-risk groups, including men who have sex with men and sex workers, to prevent further spread.
Original source text (English)
The mpox virus is increasingly being transmitted in Berlin through sex between men and women. According to the State Office for Health and Social Affairs (Lageso), several cases have recently been reported in connection with sex-positive events for heterosexual and queer people, as stated in Lageso's latest weekly report. So far in August, two women with mpox have been reported to Lageso. According to the authority, a man also became infected during the same period, stating that he may have contracted the virus during sex with a woman. All three are thought to have become infected through anonymous sexual encounters in clubs or at home. A total of four cases of mpox in women have been reported to Lageso so far this year. In fact, it is primarily men who have sex with men and frequently change sexual partners who become infected. The total number of mpox cases reported in Berlin so far this year stands at 156. This is more than in the same period last year (144) and significantly more than in the whole of 2024 (38) or 2023 (13). According to the Robert Koch Institute (RKI), the German public health agency, 375 cases of mpox have been reported across Germany so far this year (as of August 27). A total of 98% of these were men, epidemiologist Klaus Jansen from the RKI told the dpa. The men for whom the route of infection is known contracted the virus almost exclusively through sex with men. "Across Germany, we are not currently seeing a trend similar to the situation described by Lageso for Berlin." Infectious diseases epidemiologist Claudia Ruscher from Lageso said: "It is a cause of great concern to us that mpox is spreading to another group of people who previously appeared unaffected and who are therefore largely unvaccinated and poorly informed." Four women might initially seem like a small number, but there is a new dynamic at play here. The infections occurred within a short space of time. In recent years, either no women at all or just one woman per year had been infected. Only during the major outbreak in 2022 were there a total of four cases among women. However, the total number of cases in that outbreak was around ten times higher than it is at present. People who are unvaccinated and contracting mpox for the first time are more likely to experience a more severe course of the disease. "They are more likely to have a feverish illness and often develop skin lesions all over their body," said Ruscher. "These are not just blisters, but itchy pustules, some of which are also pus-filled. They are really very painful." Mpox is a viral disease and is transmitted through close physical contact, particularly during sex. The risk of infection is greatest when coming into contact with the typical skin lesions (blisters or scabs) of people infected with Mpox, which can appear all over the body. Condoms therefore do not offer sufficient protection, Ruscher said. Transmission via respiratory droplets - that is, through saliva - is also possible. A vaccine is available. In 2022, the disease spread particularly rapidly in Germany. It mainly affected men who have sex with men and who frequently change sexual partners. A targeted vaccination campaign was subsequently launched for this group, and uptake was high. Other groups of people, however, often do not even know that mpox exists, said Ruscher. If they become infected and the pustules appear, they do not initially associate them with mpox. "Instead, they might think they have razor rash, mosquito bites or cold sores, and may not go to the doctor straight away." One patient had visited four different doctors before receiving the correct diagnosis, he said. Looking ahead, he said, "we must expect further infections among women, but it is difficult to give an exact figure." The Standing Committee on Vaccination recommends the mpox vaccine for, among others, men, transgender and non-binary people who have sex with men and frequently change partners, sex workers, and people who come into contact with infected individuals. The vaccine is very effective and well tolerated, according to Jansen from the RKI. Those who become infected despite being vaccinated usually experience a significantly milder course of the disease. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

CDC报告首两例本地猴痘新毒株病例 - 台北时报

CDC reports first two local cases of new mpox strain - Taipei Times
新闻简报台湾疾病控制中心 (CDC) 于 2026 年 8 月 26 日报告了首两例本地感染的 猴痘 分支 Ib 株病例。这两名患者均为 30 多岁男性,分别于上月底和本月初出现症状,并通过医学检测和基因测序确诊。这些病例标志着台湾首次出现 Ib 分支本地感染,与更常见的 II 分支相比,该 Ib 分支更容易在家庭内传播,并且可能出现更广泛的皮疹。CDC强调,猴痘 疫苗对Ib分支仍然有效,特考韦瑞可用于重症病例。截至2026年8月26日,台湾今年已记录28例猴痘病例,其中19例本地感染,9例输入,全部涉及20至50岁的男性。该机构敦促有症状的个人就医并报告任何旅行或接触史。应对措施包括通过 318 个医疗机构开展 疫苗接种 活动,以及针对幼儿、孕妇和免疫系统较弱人群等高危人群的公共卫生指导。
查看新闻五要素
时间
美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 于 2026 年 8 月 26 日报告了首两例 猴痘 新分支 Ib 株的本地病例。第一例患者于上月底出现症状,第二例患者于本月初出现皮疹。
地点与对象
这些病例是在台湾,特别是台北报告的。两名患者均为 30 多岁的男性,受影响人群包括有无保护性行为史的个体,尤其是 20 至 50 岁的男性。
事件
CDC 确认了两例 猴痘 clade Ib 本地病例,标志着该菌株在台湾首次出现。患者出现症状,并通过医疗护理和检测进行诊断,并通过基因测序确认了菌株。
规模、影响与风险
截至2026年8月26日,台湾今年新增28例猴痘病例,其中本土感染病例19例,输入病例9例。Ib 分支菌株可能更容易在家庭内传播,并且与 II 分支具有相似的症状。高危人群包括幼儿、孕妇和免疫系统较弱的人。猴痘 疫苗 对 Ib 分支仍然有效,特考韦瑞 可以治疗重症病例。
应对、状态与不确定性
疾病预防控制中心正在进行调查,包括基因测序,以确认该菌株。他们正在宣传 疫苗接种 并敦促有症状的个人寻求医疗救助并告知医生接触史。应对措施包括公共卫生措施以及通过全国318家医疗机构分发疫苗。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

CDC reports first two local cases of new mpox strain - Taipei Times

News Brief
The Centers for Disease Control (CDC) in Taiwan reported the first two locally acquired cases of the mpox clade Ib strain on August 26, 2026. Both patients, men in their 30s, developed symptoms late last month and early this month, respectively, and were diagnosed through medical testing and genetic sequencing. These cases mark the first local infections of clade Ib in Taiwan, which is known to spread more easily within households and may have more widespread rashes compared to the more common clade II. The CDC emphasized that mpox vaccines remain effective against clade Ib, and tecovirimat can be used for severe cases. As of August 26, 2026, Taiwan had recorded 28 mpox cases this year, including 19 locally acquired and nine imported, all involving men aged 20 to 50. The agency is urging individuals with symptoms to seek medical attention and report any travel or exposure history. The response includes vaccination campaigns through 318 medical institutions and public health guidance for high-risk groups, such as young children, pregnant women, and those with weakened immune systems.
Original source text (English)
Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages Search Search All Front Page Taiwan News Business Editorial & Opinion Sports World News Features Bilingual Pages August 1, 2026 - August 30, 2026 GO Home Taiwan News Wed, Aug 26, 2026 page2 CDC reports first two local cases of new mpox strain By Hou Chia-yu and Sam Garcia / Staff reporter, with staff writer Add TT as Preferred Source Taiwan had two local cases of mpox clade Ib this month, marking the first locally acquired infections of this strain of the virus, the Centers for Disease Control (CDC) said today. The patients, both men in their 30s, are in stable condition and had not received an mpox vaccine before developing symptoms, the CDC said. The first patient developed a fever late last month, followed by a rash and pustules, and was diagnosed with mpox after seeking medical care and undergoing testing, CDC Deputy Director-General Lin Ming-cheng (林明誠) said. A sign displayed at a Centers for Disease Control news conference in Taipei shows information about mpox in an undated photograph. Photo: CNA The second patient developed a rash early this month, followed by fever and muscle aches, with genetic sequencing confirming that both were infected with mpox clade Ib, Lin said. Mpox is a viral infectious disease primarily spread through sexual contact, hugging, kissing or direct skin-to-skin contact, the CDC said. The clade Ib strain has similar symptoms to the more common clade II, including fever, swollen lymph nodes, rashes and blisters, but its rash may be more widespread and it may spread more easily within households, Lin said. Young children, pregnant women and people with chronic illnesses who have weakened immune systems face a higher risk of severe disease, he said. Mpox vaccines remain effective against clade Ib, while the antiviral drug tecovirimat can also be used to treat severe cases, he added. As of Sunday, Taiwan had recorded 28 new mpox cases this year, including 19 locally acquired and nine imported cases, all involving men aged 20 to 50, the CDC said. Since the first imported mpox clade Ib case was reported in May, Taiwan has recorded nine clade Ib infections, including seven imported and now two locally acquired cases, it said. Since Taiwan classified mpox as a Category 2 communicable disease in 2022, the nation has recorded 544 cases, including 500 locally acquired and 44 imported cases, CDC data showed. Most local cases had a history of unprotected sexual activity, with nearly 90 percent unvaccinated against mpox, the CDC said. People engaging in unprotected or risky sexual activity with multiple partners face a higher risk of infection, while the risk to the general population remains low, it said. The mpox vaccine remains the most effective way to prevent infection, Lin said, adding that as of last month, 79,216 people had completed the recommended two doses, while 31,755 people had received only one dose. Two doses provide about 90 percent protection against mpox, compared with 40 to 80 percent after one dose, so those considered at-risk should complete the two-dose series as soon as possible, he said. There are currently 318 medical institutions nationwide offering government-funded mpox vaccines. People who have engaged in high-risk sexual activity within the past year, have had a sexually transmitted infection or have a sexual partner with related risk factors are eligible for the government-funded vaccine. Mpox has an incubation period of up to 21 days, and some people may be infectious one to four days before symptoms appear, the CDC said. People who develop rashes, blisters or pustules along with fever, headaches, muscle aches or swollen lymph nodes should wear a mask and seek medical attention, and inform their doctor of any travel, exposure and contact history, it said. SF Health 官方更新 猴痘 疫苗 推送 - 补丁

SF Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push - Patch
新闻简报旧金山卫生官员正在继续努力,在即将于 9 月举行的福尔瑟姆街博览会之前推广 猴痘 疫苗接种。截至周一,2026 年已报告了 70 多例 猴痘 病例,其中包括 8 例 I 型变异,这是一种更严重的变异。第一个分支 I 病例于 4 月 14 日确诊,涉及一名住院治疗的未接种疫苗的成年人。官员们强调,进化枝 I 与国际旅行有关,疫苗接种 可以降低感染和严重疾病的风险。疫苗 可通过医疗保健提供者和药房获得,没有保险的人可以访问 sf.gov/猴痘 获取资源。虽然建议使用 疫苗接种,但卫生官员警告说,这并不能消除感染的可能性,完全接种疫苗的个人仍应监测症状并在需要时寻求检测。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该通报是在定于 9 月举行的福尔瑟姆街集市之前发布的。第一例 I 型分支病例于 4 月 14 日 (N8) 得到确认。截至周一,2026 年(N6)已报告了 70 多例 猴痘 病例。
地点与对象
该活动在旧金山举行,特别是在市场南部社区 (N4)。受影响人群包括计划参加福尔瑟姆街集市的个人,以及有 猴痘 风险的人,特别是同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者,以及男男性行为者(N18)。
事件
旧金山卫生官员在福尔瑟姆街博览会之前再次推动 猴痘 疫苗接种,理由是已报告的病例以及活动期间传播的可能性(N1、N2、N6)。该部门确认了该市首例 I 类病例,与国际旅行有关(N8、N10、N11)。
规模、影响与风险
截至周一,旧金山已报告 2026 年超过 70 例 猴痘 病例,其中包括 8 例 I 型(N6)病例。进化枝 I 可能比进化枝 II (N12) 引起更严重的疾病。该部门建议有风险的人使用 疫苗接种,包括那些计划前往已报告 I 型分支的地区的人 (N21)。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员鼓励 疫苗接种 并敦促人们在出现症状时寻求检测(N16、N27)。疫苗 可通过医疗保健提供者和当地药房 (N28) 购买。该部门还为那些没有保险或难以找到 疫苗 (N29) 的人提供资源。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

SF Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push - Patch

News Brief
San Francisco health officials are renewing their efforts to promote mpox vaccination ahead of the upcoming Folsom Street Fair, which is scheduled for September. As of Monday, over 70 mpox cases have been reported in 2026, including eight cases of clade I, a more severe variant. The first clade I case was confirmed on April 14 and involved an unvaccinated adult who was hospitalized. Officials emphasize that clade I is linked to international travel and that vaccination reduces the risk of infection and severe illness. The vaccine is available through healthcare providers and pharmacies, and those without insurance can access resources at sf.gov/mpox. While vaccination is recommended, health officials caution that it does not eliminate the possibility of infection, and fully vaccinated individuals should still monitor for symptoms and seek testing if needed.
Original source text (English)
Crime & Safety SF Health Officials Renew Mpox Vaccine Push The San Francisco Department of Public Health issued the advisory ahead of the Folsom Street Fair. Bay City News , News Partner Posted Wed, Aug 26, 2026 at 2:21 pm PT Read More SAN FRANCISCO, CA With the Folsom Street Fair about a month away and more than 70 mpox cases reported in San Francisco this year, city health officials this week urged people to get vaccinated and watch for symptoms. The Folsom Street Fair is scheduled for Sept. 27 from 11 a.m. to 6 p.m. in San Francisco's South of Market neighborhood, according to organizers. The San Francisco Department of Public Health issued the advisory ahead of the fair, saying there is still time for people who haven't been vaccinated to begin the two-dose series. As of Monday, more than 70 cases of mpox had been reported in San Francisco in 2026, including eight cases of clade I mpox, according to the Department of Public Health. Find out what's happening in San Francisco for free with the latest updates from Patch. The department confirmed the city's first clade I case, one of the two types of mpox, on April 14. The case involved an unvaccinated adult who was hospitalized and improving when health officials announced it two days later. The person reported close contact with someone who had traveled internationally. The department says all confirmed clade I cases in San Francisco so far have been associated with international travel. Clade I may cause more severe illness than clade II, which caused a large U.S. outbreak in 2022. Find out what's happening in San Francisco for free with the latest updates from Patch. Both spread primarily through close skin-to-skin contact, including during sexual activity, and can cause similar symptoms. Both clade strands can cause similar symptoms such as fever, swollen lymph nodes or fatigue, followed by a rash that can resemble pimples or blisters and may be painful. The department recommends that people who develop a rash that looks like mpox contact a health care provider for testing and notify partners who may have been exposed. Anyone can contract mpox, but the department said most reported U.S. cases have been among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men, as well as transgender people who have sex with men. "While we are in a much better place than we were during the 2022 outbreak, mpox infections can and still do occur," San Francisco Health Officer Dr. Susan Philip said in the department's advisory. Health officials are encouraging people at risk for mpox, including those planning to travel to parts of Africa and Europe where clade I has been reported, to receive both doses of the vaccine. The second dose can be given at least 28 days after the first, and the department says people who wait longer than 28 days don't need to restart the series. Booster doses aren't currently recommended for people who have completed both doses, and vaccination is not recommended at this time for people who previously had mpox. Dr. Julia Janssen, deputy director of the department's sexually transmitted infection and HIV branch, said vaccination reduces the chance of infection and helps protect against severe illness, hospitalization and death. The department also says the vaccine can be given to people who are immunocompromised, including people living with HIV or taking medications that suppress the immune system. Health officials cautioned that vaccination does not eliminate the possibility of infection. Fully vaccinated people should still watch for symptoms and seek testing if they develop them. The vaccine is available through health care providers and local pharmacies. The Department of Public Health directs people without insurance or those having difficulty finding a vaccine to its mpox information and vaccination resources at sf.gov/mpox. B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox Surge in Guinea-Bissau: Why Children Are Being Hit?

News Brief
Guinea-Bissau is experiencing its first-ever mpox outbreak, with 46 suspected cases reported as of August 17, 2026. Among these, 23 cases involved children under 15, with 16 specifically aged 0-4, raising concerns about the risk of severe complications in young children. The outbreak was declared on July 4, 2026, and seven confirmed cases were reported, all among adults. Health authorities are emphasizing early detection, testing, and contact tracing to contain the virus, as the lack of clear transmission links suggests possible undetected chains of spread. UNICEF is supporting the Ministry of Public Health and WHO with surveillance, prevention, and community awareness efforts. The reopening of schools in September could increase close contact among children, potentially facilitating further transmission. No deaths have been reported, but the high proportion of pediatric cases has prompted urgent action to prevent the spread of mpox in the country.

Mpox Surge in Guinea-Bissau: Why Children Are Being Hit? Written by Dr. R.S Janani Reviewed by The Medindia News Review Team on Aug 25 2026 12:48 PM Busy? Tap to listen instead of reading 0:00/0:00 A new mpox outbreak is putting children in focus as health authorities investigate suspected infections and work to contain the virus in Guinea-Bissau. Guinea-Bissau is confronting its first-ever mpox outbreak, with 46 suspected cases reported and 23 involving children under 15. UNICEF says 16 of the suspected pediatric cases are children aged 0-4, raising concern over the risk of severe complications. The outbreak was declared on July 4, 2026, and seven cases had been confirmed among adults by August 17. UNICEF has warned that fragile disease surveillance could mean additional infections are going undetected. (1✔ ✔Trusted Source Children account for half of suspected mpox cases in Guinea-Bissau Go to source) WHO Detects Hybrid Mpox Virus After Two Monkeypox Strains Combine WHO detects hybrid mpox strain combining clades Ib and IIb and calls for strengthened surveillance amid ongoing global outbreak activity. Guinea-Bissau Reports Its First Mpox Outbreak Guinea-Bissau declared its first-ever mpox outbreak on July 4, 2026. By August 17, the country had reported seven confirmed cases, all among adults. Six cases were in Bissau, and one was in the Biombo Region. No deaths had been reported. The outbreak is attracting particular attention because of the number of suspected cases among children. Mpox Cases Expanding Beyond At-Risk Groups The 2024 Mpox outbreak is spreading beyond traditional at-risk groups, affecting a broader demographic globally compared to the 2022 outbreak. Half of Suspected Mpox Cases Are Children Of the 46 suspected mpox cases, 23 were children under 15, meaning children account for half of all suspected cases. Sixteen of these children were between 0 and 4 years old. Young children are a major concern because they can be more vulnerable to complications from mpox. The reopening of schools in September could also increase close contact among children and create more opportunities for mpox transmission. When children make up half of suspected cases, every missed infection matters. Which Crucial Protein Elicited Mpox Vaccine Through AI? A novel AI model helped find a key protein, named OPG153, in monkeypox virus, which could lead to cheaper and simple vaccine or a new antibody drug design. Why Health Officials Are Worried About Hidden Transmission? Another concern is that the confirmed mpox (monkeypox) cases do not appear to be linked to one another. This raises the possibility that there are undetected chains of transmission in the community. Guinea-Bissau also has gaps in community surveillance, testing, contact tracing, and identifying how the virus is spreading. These challenges can make it harder to find infections before they spread further. UNICEF is supporting the Ministry of Public Health, WHO, and other partners with surveillance, contact tracing, infection prevention, and community awareness. The sooner an infection is found, the easier it is to stop the chain. Is Monkeypox the Next Pandemic? Mpox (Monkeypox) is spreading worldwide, but is it as dangerous as COVID-19? Explore the facts and WHO's stance on this growing health concern. Mpox Explained: What Causes It, and How Does the Virus Spread? Mpox is an infectious disease caused by the mpox virus, which belongs to the Orthopoxvirus family. It can cause A rash or skin lesions Fever Swollen lymph nodes Headache Muscle aches Tiredness Most people recover, but some can develop serious illness. The virus mainly spreads through close physical contact with an infected person, particularly contact with skin lesions, body fluids, or respiratory secretions. It can also spread through contaminated clothing, bedding, and other materials. Mpox can also spread from infected animals to humans through direct contact. During pregnancy, the virus can pass from an infected pregnant person to the fetus or newborn. What Happens Next? Health authorities are focusing on early detection, testing, contact tracing, and infection prevention to slow the spread of mpox in Guinea-Bissau. Families are being encouraged to seek medical care quickly if a child or other family member develops a rash, fever, swollen lymph nodes, or other possible mpox symptoms. UNICEF says it needs more than US$150,000 in emergency funding to support key parts of the mpox response, including disease surveillance, prevention, and care. Strengthening these efforts will be important as health teams continue to look for new infections. (2✔ ✔Trusted Source Mpox Go to source) Every confirmed case gives health workers another chance to break the chain of infection. Guinea-Bissau's mpox outbreak remains limited in confirmed cases, but the high number of suspected infections among children has raised concern. Continued testing, surveillance, and public awareness can help prevent further spread. When it comes to mpox, catching the virus early is better than chasing it later. The outbreak remains limited in confirmed cases, but the high number of suspected infections among children has raised concern.

安哥拉确认本戈省首例 猴痘 病例,其中 暴发 地区 - Streamlinefeed.co.ke

Angola Confirms First Mpox Case in Bengo Province Amid Regional Outbreak - streamlinefeed.co.ke
新闻简报安哥拉本戈省确认了首例 猴痘 病例,标志着正在进行的地区 暴发 疫情取得重大进展。本戈省卫生办公室负责人 Josefa Gomes 于 2026 年 8 月 30 日宣布了这一确认。该病例正值一场更广泛的全国健康危机之际,自 2024 年 11 月以来已记录了 14 例 猴痘 确诊病例。卡宾达和扎伊尔最近分别出现 7 例和 6 例新感染病例,主要影响 15 至 19 岁的青少年和年轻人。猴痘 对安哥拉医疗系统面临的压力表示担忧,该系统也正在应对严重的霍乱疫情。公共卫生当局目前正在追踪本戈确诊病例的接触者,同时已针对高危人群启动了预防性 疫苗接种 活动。这种情况凸显了管理并发传染病 暴发 的挑战,特别是在资源有限的环境中。安哥拉政府正在平衡当前的医疗支出与长期经济稳定,因为如果不能有效控制,暴发可能会导致全国性影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026年8月27日(发布日期)、2026年8月30日(活动日期)。
地点与对象
安哥拉本戈省,伊科洛·本戈市出现疑似病例,扎伊尔省和卡宾达省出现确诊病例。
事件
安哥拉当局确认本戈省首例猴痘病例,标志着暴发地区的重大发展。本戈省卫生办公室主任 Josefa Gomes 宣布了这一消息。
规模、影响与风险
截至2024年11月,安哥拉全国已记录14例猴痘确诊病例。过去一周,卡宾达报告了 7 例新感染病例,扎伊尔记录了 6 例新病例,主要影响 15 至 19 岁的青少年和年轻人。暴发 加剧了人们对疾病传播和医疗保健系统压力的担忧。
应对、状态与不确定性
公共卫生当局正在追踪本戈确诊病例的接触者。针对高危人群(包括医护人员和边境居民)发起了先发制人的 疫苗接种 活动。猴痘 和霍乱的双重负担使资源分配和后勤变得更加复杂。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Angola Confirms First Mpox Case in Bengo Province Amid Regional Outbreak - streamlinefeed.co.ke

News Brief
Angola has confirmed its first Mpox case in the Bengo province, marking a significant development in the ongoing regional outbreak. The confirmation was announced by Josefa Gomes, head of the Provincial Health Office in Bengo, on August 30, 2026. This case comes amid a broader national health crisis, with 14 confirmed Mpox cases recorded since November 2024. Recent outbreaks in Cabinda and Zaire have seen seven and six new infections, respectively, primarily affecting adolescents and young adults aged 15 to 19. The spread of Mpox is raising concerns about the strain on Angola's healthcare system, which is also dealing with a severe cholera epidemic. Public health authorities are now tracing contacts of the confirmed case in Bengo, while preemptive vaccination campaigns have been launched for high-risk groups. The situation highlights the challenges of managing concurrent infectious disease outbreaks, especially in resource-constrained settings. The Angolan government is balancing immediate health expenditures with long-term economic stability, as the outbreak could lead to nationwide implications if not controlled effectively.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Global editionSunday, August 30, 2026 Streamline Search⌘KWriteSign inJoin Streamline StreamlineGlobal edition LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSportDirectoryHouse HuntingPeopleHistoryMore LatestWorldBusinessPoliticsTechnologyScienceCultureSport Back to News/World News World News Angola Confirms First Mpox Case in Bengo Province Amid Regional Outbreak Bengo province records its first Mpox case as the viral outbreak spreads across Angola, complicating the nation's ongoing battle against a severe cholera epidemic. The first 10 seconds cannot be skipped. Ad playing Ad playing. Learn more Build your reading streak0/3 today·Counts at 25% Angolan public health authorities have officially confirmed the first positive case of Mpox in the northwestern province of Bengo, escalating concerns over the steady proliferation of the viral disease across the southern African nation. The formal confirmation marks a critical turning point in regional epidemiological containment strategies, as provincial borders become increasingly porous to viral transmission. Josefa Gomes, the head of the Public Health Department at the Provincial Health Office in Bengo, announced the positive diagnosis to the press on Thursday. The confirmation shatters a period of cautious optimism in the province, which had previously recorded negative laboratory results for three suspected pediatric cases in the adjacent Icolo e Bengo municipality in June 2026. The shift from suspected to confirmed status underscores the rapid evolution of the continental health emergency, prompting immediate reviews of national resource allocation and disease surveillance protocols. National Epidemiological Landscape The confirmed case in Bengo is not an isolated incident but rather the latest data point in a growing national health crisis. Angolan authorities have been battling a persistent Mpox outbreak that has stubbornly expanded despite targeted interventions. Epidemiological data paints a concerning picture of the viral spread across multiple administrative zones. Since November 2024, the Angolan Ministry of Health has officially recorded 14 confirmed cases of Mpox nationwide. Within the past week, the oil-rich enclave of Cabinda reported seven new confirmed infections within a 24-hour window. Simultaneously, the northern province of Zaire documented six new cases, predominantly affecting adolescents and young adults aged 15 to 19 from the Tando Zinze, Liambo, and Ngoio municipalities. To date, suspected cases have triggered epidemiological investigations in at least five distinct provinces, draining laboratory resources at the National Institute of Health Research in Luanda. The concentration of recent cases in border provinces like Zaire and Cabinda highlights the vulnerability of cross-border transit routes. Prior to the confirmed infection in Bengo, local health administrators had proactively initiated preemptive vaccination campaigns. These drives targeted high-risk demographics, including frontline healthcare workers, defense and security personnel, and residents in border municipalities such as Ambriz, Nambuangongo, Muxaluando, and Quicunzo. Overlapping Public Health Crises The Mpox escalation arrives at a highly precarious moment for the Angolan healthcare system, which is simultaneously grappling with a severe cholera epidemic. According to recent situational reports from the Africa Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (Africa CDC), cholera infections have penetrated 17 of Angola's 21 provinces. The epicenters of the cholera outbreak, including Luanda, Cuanza Norte, and crucially, Bengo and Icolo e Bengo, account for approximately 98 percent of all reported cases. The dual burden of Mpox and cholera is stretching provincial medical infrastructure to its absolute limits. The Africa CDC has deployed specialized incidence management teams to Luanda to assist in coordinating the multidisciplinary response. However, the convergence of a waterborne bacterial outbreak with a contact-transmitted viral epidemic necessitates fundamentally different logistical supply chains, complicating the distribution of vaccines, therapeutics, and personal protective equipment. The financial toll on the state budget is mounting, with contingency funds being rapidly depleted to sustain dual-track epidemiological surveillance. Global and East African Implications The steady march of the Mpox virus through Angola carries significant implications for the broader African continent and global health security networks. For East African nations, particularly Kenya and Uganda, the Angolan outbreak serves as a stark warning regarding the speed of regional transmission. The Central Bank of Kenya estimates that cross-border trade disruptions caused by health emergencies can severely impact regional gross domestic product. If the outbreak necessitates border closures or strict quarantine measures within the Southern African Development Community (SADC), supply chain bottlenecks could artificially inflate commodity prices across the continent. From an economic perspective, the mobilization of domestic health resources diverts critical funding away from infrastructure and development projects. The cost of deploying comprehensive vaccination rings and maintaining strict isolation wards runs into millions of dollars. For instance, equivalent public health mobilizations in Kenya routinely cost upwards of KES 4.5 billion to sustain over a fiscal quarter. The Angolan government must now balance these immediate health expenditures against long-term economic stability. As the Ministry of Health works to trace the contacts of the Bengo patient, the international community watches closely. The efficacy of Angola's localized containment strategy will likely inform global best practices for managing concurrent infectious disease outbreaks in resource-constrained environments. Without rapid intervention and sustained international support, the localized clusters in Bengo, Zaire, and Cabinda risk coalescing into a nationwide epidemic. How did this story land? 谁应该在旧金山秋季派对季节开始之前获得 猴痘 疫苗?

Who Should Get an Mpox Vaccine Before San Francisco’s Fall Party Season Begins?
新闻简报旧金山卫生官员敦促高危居民在秋季派对季节之前接种 猴痘 疫苗,秋季派对季节包括福尔瑟姆街 (Folsom Street) 和波托拉音乐节 (Portola Music Festival) 等活动。旧金山公共卫生部今年确认了 70 多例 猴痘 病例,其中 8 例涉及 I 型菌株,该菌株与其他地区出现更严重的症状有关。官员们指出,由于社交聚会的增加,猴痘 病例通常在夏季和秋季上升。他们推荐 Jynneos 疫苗,它可以预防分支 I 和分支 II 猴痘,特别是对于那些计划前往分支 I 传播的地区的人。虽然没有证据表明旧金山存在本地分支 I 传播,但所有确诊病例都与国际旅行有关。疫苗 是一个双剂量系列,在第二次剂量两周后可建立最大免疫力。卫生官员强调,疫苗 是现有的最佳保护,已经接种一剂的个人如果距离第一剂已经过去 28 天或更长时间,则应接种第二剂。

时间

地点与对象
美国旧金山;高危人群包括男同性恋和双性恋男性、男男性行为的跨性别者以及计划前往 I 猴痘 分支传播地区的个人。
事件
旧金山卫生官员提醒高危居民在秋季派对季节之前全面接种 猴痘 疫苗,包括福尔瑟姆街 (Folsom Street) 和波托拉音乐节 (Portola Music Festival) 等活动。旧金山公共卫生部今年确认了 70 多例 猴痘 病例,其中 8 例涉及 I 分支 猴痘,该分支与其他地区更严重的症状相关。
规模、影响与风险
今年旧金山报告了 70 多起 猴痘 病例,其中 8 起涉及 I 支系 猴痘。该病毒通过密切的皮肤接触(包括性行为)传播,病例通常在夏季和秋季增加。卫生官员建议使用 Jynneos 疫苗 来预防 传播,特别是对于那些前往 I 型 猴痘 传播地区旅行的人。
应对、状态与不确定性
旧金山卫生官员强调 Jynneos 疫苗 是主要的保护工具。他们建议高危人群和前往 I 型 猴痘 传播地区旅行的人群使用 疫苗接种。没有证据表明旧金山境内存在 I 传播 分支,所有确诊病例都与国际旅行有关。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Who Should Get an Mpox Vaccine Before San Francisco’s Fall Party Season Begins?

News Brief
San Francisco health officials are urging at-risk residents to get vaccinated against mpox ahead of the fall party season, which includes events like the Folsom Street and Portola Music Festival. The San Francisco Department of Public Health confirmed over 70 cases of mpox this year, with eight involving clade I, a strain linked to more severe symptoms in other regions. Officials note that mpox cases typically rise during summer and fall due to increased social gatherings. They recommend the Jynneos vaccine, which protects against both clade I and clade II mpox, especially for those planning to travel to areas where clade I is spreading. While there is no evidence of local clade I transmission in San Francisco, all confirmed cases are associated with international travel. The vaccine is a two-dose series, with maximal immunity building two weeks after the second dose. Health officials emphasize that the vaccine is the best protection available and that individuals who have already received one dose should get the second dose if it has been 28 days or more since the first.
Original source text (English)

Donor-Advised Funds Support KQED by using your donor-advised fund to make a charitable gift. upper waypoint News Home / Guides and Explainers Who Should Get an Mpox Vaccine Before San Francisco’s Fall Party Season Begins? Ahead of San Francisco's slate of gatherings and shows, city health officials are reminding at-risk residents to get fully vaccinated against mpox. Here’s how to find an mpox vaccine. Aug 27, 2026 Updated 12:16 pm PT Carlos Cabrera-LomelíCarly Severn Ria Garcia, licensed vocational nurse with the Los Angeles County Department of Public Health, draws up mpox vaccines at the Balboa Sports Complex vaccine site in Encino on Sept. 8, 2022. (Sarah Reingewirtz/MediaNews Group/Los Angeles Daily News via Getty Images) Ahead of San Francisco’s jam-packed fall slate of gatherings and concerts — including Folsom Street and Portola Music Festival — city health officials are reminding at-risk residents to get fully vaccinated against mpox. The San Francisco Department of Public Health confirmed on Tuesday that over 70 cases of mpox — formerly known as monkeypox — have been reported in San Francisco this year. Eight of these infections involve clade I mpox: a strain that in other parts of the world has caused more severe symptoms than the clade II strain associated with the 2022 outbreak in the Bay Area. According to SFDPH, mpox cases usually increase in summer and fall — especially when the city is hosting events that draw people from all over the world. “People come together, they’re meeting,” said Dr. Julia Janssen, deputy director of the STI and HIV branch at SFDPH. “That’s why we emphasize and recommend the Jynneos vaccine during this time.” Thousands of people dance as Disclosure performs at Portola Music Festival in San Francisco on Sept. 29, 2024. (Estefany Gonzalez for KQED) Ahead of San Francisco Pride celebrations back in June, health officials recommended the mpox vaccine — which has the brand name Jynneos — to the same at-risk populations that have been most affected by previous mpox outbreaks. These included gay and bisexual men, along with transgender people who have sex with men. But the fact that the clade I strain is also starting to spread among local populations in popular tourist destinations — including Italy, Spain and Portugal — has prompted San Francisco health officials to now recommend that more people beyond those primary at-risk groups get vaccinated if they’re planning to travel to these areas. “We have started to see local transmission in Western Europe,” Janssen said. “We recommend that anyone traveling outside of the United States — particularly to a country where clade I mpox is spreading between people — and [who] anticipates having sex during travel: Get the Jynneos vaccine.” The recommendation is also extended to Bay Area folks who have sex here with anyone who has just traveled to those areas. “There’s currently no evidence of local clade I transmission within San Francisco,” Janssen said. All confirmed clade I mpox cases in the city have been associated with international travel. ‘The absolute best protection’ The symptoms of clade I and clade II mpox are similar and can initially resemble the flu. The virus also appears as a rash or sores or spots that resemble pimples or blisters on the skin anywhere on the body, including the face, inside the mouth, hands, feet, chest, genitals and anus. Related Article Clade I mpox, however, has historically caused more severe disease and more deaths in other countries, according to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. During the 2022 outbreak, more than 15,000 San Francisco residents received both doses of the mpox vaccine. “That led to a dramatic decrease in cases,” Janssen said. “We’re in a much better place now because of the uptake of the Jynneos vaccine in San Francisco.” She called the vaccine “really the absolute best protection” and “the main tool in our toolbox.” Both clade I and clade II usually spread through close skin-to-skin contact, including sex. Anyone can get mpox, but most cases in the United States are among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men, as well as transgender people who have sex with men. On the heels of SDFPH’s advisory, here’s what to know about mpox and the Jynneos vaccine in the Bay Area this fall. Who’s eligible for an mpox vaccine in the Bay Area? The vaccine currently available in the U.S. (brand name: Jynneos) is a two-dose series, with roughly a month between doses. Maximal immunity will build two weeks after your second dose. The vaccine “protects against both clade 1 and clade 2 mpox,” Janssen said, and added that if someone just got one dose in the past, they may just need the second dose now. “Anyone who has received one dose can go ahead and receive their second dose if it has been 28 days or more since their first dose,” she said. “There’s no need to restart.” The Leather Pride flag flies at the Folsom Street Fair in San Francisco on Sept. 30, 2018. (Michelle Wiley/KQED) The CDC recommends the two-dose mpox vaccine to gay and bisexual men and other men who have sex with men, as well as transgender, nonbinary or gender-diverse people who in the past 6 months have had: More than one sexual partner and/or A new diagnosis of one or more sexually transmitted infections. The CDC’s vaccine recommendations also include anyone who has had sex at a commercial sex venue like a sex club or bathhouse. See the CDC’s full mpox vaccine eligibility recommendations. San Francisco additionally recommends vaccination for anyone living with HIV, anyone taking PrEP or who’s eligible to take it and sex workers. What about travel and mpox? In 2025, SFDPH expanded their mpox vaccine recommendations to include anyone traveling outside the U.S. — particularly to a country where clade I mpox is spreading between people — who anticipates having sex while they travel. The CDC recommends getting both doses of the Jynneos vaccine “if you anticipate experiencing any of the following” while traveling to these countries: Warren Manio, 36, sits on top of his friend’s shoulders as Natasha Bedingfield performs at Portola Music Festival in San Francisco on Sept. 28, 2024. (Estefany Gonzalez for KQED) Sex with a new partner Sex at a commercial sex venue, such as a sex club or bathhouse Sex in exchange for money, goods, drugs or other trade Sex in association with a large public event, such as a rave, party or festival. As of August this year, SFDPH also recommends that if you’re a Bay Area resident who is having sex with a person who’s traveled to these countries, you should also seek out the vaccine. The mpox vaccine was also originally only available for people aged 18 and older, but in 2022, the Food and Drug Administration (FDA) issued an emergency-use authorization that allows providers to also give the vaccine to young people aged under 18 who are “determined to be at high risk” of infection. Where can I find an mpox vaccine? If you have a regular health care provider, SFDPH recommends you ask them first about getting the mpox vaccine. Your vaccine will be free, but you may be charged a regular copay for seeing your provider. If you’re eligible for the vaccine, your health insurance should cover the costs thanks to the CDC’s recommendations. If you don’t have a regular health care provider or insurance, SFDPH said you can visit one of these four pharmacies in the city (two Walgreens and two CVS locations). A vial of the mpox vaccine is displayed by a medical professional at a vaccination site in July, 2022. (James Carbone/Newsday RM via Getty Images) (As with the COVID-19 vaccine, receiving an mpox vaccine won’t make you a public charge or affect any future immigration processes you may enter into, and you won’t be asked about your immigration status to receive the mpox vaccine.) If you get your health care through a health system like Kaiser Permanente, it’s unlikely you’ll be able to get your mpox vaccine covered by insurance at a pharmacy like CVS or Walgreens — the way you can’t get your COVID-19 or flu shot covered by Kaiser at a pharmacy either — and may have to seek it directly from a Kaiser provider. If you’re able to make an appointment online at a pharmacy for your mpox vaccine, you should consider calling that location ahead of time to verify that they do indeed have supplies in stock. Location and population
San Francisco, United States; at-risk populations include gay and bisexual men, transgender people who have sex with men, and individuals planning to travel to areas where clade I mpox is spreading.
Event
San Francisco health officials are reminding at-risk residents to get fully vaccinated against mpox ahead of the fall party season, including events like Folsom Street and Portola Music Festival. The San Francisco Department of Public Health confirmed over 70 cases of mpox this year, with eight involving clade I mpox, which is associated with more severe symptoms in other regions.
Scale, impact and risk
Over 70 mpox cases have been reported in San Francisco this year, with eight involving clade I mpox. The virus spreads through close skin-to-skin contact, including sex, and cases usually increase in summer and fall. Health officials recommend the Jynneos vaccine to prevent transmission, especially for those traveling to areas with clade I mpox spread.
Response, status and uncertainty
San Francisco health officials are emphasizing the Jynneos vaccine as the main tool for protection. They recommend vaccination for at-risk populations and those traveling to areas with clade I mpox spread. There is no evidence of local clade I transmission within San Francisco, and all confirmed cases are associated with international travel.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

菲律宾在猴痘威胁中“加强”边境筛查 - Rappler

Philippines ‘intensifying’ border screening amid monkeypox threat - Rappler
新闻简报菲律宾于 2022 年 5 月 20 日加强了边境筛查和 监测 系统,以监测欧洲国家、美国、英国和加拿大发现的猴痘威胁。卫生部(DOH)证实,菲律宾及其边境尚未发现猴痘。猴痘是一种病毒性疾病,主要发现于中非和西非,引起发烧、皮疹和淋巴结肿大。它有两种主要毒株:刚果毒株,该毒株较为严重,死亡率高达10%;西非毒株,致死率约为1%。据报道,英国病例为西非毒株。卫生部敦促公众维持最低健康标准,以预防 传播。该疾病通过与受感染的个人、动物或受污染的材料密切接触而传播。虽然猴痘与天花相似,但其传染性较低,引起的疾病也较轻。本周早些时候,卫生部还确认了 Omicron 亚变体 BA.2.12.1 的本地 传播,突显了疫情期间持续存在的健康挑战。
查看新闻五要素
时间
本周早些时候,卫生部确认了 Omicron 亚变体 BA.2.12.1 的本地 传播,菲律宾于 2022 年 5 月 20 日加强了边境筛查。
地点与对象
菲律宾,特别关注边境地区和普通民众。
事件
在欧洲国家、美国、英国和加拿大发现猴痘威胁后,菲律宾加强了边境筛查和 监测 系统来监测猴痘威胁。卫生部表示,菲律宾及其边境尚未发现猴痘。
规模、影响与风险
猴痘是一种病毒性疾病,主要发生在中非和西非的热带雨林地区,偶尔也会传播到其他地区。它会引起发烧症状和独特的凹凸不平的皮疹,有两种主要菌株:刚果菌株(高达 10% 死亡率)和西非菌株(死亡率约为 1%)。据报道,英国病例为西非毒株。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部敦促公众遵守预防猴痘的最低健康标准 传播。卫生部本周早些时候确认了 Omicron 亚变体 BA.2.12.1 的本地 传播,但在菲律宾或其边境没有发现猴痘病例。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Philippines ‘intensifying’ border screening amid monkeypox threat - Rappler

News Brief
The Philippines intensified border screening and surveillance systems on May 20, 2022, to monitor the monkeypox threat following its detection in European countries, the United States, the United Kingdom, and Canada. The Department of Health (DOH) confirmed that monkeypox has not been detected in the Philippines or at its borders. Monkeypox is a viral disease primarily found in Central and West Africa, causing fever, rash, and swollen lymph nodes. It has two main strains: the Congo strain, which is more severe with up to 10% mortality, and the West African strain, with a fatality rate of about 1%. The UK cases were reported as the West African strain. The DOH urged the public to maintain minimum health standards to prevent transmission. The disease spreads through close contact with infected individuals, animals, or contaminated materials. While monkeypox resembles smallpox, it is less contagious and causes less severe illness. The DOH also confirmed the local transmission of the Omicron subvariant BA.2.12.1 earlier this week, highlighting ongoing health challenges amid the pandemic.
Original source text (English)

An image created during an investigation into an outbreak of monkeypox, which took place in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, 1996 to 1997, shows the arms and torso of a patient with skin lesions due to monkeypox, in this undated image obtained by Reuters on May 18, 2022. CDC/Brian W.J. Mahy/Handout via Reuters INFO The Department of Health says that monkeypox has not been detected in the Philippines or at its borders Play Video MANILA, Philippines – The Department of Health (DOH) on Friday, May 20, said it was “intensifying” border screening and “ensuring that surveillance systems are actively monitoring the situation” following the detection of monkeypox cases in European countries, the United States, the United Kingdom, and Canada. The DOH said that monkeypox has not been detected in the Philippines or at its borders. Monkeypox is a viral disease coming from animals “that occurs primarily in tropical rainforest areas of Central and West Africa and is occasionally exported to other regions.” Monkeypox is a virus that causes fever symptoms as well as a distinctive bumpy rash. It is usually mild, although there are two main strains: the Congo strain, which is more severe – with up to 10% mortality – and the West African strain, which has a fatality rate in about 1% of cases. The UK cases have been reported as the West African strain. Symptoms of monkeypox include fever, rash, and swollen lymp nodes, which may lead to a range of medical implications. The DOH urged the public to continue observing minimum health standards to prevent monkeypox transmission. The viral disease is being transmitted to humans through close contact with an infected person, animal, or contaminated materials. “While monkeypox look like smallpox, it is less contagious and causes less severe illness,” the DOH said. Must Read EXPLAINER: Why monkeypox cases are spreading in Europe, US The recent news about monkeypox comes as the Philippines and the rest of the world is still dealing with the COVID-19 pandemic. Earlier this week, the DOH confirmed the local transmission of the more contagious Omicron subvariant BA.2.12.1. – Rappler.com Share in Chat Share article Facebook X (Twitter) Copy Link Copied How does this make you feel? SSM确认澳门输入猴痘病例 - 澳门邮报

SSM confirms Macau imported mpox case - The Macau Post Daily
新闻简报澳门卫生局(SSM)确认一宗输入性猴痘病例,涉及一名本地年轻男性居民,8月18日在外地旅行时出现发烧、头痛和肌肉疼痛等症状。后来他出现了皮疹,并在没有就医的情况下自行用药。返澳后,他于8月24日到私立镜湖医院求诊及采集样本,结果猴痘呈阳性。该患者目前正在公立 Conde Januário 医院中心接受住院治疗,病情稳定。SSM指出,患者在澳门或出现症状后没有高风险性行为,并否认与啮齿动物或灵长类动物接触。该局强调,公众风险仍然较低,大规模疫苗接种没有必要。它敦促公众实行安全性行为,避免临时性伴侣或多名伴侣,并避免接触疑似感染的人、动物或受污染的物品。患者的同居者尚未出现症状,但 SSM 将安排他们接受 猴痘 疫苗。卫生局还强调,高危人群(包括男男性行为者)可以通过当地卫生中心获得暴露前 疫苗接种。
查看新闻五要素
时间
卫生局于2026年8月24日公布该病例,患者于8月18日在国外出现病征,返回澳门后求医。
地点与对象
澳门,特别涉及一名曾出国旅行并返回澳门的当地年轻男性居民。
事件
卫生局证实一宗输入性猴痘病例,涉及一名本地年轻男性居民,他在外地旅行时出现病征,返回澳门后检测结果呈猴痘阳性。
规模、影响与风险
该病例是孤立病例,没有报告同居者出现症状。卫生局表示,公众风险仍然较低,没有必要进行大规模疫苗接种。该患者正在住院治疗治疗,病情稳定。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生局正进行流行病学调查,并已安排同居者领取猴痘 疫苗。该患者正在接受住院治疗,该局正在敦促采取安全性行为,并避免与疑似感染者接触。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

SSM confirms Macau imported mpox case - The Macau Post Daily

News Brief
The Health Bureau (SSM) of Macau confirmed an imported mpox case involving a young male local resident who developed symptoms including fever, headache, and muscle pain on August 18 while traveling abroad. He later developed a skin rash and self-medicated without seeking medical attention. Upon returning to Macau, he visited the private Kiang Wu Hospital for consultation and sample collection on August 24, testing positive for mpox. The patient is currently receiving inpatient treatment at the public Conde Januário Hospital Centre and is in stable condition. The SSM noted that the patient had no high-risk sexual behavior in Macau or after symptom onset and denied contact with rodents or primates. The bureau emphasized that the general public risk remains low, and mass vaccination is unnecessary. It urged the public to practice safe sex, avoid casual or multiple partners, and refrain from contact with people, animals, or contaminated items suspected to be infected. The patient’s cohabitants have not shown symptoms, but the SSM will arrange for them to receive the mpox vaccine. The Health Bureau also highlighted that pre-exposure vaccination is available for high-risk individuals, including men who have sex with men, through local health centres.
Original source text (English)
The Health Bureau (SSM) announced in a statement yesterday that it had received a report of one imported mpox* case involving a young male local resident. According to the statement, the patient had developed symptoms including fever, headache, and muscle pain on August 18 while travelling abroad, developing a skin rash the following day and self-medicating without seeking medical attention. When he returned to Macau, the statement said he visited the private Kiang Wu Hospital for consultation and sample collection on Monday, testing positive for mpox. As his symptoms persisted, he subsequently sought care at the public Conde S. Januário Hospital Centre and is currently receiving inpatient treatment. The statement noted that he was in stable condition. The statement added that the patient’s cohabitants have not shown symptoms, though the bureau will still arrange for them to receive the mpox vaccine. The statement pointed out that the patient had, according to epidemiological investigations, engaged in “high-risk sexual behaviour in the Chinese mainland during the incubation period”. He had no high-risk sexual behaviour in Macau or after symptom onset, and denied contact with rodents or primates. He had not been vaccinated for the disease. Mpox, according to the statement, spreads primarily through high-risk sexual behavior – especially among individuals with multiple partners or men who have sex with men—as well as through direct contact with infected skin lesions, contaminated objects, prolonged face-to-face respiratory droplets, or infected animals like rodents and primates. The Health Bureau urged the public to practise safe sex and avoid casual or multiple partners, as well as avoid contact with people, animals, or contaminated items suspected to be infected. Moreover, the bureau said that if symptoms appear, one should immediately seek medical attention, disclose relevant exposure history, and avoid sexual activity or close contact with others, the statement said. The statement added that “high-risk individuals aged 18 or above (including men who have sex with men) may consider getting vaccinated against mpox”. The bureau emphasised that, according to information from the WHO and other regions, general public risk remains low and mass vaccination is unnecessary. The statement said it carries a low global fatality rate of around 0.1 percent in non-endemic areas. Pre-exposure vaccination is available for high-risk individuals via local health centres. Macau residents assessed as high risk can receive the vaccine free of charge, while non-resident workers or those legally permitted to stay in Macau may receive paid shots, while tourists remain ineligible. *In late 2022, the World Health Organisation (WHO) recommended transitioning from “monkeypox” to “mpox” to mitigate racist and stigmatising language online, as well as to prevent inaccurate associations with monkeys (rodents are actually the main primary reservoir). – Gemini This image downloaded from the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) shows the symptoms of mpox
View five news elements
Time
The Health Bureau (SSM) announced the case on August 24, 2026, with the patient developing symptoms on August 18 while abroad and seeking medical attention upon returning to Macau.
Location and population
Macau, specifically involving a young male local resident who had traveled abroad and returned to Macau.
Event
The Health Bureau confirmed an imported mpox case involving a young male local resident who developed symptoms while traveling abroad and tested positive for mpox upon returning to Macau.
Scale, impact and risk
The case is isolated, with no reported symptoms in cohabitants. The Health Bureau stated the general public risk remains low and mass vaccination is unnecessary. The patient is receiving inpatient treatment and is in stable condition.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Health Bureau is conducting epidemiological investigations and has arranged for cohabitants to receive the mpox vaccine. The patient is under inpatient treatment, and the bureau is urging safe sex practices and avoiding contact with suspected infected individuals.

Published: 2026-08-25  |  Publisher: AOL.com

卫生官员在 Folsom Street Fair 之前推荐 猴痘 疫苗接种 - AOL.com

Health officials recommend mpox vaccination before Folsom Street Fair - AOL.com
新闻简报旧金山卫生官员敦促有 猴痘 风险的人们在即将于 2026 年 8 月举行的福尔瑟姆街集市之前接种疫苗。截至 8 月 24 日,该市已报告了 70 多例 猴痘 病例,凸显了该病毒的持续风险。旧金山公共卫生部强调,虽然与 2022 年 暴发 相比情况有所改善,但 猴痘 感染仍然可能发生。他们强烈推荐高危人群使用疫苗接种,特别是男男性行为者和男男性行为者。疫苗 可通过医疗保健提供者和当地药房购买,并为没有保险的人提供额外支持。官员们还鼓励个人注意 猴痘 症状,包括发烧、淋巴结肿大、疲劳以及类似丘疹或水泡的皮疹。该建议旨在确保与会者度过一个安全健康的福尔瑟姆街博览会周末。
查看新闻五要素
时间
旧金山公共卫生部敦促人们在 2026 年 8 月福尔瑟姆街集市即将到来之前保护自己免受 猴痘 感染。24 日,旧金山已报告了 70 多起 猴痘 病例。
地点与对象
美国旧金山。受影响人群包括有 猴痘 风险的人群,特别是 LGBTQ+ 群体中的人群,因为美国报告的大多数病例是同性恋、双性恋和其他男男性行为者,以及男同性恋者的跨性别者。
事件
卫生官员在福尔瑟姆街集市之前向高危人群推荐 猴痘 疫苗接种。旧金山公共卫生部敦促个人充分接种疫苗并了解 猴痘 的症状。
规模、影响与风险
正如卫生官员所说,尽管情况比 2022 年 暴发 期间要好,但 猴痘 感染的风险仍然存在。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生官员推荐 疫苗接种 并敦促人们注意症状。猴痘 疫苗 可通过医疗保健提供者和当地药房购买,没有保险的人可以在该市的网站上找到更多信息。

Health officials recommend mpox vaccination before Folsom Street Fair - AOL.com

News Brief
Health officials in San Francisco are urging people at risk of mpox to get vaccinated before the upcoming Folsom Street Fair, which is set to take place in August 2026. As of August 24, more than 70 mpox cases have been reported in the city, highlighting the ongoing risk of the virus. The San Francisco Department of Public Health emphasized that while the situation has improved compared to the 2022 outbreak, mpox infections can still occur. They strongly recommend vaccination for those in high-risk groups, particularly men who have sex with men and transgender individuals who have sex with men. The vaccine is available through healthcare providers and local pharmacies, with additional support for those without insurance. Officials also encourage individuals to be aware of mpox symptoms, which include fever, swollen lymph nodes, fatigue, and a rash resembling pimples or blisters. The recommendation aims to ensure a safe and healthy Folsom Street Fair weekend for attendees.
Original source text (English)
SAN FRANCISCO ( KRON ) -- With the Folsom Street Fair on the horizon, the San Francisco Department of Public Health is urging people to protect themselves against mpox. The two-dose vaccine is strongly encouraged for people at risk. This year, more than 70 mpox cases have been reported in San Francisco as of Aug. 24, according to health officials. Anyone can get mpox, but most reported cases in the United States are among gay, bisexual and other men who have sex with men, as well as transgender persons who have sex with men. “While we are in a much better place than we were during the 2022 outbreak, mpox infections can and still do occur," said San Francisco Health Officer Dr. Susan Philip in a news release. "SFDPH wants people to have a fun and healthy Folsom Street Fair weekend. We strongly encourage people at risk of getting mpox to know the symptoms and be fully vaccinated. There is still time to start the vaccine series before the events.” 49ers owner Jed York prostitution bust: What are ‘criminal tools?’ Symptoms include fever, swollen lymph nodes or fatigue, followed by a rash that looks like pimples or blisters and can be painful. People who have a rash that looks like mpox should contact their healthcare provider. The mpox vaccine is available through healthcare providers and local pharmacies. Those without insurance, or who have difficulty accessing care, can find more information on the city's website . Copyright 2026 Nexstar Media, Inc. All rights reserved. WAAC 推出以霍顿·谢泼德 (Holden Sheppard) 为主角的厚脸皮新 猴痘 PSA - 明星观察者网

WAAC Launches Cheeky New Mpox PSA Featuring Holden Sheppard - Star Observer
新闻简报西澳大利亚州艾滋病委员会 WAAC 于 2026 年 8 月 30 日发布了一项公益公告 (PSA),其中霍尔顿·谢泼德 (Holden Sheppard) 向男男性接触者宣传 猴痘 疫苗接种。该公益广告是针对据报道西澳大利亚 猴痘 病例增加(超过 2025 年)而发布的。该活动针对可能不会公开露面的“低调”男性,强调 疫苗接种 对于预防皮肤接触 传播 的重要性。该 PSA 在 WAAC 的 Instagram 上发布,鼓励社区成员在 Perth Steam Works 接种疫苗,那里每周提供两次免费医疗预约和 疫苗接种。屡获殊荣的作家霍尔顿·谢泼德 (Holden Sheppard) 以幽默而引人入胜的语气主持了该视频,而当地医生 Daniel Vujcich 医生则提供了有关 疫苗接种 过程的医疗指导。该倡议旨在提高高危人群的认识并提高 疫苗接种 率。
查看新闻五要素
时间
据报道 2026 年早些时候西澳大利亚州 猴痘 病例有所增加,该 PSA 于 2026 年 8 月 30 日启动。
地点与对象
该公益广告针对的是西澳大利亚州的男男性行为者,特别是那些经常在珀斯蒸汽工厂 (Perth Steam Works) 场所发生性行为的男性。
事件
WAAC(西澳大利亚艾滋病委员会)推出了一项以 Holden Sheppard 为主角的公益公告 (PSA),以在男男性行为者中宣传 猴痘 疫苗接种。
规模、影响与风险
西澳的猴痘病例已超过2025年,表明病例数有所上升。PSA 旨在鼓励 疫苗接种 减轻病毒的传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
WAAC 正在通过 PSA 积极推广 疫苗接种,每周两次在 Perth Steam Works 提供免费医疗预约和 疫苗接种。响应正在进行中,旨在提高社区意识和参与度。

WAAC Launches Cheeky New Mpox PSA Featuring Holden Sheppard - Star Observer

News Brief
WAAC, the Western Australia Aids Council, launched a public service announcement (PSA) on August 30, 2026, featuring Holden Sheppard to promote Mpox vaccinations among men who have sex with men. The PSA was released in response to a reported rise in Mpox cases in Western Australia, which exceeded those in 2025. The campaign targets men on the 'down low' who may not be out publicly, emphasizing the importance of vaccination to prevent skin-to-skin transmission. The PSA, launched on WAAC's Instagram, encourages community members to get vaccinated at Perth Steam Works, where free medical appointments and vaccinations are available twice weekly. Holden Sheppard, an award-winning author, leads the video with a humorous and engaging tone, while local doctor Dr. Daniel Vujcich provides medical guidance on the vaccination process. The initiative aims to raise awareness and increase vaccination rates among at-risk populations.
Original source text (English)

The sexual health organisation has teamed up with Invisible Boys author Holden Sheppard for their latest video. And it delivers some very unique and cheeky one liners. Mpox Vaccinsations at Perth Steamworks Earlier this year, we reported that MPox cases were on the rise in Western Australia after data released indicated that current cases to date in 2026 have exceeded all of those in 2025. In response, WAAC (formerly the Western Australia Aids Council) is reaching out to the community to encourage everyone to get vaccinated. Launching on their Instagram this week, the organisation dropped their latest public health announcement via video, taking a light-hearted approach to the serious medical issue. “Dragging your feet when it comes to mpox? @holdensheppard is here to tell YOU why you should stop being a drongo and why getting vaccinated for mpox is important for you, your s*x life, and your health” they wrote. Holden Sheppard leads the video, introducing himself as an “award winning author, non award winning labourer and notorious homosexual” as he makes his way to the local sex on premises venue, Perth Steam Works. The video cleverly targets men who are on the “down low” and who may not be out publicly to friends and family, reminding them of the transmission of Mpox through skin-to-skin contact. Sheppard is joined in the video by local Dr Daniel Vujcich, who reminds viewers they simply need to vaccination needles, 28 days apart. “Some guys complete that they don’t like needles. But we know that the guys who come her can handle getting poked” Sheppard quips as he stands in the change room encouraging people to get their jabs. The video goes on to detail that Perth Steam Works also hosts free medical appointments, including vaccinations on site, twice per week, Mondays from 12-2pm or Thursdays from 5.30-7.30pm. Sheppard ends the video hilariously reminding everyone, next time they head to the “vegemite valley,” to head on in and “get a couple of pricks up ya” at the Perth Steam Works. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

欧盟卫生机构:猴痘不易传播 - Yeni Safak

Monkeypox does not spread easily: EU health agency - Yeni Safak English
新闻简报The EU health agency, specifically the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC), stated that monkeypox does not spread easily between people. The agency emphasized that transmission occurs through close contact with infectious material from skin lesions, prolonged face-to-face contact via respiratory droplets, and fomites. Most patients diagnosed with monkeypox in Europe were gay men, and the nature of the presenting lesions in some cases suggests transmission occurred during sexual intercourse. The ECDC noted that patients have so far only presented mild symptoms during the current outbreak, but monkeypox could cause severe disease in general. To mitigate risks, the ECDC recommended that EU member states require monkeypox patients to remain isolated and avoid close physical contact until their rash heals completely. Close contacts of monkeypox patients should observe symptoms carefully for 21 days and refrain from donating blood, organs, or bone marrow. The report was published on 2022-05-24 and is still relevant as of 2026-08-26.
查看新闻五要素
时间
2022-05-24 to 2026-08-26.
地点与对象
九个欧盟国家:奥地利、比利时、法国、德国、意大利、葡萄牙、西班牙、瑞典和荷兰。拥有多个性伴侣的男同性恋者面临更高的风险。
事件
欧盟卫生机构,特别是欧洲疾病预防和控制中心(ECDC)表示,猴痘不易在人与人之间传播。它强调,传播 是通过密切接触皮肤损伤的传染性物质、通过呼吸道飞沫和污染物长时间面对面接触而发生的。
规模、影响与风险
在欧洲,大多数被诊断出猴痘的患者都是男同性恋。ECDC 指出,某些病例中出现的病变的性质表明 传播 发生在性交过程中。到目前为止,患者在当前的暴发期间仅出现轻微症状,但猴痘一般可能导致严重疾病。
应对、状态与不确定性
ECDC建议欧盟成员国要求猴痘患者保持隔离,避免密切的身体接触,直到皮疹完全治愈。猴痘患者的密切接触者应在21天内仔细观察症状,不要捐献血液、器官或骨髓。

Advertisement Home Life Health Monkeypox does not spread easily: EU health agency Monkeypox does not spread easily: EU health agency 10:30, 24/05/2022, Tuesday AA See Yeni Şafak News More Often: Click and Add to Favorites on Google! File photo People with multiple sexual partners are at higher risk of infection, according to risk assessment The monkeypox disease does not spread easily between people, the EU health agency said on Monday. "Human-to-human transmission occurs through close contact with infectious material from skin lesions of an infected person, through respiratory droplets in prolonged face-to-face contact, and through fomites," the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control (ECDC) stated in a risk assessment after an outbreak of the illness in non-endemic countries. The report pointed out that most patients diagnosed with monkeypox in Europe were gay men and "the nature of the presenting lesions in some cases, suggest transmission occurred during sexual intercourse." The EU health agency warned that people who have multiple sexual partners are at higher risk of infection. According to the risk assessment, patients have so far only presented mild symptoms during the current outbreak. But, it warned that monkeypox, in general, could cause severe disease. The ECDC recommended EU member states require monkeypox patients to remain isolated and avoid close physical contact until their rash heals completely. Patients can recover at home with supportive care, the document adds. Close contacts of monkeypox patients should observe symptoms carefully for 21 days and refrain from donating blood, organs, or bone marrow. Since the beginning of May, several monkeypox cases have been detected in nine EU countries: Austria, Belgium, France, Germany, Italy, Portugal, Spain, Sweden, and the Netherlands. Monkeypox is a virus transmitted to humans from animals, with symptoms including fever, rash, and swollen lymph nodes.
